[ACP_SRC_CD] Full original IsCD, including finite residue field
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/CurveDVRSpecialization/IsUnitOrIsUnitOfAddOne.lean#L107-L110
class IsCD (O A : Type) [CommRing O] [CommRing A] [Algebra O A] : Prop extends IsDomain A, IsDiscreteValuationRing A where
  complete : IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal A) A
  isLocalHom : IsLocalHom (algebraMap O A)
  fin : Finite (ResidueField A)


[ACP_SRC_CO] Original IsCO, automatic residue preservation and adic continuity
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/CommutativeAlgebra/ResMapBijective.lean#L9-L75
class IsCO (O R : Type) [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] : Prop
    extends IsLocalRing R, IsNoetherianRing R where
  complete : IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal R) R
  isLocalHom : IsLocalHom (algebraMap O R)
  res_surj : ∀ x : R, ∃ a : O, x - algebraMap O R a ∈ maximalIdeal R

attribute [instance] IsCO.complete IsCO.isLocalHom

instance IsCO.self (O : Type) [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] [IsNoetherianRing O]
    [IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal O) O] : IsCO O O where
  complete := inferInstance
  isLocalHom := ⟨fun _ h => h⟩
  res_surj x := ⟨x, by simp⟩

section Res

variable (O R : Type) [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] [IsCO O R]

theorem resMap_bijective : Function.Bijective (ResidueField.map (algebraMap O R)) := by
  refine ⟨(ResidueField.map (algebraMap O R)).injective, fun y => ?_⟩
  obtain ⟨x, rfl⟩ := residue_surjective y
  obtain ⟨a, ha⟩ := IsCO.res_surj (O := O) x
  refine ⟨residue O a, ?_⟩
  rw [ResidueField.map_residue]
  exact ((Ideal.Quotient.eq).2 ha).symm

def res : R →ₐ[O] ResidueField O :=
  { ((RingEquiv.ofBijective _ (resMap_bijective O R)).symm.toRingHom.comp (residue R)) with
    commutes' := fun a => by
      show (RingEquiv.ofBijective _ (resMap_bijective O R)).symm (residue R (algebraMap O R a)) = residue O a
      rw [RingEquiv.symm_apply_eq]
      exact (ResidueField.map_residue _ _).symm }

theorem res_eq_zero_iff (x : R) : res O R x = 0 ↔ x ∈ maximalIdeal R := by
  show (RingEquiv.ofBijective _ (resMap_bijective O R)).symm (residue R x) = 0 ↔ _
  rw [map_eq_zero_iff _ (RingEquiv.injective _), residue_eq_zero_iff]

variable {O R} in

theorem res_map {R' : Type} [CommRing R'] [Algebra O R'] [IsCO O R'] (f : R →ₐ[O] R') (x : R) :
    res O R' (f x) = res O R x := by
  obtain ⟨a, ha⟩ := IsCO.res_surj (O := O) x
  have h1 : res O R x = algebraMap O _ a := by
    have := (res_eq_zero_iff O R _).2 ha
    rwa [map_sub, AlgHom.commutes, sub_eq_zero] at this
  have hm : ∀ y ∈ maximalIdeal R, f y ∈ maximalIdeal R' := fun y hy => by
    obtain ⟨b, hb⟩ := IsCO.res_surj (O := O) (f y)
    by_cases hu : IsUnit b
    · exfalso
      have h2 : IsUnit (y - algebraMap O R b) := by
        by_contra hn
        have h3 : y - algebraMap O R b ∈ maximalIdeal R := hn
        have h4 : algebraMap O R b ∈ maximalIdeal R := by simpa using Ideal.sub_mem _ hy h3
        exact h4 (hu.map _)
      have h5 := h2.map f
      rw [map_sub, AlgHom.commutes] at h5
      exact hb h5
    · have h6 : algebraMap O R' b ∈ maximalIdeal R' := fun h => hu (IsLocalHom.map_nonunit b h)
      simpa using Ideal.add_mem _ hb h6
  have h2 := (res_eq_zero_iff O R' _).2 (hm _ ha)
  rw [map_sub, map_sub, AlgHom.commutes, AlgHom.commutes, sub_eq_zero] at h2
  rw [h2, h1]

end Res

def AdicCont {Γ R : Type} [TopologicalSpace Γ] [CommRing R] (I : Ideal R) (f : Γ → R) : Prop :=
  ∀ N : ℕ, IsLocallyConstant fun g => Ideal.Quotient.mk (I ^ N) (f g)


[ACP_SRC_CLSUB] General residue-constant subring construction; ACP1 proves the exact DVR/scalar-tower specialization directly
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/CommutativeAlgebraHeight/ClSub.lean#L1-L339
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.CommutativeAlgebraHeight.IsLocalHom
import Development.AbelianVarietyTheory.Proofs.ModuliProofs.IsNoetherianRingOfIsAdicComplete

open IsLocalRing UpperTheory.V.Alg UpperTheory.V.CA ReciprocityTheory.CA

private theorem exists_subalgebra_of_residue_constants (O : Type) [CommRing O] (R : Type) [CommRing R] [Algebra O R]
    [IsLocalRing R] :
    ∃ S : Subalgebra O R, ∀ c, c ∈ S ↔ ∃ a : O, c - algebraMap O R a ∈ maximalIdeal R := by
  refine ⟨{ carrier := {c | ∃ a : O, c - algebraMap O R a ∈ maximalIdeal R}
            mul_mem' := ?_, one_mem' := ?_, add_mem' := ?_, zero_mem' := ?_,
            algebraMap_mem' := ?_ }, fun c => Iff.rfl⟩
  · rintro x y ⟨a, ha⟩ ⟨b, hb⟩
    refine ⟨a * b, ?_⟩
    have e : x * y - algebraMap O R (a * b)
        = (x - algebraMap O R a) * y + algebraMap O R a * (y - algebraMap O R b) := by
      rw [map_mul]; ring
    rw [e]
    exact Ideal.add_mem _ (Ideal.mul_mem_right _ _ ha) (Ideal.mul_mem_left _ _ hb)
  · exact ⟨1, by simp⟩
  · rintro x y ⟨a, ha⟩ ⟨b, hb⟩
    refine ⟨a + b, ?_⟩
    have e : x + y - algebraMap O R (a + b)
        = (x - algebraMap O R a) + (y - algebraMap O R b) := by
      rw [map_add]; ring
    rw [e]
    exact Ideal.add_mem _ ha hb
  · exact ⟨0, by simp⟩
  · intro a
    exact ⟨a, by simp⟩

private theorem maximalIdeal_mem_residue_constant_subalgebra (O : Type) [CommRing O] (R : Type) [CommRing R] [Algebra O R]
    [IsLocalRing R] (S : Subalgebra O R)
    (hS : ∀ c, c ∈ S ↔ ∃ a : O, c - algebraMap O R a ∈ maximalIdeal R)
    (x : R) (hx : x ∈ maximalIdeal R) : x ∈ S :=
  (hS x).2 ⟨0, by simpa using hx⟩

private theorem residue_constant_subalgebra_isUnit_iff (O : Type) [CommRing O] (R : Type) [CommRing R] [Algebra O R]
    [IsLocalRing R] [IsLocalHom (algebraMap O R)] (S : Subalgebra O R)
    (hS : ∀ c, c ∈ S ↔ ∃ a : O, c - algebraMap O R a ∈ maximalIdeal R)
    (c : S) : IsUnit c ↔ (c : R) ∉ maximalIdeal R := by
  constructor
  · intro hc hm
    have : IsUnit (c : R) := hc.map S.val
    exact (IsLocalRing.mem_maximalIdeal _).1 hm this
  · intro hc
    obtain ⟨a, ha⟩ := (hS c).1 c.2
    have hu : IsUnit (c : R) := by
      by_contra h
      exact hc ((IsLocalRing.mem_maximalIdeal _).2 h)
    have ha' : IsUnit (algebraMap O R a) := by
      by_contra h
      have h1 : algebraMap O R a ∈ maximalIdeal R := (IsLocalRing.mem_maximalIdeal _).2 h
      apply hc
      have e : (c : R) = (c - algebraMap O R a) + algebraMap O R a := by ring
      rw [e]
      exact Ideal.add_mem _ ha h1
    have hau : IsUnit a := IsUnit.of_map (algebraMap O R) a ha'
    obtain ⟨u, hu'⟩ := hu
    obtain ⟨v, hv⟩ := hau
    have hmem : (↑u⁻¹ : R) ∈ S := by
      refine (hS _).2 ⟨↑v⁻¹, ?_⟩
      have e : (↑u⁻¹ : R) - algebraMap O R ↑v⁻¹
          = (↑u⁻¹ : R) * algebraMap O R ↑v⁻¹ * (algebraMap O R a - (c : R)) := by
        have h1 : (↑u⁻¹ : R) * (c : R) = 1 := by rw [← hu']; simp
        have h2 : algebraMap O R ↑v⁻¹ * algebraMap O R a = 1 := by
          rw [← map_mul, ← hv]; simp
        linear_combination algebraMap O R ↑v⁻¹ * h1 - (↑u⁻¹ : R) * h2
      rw [e]
      apply Ideal.mul_mem_left
      have : algebraMap O R a - (c : R) = -((c : R) - algebraMap O R a) := by ring
      rw [this]
      exact (Ideal.neg_mem_iff _).2 ha
    refine isUnit_iff_exists_inv.2 ⟨⟨_, hmem⟩, ?_⟩
    apply Subtype.ext
    simp [← hu']

private theorem residue_constant_subalgebra_isLocalRing (O : Type) [CommRing O] (R : Type) [CommRing R] [Algebra O R]
    [IsLocalRing R] [IsLocalHom (algebraMap O R)] (S : Subalgebra O R)
    (hS : ∀ c, c ∈ S ↔ ∃ a : O, c - algebraMap O R a ∈ maximalIdeal R) :
    IsLocalRing S := by
  haveI : Nontrivial S := ⟨⟨0, 1, fun h => zero_ne_one (congrArg Subtype.val h)⟩⟩
  refine IsLocalRing.of_nonunits_add ?_
  intro a b ha hb
  rw [mem_nonunits_iff, residue_constant_subalgebra_isUnit_iff O R S hS, not_not] at ha hb ⊢
  simpa using Ideal.add_mem _ ha hb

private theorem residue_constant_subalgebra_mem_maximalIdeal_iff (O : Type) [CommRing O] (R : Type) [CommRing R] [Algebra O R]
    [IsLocalRing R] [IsLocalHom (algebraMap O R)] (S : Subalgebra O R)
    (hS : ∀ c, c ∈ S ↔ ∃ a : O, c - algebraMap O R a ∈ maximalIdeal R) [IsLocalRing S]
    (c : S) : c ∈ maximalIdeal S ↔ (c : R) ∈ maximalIdeal R := by
  rw [IsLocalRing.mem_maximalIdeal, mem_nonunits_iff, residue_constant_subalgebra_isUnit_iff O R S hS, not_not]

private theorem residue_constant_subalgebra_mem_maximalIdeal_pow_iff (O : Type) [CommRing O] (R : Type) [CommRing R] [Algebra O R]
    [IsLocalRing R] [IsLocalHom (algebraMap O R)] (S : Subalgebra O R)
    (hS : ∀ c, c ∈ S ↔ ∃ a : O, c - algebraMap O R a ∈ maximalIdeal R) [IsLocalRing S]
    (N : ℕ) (c : S) : c ∈ maximalIdeal S ^ N ↔ (c : R) ∈ maximalIdeal R ^ N := by
  constructor
  · intro hc
    have h1 : Ideal.map S.val.toRingHom (maximalIdeal S) ≤ maximalIdeal R := by
      rw [Ideal.map_le_iff_le_comap]
      intro x hx
      exact (residue_constant_subalgebra_mem_maximalIdeal_iff O R S hS x).1 hx
    have h2 := Ideal.mem_map_of_mem S.val.toRingHom hc
    rw [Ideal.map_pow] at h2
    exact Ideal.pow_right_mono h1 N h2
  · induction N generalizing c with
    | zero => intro _; simp
    | succ n ih =>
      intro hc
      rcases n with _ | n
      · simpa [residue_constant_subalgebra_mem_maximalIdeal_iff O R S hS] using hc
      · rw [pow_succ'] at hc ⊢
        have key : ∀ x : R, x ∈ maximalIdeal R * maximalIdeal R ^ (n + 1) →
            ∃ hx : x ∈ S, (⟨x, hx⟩ : S) ∈ maximalIdeal S * maximalIdeal S ^ (n + 1) := by
          intro x hx
          refine Submodule.mul_induction_on hx ?_ ?_
          · intro i hi j hj
            have hjm : j ∈ maximalIdeal R := Ideal.pow_le_self (Nat.succ_ne_zero n) hj
            have hiS := maximalIdeal_mem_residue_constant_subalgebra O R S hS i hi
            have hjS := maximalIdeal_mem_residue_constant_subalgebra O R S hS j hjm
            refine ⟨S.mul_mem hiS hjS, ?_⟩
            have : (⟨i * j, S.mul_mem hiS hjS⟩ : S) = ⟨i, hiS⟩ * ⟨j, hjS⟩ := rfl
            rw [this]
            exact Ideal.mul_mem_mul ((residue_constant_subalgebra_mem_maximalIdeal_iff O R S hS _).2 hi) (ih ⟨j, hjS⟩ hj)
          · rintro x y ⟨hx, hx'⟩ ⟨hy, hy'⟩
            exact ⟨S.add_mem hx hy, Ideal.add_mem _ hx' hy'⟩
        obtain ⟨_, h⟩ := key _ hc
        exact h

private theorem residue_constant_subalgebra_module_finite (O : Type) [CommRing O] (R : Type) [CommRing R] [Algebra O R]
    [IsLocalRing R] [Module.Finite O (ResidueField R)] (S : Subalgebra O R)
    (hS : ∀ c, c ∈ S ↔ ∃ a : O, c - algebraMap O R a ∈ maximalIdeal R) :
    Module.Finite S R := by
  classical
  obtain ⟨s, hs⟩ := Module.finite_def.1 (inferInstance : Module.Finite O (ResidueField R))
  let g := Function.surjInv (IsLocalRing.residue_surjective (R := R))
  have hg : ∀ z, IsLocalRing.residue R (g z) = z := Function.surjInv_eq _
  refine ⟨⟨insert 1 (s.image g), ?_⟩⟩
  rw [eq_top_iff]
  intro x _
  let f := (IsScalarTower.toAlgHom O R (ResidueField R)).toLinearMap
  have hf : ∀ r, f r = IsLocalRing.residue R r := fun r => rfl
  have hx : IsLocalRing.residue R x ∈ Submodule.map f (Submodule.span O (g '' (s : Set _))) := by
    rw [Submodule.map_span, ← Set.image_comp]
    have : (f ∘ g) '' (s : Set _) = s := by
      ext z; simp [Function.comp, hf, hg]
    rw [this, hs]; trivial
  obtain ⟨y, hy, hxy⟩ := hx
  rw [hf] at hxy
  have hm : x - y ∈ maximalIdeal R := by
    rw [← IsLocalRing.residue_eq_zero_iff, map_sub, hxy, sub_self]
  have hmS := maximalIdeal_mem_residue_constant_subalgebra O R S hS _ hm
  have e : x = (⟨x - y, hmS⟩ : S) • (1 : R) + y := by
    rw [Subalgebra.smul_def]; simp
  rw [e]
  refine Submodule.add_mem _ (Submodule.smul_mem _ _ (Submodule.subset_span (by simp))) ?_
  have hle : Submodule.span O (g '' (s : Set _))
      ≤ (Submodule.span S (↑(insert 1 (s.image g)) : Set R)).restrictScalars O := by
    refine le_trans (Submodule.span_le_restrictScalars O S _) ?_
    exact Submodule.restrictScalars_mono O (Submodule.span_mono (by simp [Set.subset_insert]))
  exact hle hy

private theorem isNoetherian_of_finite_torsion_by_maximalIdeal (A : Type) [CommRing A] [IsLocalRing A] (P : Type) [AddCommGroup P]
    [Module A P] [Module.Finite A P]
    (h : Module.IsTorsionBySet A P (maximalIdeal A : Set A)) : IsNoetherian A P := by
  letI : Module (A ⧸ maximalIdeal A) P := h.module
  haveI : IsScalarTower A (A ⧸ maximalIdeal A) P := h.isScalarTower
  letI : Field (A ⧸ maximalIdeal A) := Ideal.Quotient.field _
  haveI : Module.Finite (A ⧸ maximalIdeal A) P := Module.Finite.of_restrictScalars_finite A _ _
  haveI : IsNoetherian (A ⧸ maximalIdeal A) P := inferInstance
  refine ⟨fun N => ?_⟩
  let N' : Submodule (A ⧸ maximalIdeal A) P :=
    { carrier := N
      add_mem' := fun hx hy => N.add_mem hx hy
      zero_mem' := N.zero_mem
      smul_mem' := by
        intro c x hx
        obtain ⟨a, rfl⟩ := Ideal.Quotient.mk_surjective c
        have : (Ideal.Quotient.mk (maximalIdeal A) a) • x = a • x := by
          rw [← Ideal.Quotient.algebraMap_eq, algebraMap_smul]
        rw [this]
        exact N.smul_mem a hx }
  obtain ⟨t, ht⟩ := IsNoetherian.noetherian N'
  refine ⟨t, ?_⟩
  rw [← Submodule.restrictScalars_span A (A ⧸ maximalIdeal A) Ideal.Quotient.mk_surjective, ht]
  rfl

private theorem residue_constant_subalgebra_isNoetherian (O : Type) [CommRing O] (R : Type) [CommRing R] [Algebra O R]
    [IsLocalRing R] [IsLocalHom (algebraMap O R)] [IsNoetherianRing R] (S : Subalgebra O R)
    (hS : ∀ c, c ∈ S ↔ ∃ a : O, c - algebraMap O R a ∈ maximalIdeal R) [IsLocalRing S]
    [Module.Finite S R] : IsNoetherian S R := by
  refine ⟨fun N => ?_⟩
  let J : Submodule R R := Submodule.span R (N : Set R)
  let J' : Submodule S R := J.restrictScalars S
  let L' : Submodule S R := (maximalIdeal R • J).restrictScalars S
  have hJfg : J'.FG := Submodule.FG.restrictScalars (IsNoetherian.noetherian J)
  have hLfg : L'.FG := Submodule.FG.restrictScalars (IsNoetherian.noetherian (maximalIdeal R • J))
  have hNJ : N ≤ J' := by
    intro x hx
    rw [Submodule.restrictScalars_mem]
    exact Submodule.subset_span hx
  have hLJ : L' ≤ J' := by
    intro x hx
    rw [Submodule.restrictScalars_mem] at hx ⊢
    exact Submodule.smul_le_right hx
  have hJm : ∀ j ∈ J, ∀ y ∈ maximalIdeal R, y * j ∈ N := by
    intro j hj
    induction hj using Submodule.span_induction with
    | mem x hx =>
      intro y hy
      have : y * x = (⟨y, maximalIdeal_mem_residue_constant_subalgebra O R S hS y hy⟩ : S) • x := by
        rw [Subalgebra.smul_def, smul_eq_mul]
      rw [this]
      exact N.smul_mem _ hx
    | zero => intro y _; simp
    | add x z _ _ hx hz =>
      intro y hy
      rw [mul_add]
      exact N.add_mem (hx y hy) (hz y hy)
    | smul r x _ hx =>
      intro y hy
      have : y * (r • x) = (y * r) * x := by rw [smul_eq_mul]; ring
      rw [this]
      exact hx _ (Ideal.mul_mem_right _ _ hy)
  have hLN : L' ≤ N := by
    intro x hx
    rw [Submodule.restrictScalars_mem] at hx
    refine Submodule.smul_induction_on hx ?_ ?_
    · intro y hy j hj
      rw [smul_eq_mul]
      exact hJm j hj y hy
    · intro x z hx hz
      exact N.add_mem hx hz
  obtain ⟨t, ht⟩ := hJfg
  let K : Submodule S R := Submodule.span S (t : Set R)
  have hKJ : ∀ x ∈ K, x ∈ J := by
    intro x hx
    have : x ∈ J' := by rw [← ht]; exact hx
    rwa [Submodule.restrictScalars_mem] at this
  have hNK : N ≤ K := by
    intro x hx
    have := hNJ hx
    rw [← ht] at this
    exact this
  have hLK : L' ≤ K := by
    intro x hx
    have := hLJ hx
    rw [← ht] at this
    exact this
  haveI : Module.Finite S K := Module.Finite.iff_fg.2 ⟨t, rfl⟩
  let L0 : Submodule S K := L'.comap K.subtype
  let N0 : Submodule S K := N.comap K.subtype
  have hL0fg : L0.FG := by
    refine Submodule.fg_of_fg_map_injective K.subtype Subtype.val_injective ?_
    rw [Submodule.map_comap_subtype, inf_eq_right.2 hLK]
    exact hLfg
  have tors : Module.IsTorsionBySet S (K ⧸ L0) (maximalIdeal S : Set S) := by
    intro p x
    induction p using Submodule.Quotient.induction_on with
    | H j =>
      rw [← Submodule.Quotient.mk_smul, Submodule.Quotient.mk_eq_zero]
      change ((x : S) : R) • (j : R) ∈ (maximalIdeal R • J).restrictScalars S
      rw [Submodule.restrictScalars_mem]
      exact Submodule.smul_mem_smul ((residue_constant_subalgebra_mem_maximalIdeal_iff O R S hS _).1 x.2) (hKJ _ j.2)
  haveI : IsNoetherian S (K ⧸ L0) := isNoetherian_of_finite_torsion_by_maximalIdeal S (K ⧸ L0) tors
  have hN0 : N0.FG := by
    refine Submodule.fg_of_fg_map_of_fg_inf_ker L0.mkQ (IsNoetherian.noetherian _) ?_
    rw [Submodule.ker_mkQ, inf_eq_right.2]
    · exact hL0fg
    · intro x hx
      exact hLN hx
  have e : Submodule.map K.subtype N0 = N := by
    rw [Submodule.map_comap_subtype, inf_eq_right.2 hNK]
  rw [← e]
  exact hN0.map _

private theorem smodEq_ideal_smul_univ_iff_sub_mem (A : Type) [CommRing A] (I : Ideal A) (x y : A) :
    x ≡ y [SMOD I • (⊤ : Submodule A A)] ↔ x - y ∈ I := by
  rw [SModEq.sub_mem, smul_eq_mul, Ideal.mul_top]

private theorem isAdicComplete_residue_constant_subalgebra (O : Type) [CommRing O] (R : Type) [CommRing R] [Algebra O R]
    [IsLocalRing R] [IsLocalHom (algebraMap O R)] [IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal R) R]
    (S : Subalgebra O R)
    (hS : ∀ c, c ∈ S ↔ ∃ a : O, c - algebraMap O R a ∈ maximalIdeal R) [IsLocalRing S] :
    IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal S) S := by
  refine { haus' := ?_, prec' := ?_ }
  · intro x hx
    apply Subtype.ext
    refine IsHausdorff.haus' (I := maximalIdeal R) (x : R) (fun n => ?_)
    rw [smodEq_ideal_smul_univ_iff_sub_mem]
    have := hx n
    rw [smodEq_ideal_smul_univ_iff_sub_mem, residue_constant_subalgebra_mem_maximalIdeal_pow_iff O R S hS] at this
    simpa using this
  · intro f hf
    obtain ⟨L, hL⟩ := IsPrecomplete.prec' (I := maximalIdeal R) (fun n => (f n : R)) (by
      intro m n hmn
      rw [smodEq_ideal_smul_univ_iff_sub_mem]
      have := hf hmn
      rw [smodEq_ideal_smul_univ_iff_sub_mem, residue_constant_subalgebra_mem_maximalIdeal_pow_iff O R S hS] at this
      simpa using this)
    have h1 : (f 1 : R) - L ∈ maximalIdeal R := by
      have := hL 1
      rw [smodEq_ideal_smul_univ_iff_sub_mem, pow_one] at this
      exact this
    have hLS : L ∈ S := by
      have e : L = (f 1 : R) - ((f 1 : R) - L) := by ring
      rw [e]
      exact S.sub_mem (f 1).2 (maximalIdeal_mem_residue_constant_subalgebra O R S hS _ h1)
    refine ⟨⟨L, hLS⟩, fun n => ?_⟩
    rw [smodEq_ideal_smul_univ_iff_sub_mem, residue_constant_subalgebra_mem_maximalIdeal_pow_iff O R S hS]
    have := hL n
    rw [smodEq_ideal_smul_univ_iff_sub_mem] at this
    simpa using this

theorem UpperTheory.V.CA.cl_sub :
    ∀ (O : Type) [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] [IsNoetherianRing O] [IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal O) O] (R : Type) [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] [IsLocalRing R], IsCL O R → ∃ S : Subalgebra O R, (∀ c, c ∈ S ↔ ∃ a : O, c - algebraMap O R a ∈ maximalIdeal R) ∧ Module.Finite S R ∧ ∃ _ : IsCO O S, ∀ (N : ℕ) (c : S), c ∈ maximalIdeal S ^ N ↔ (c : R) ∈ maximalIdeal R ^ N := by
  intro O _ _ _ _ R _ _ _ hR
  haveI := hR.noeth
  haveI := hR.complete
  haveI := hR.isLocalHom
  haveI := hR.res_fin
  obtain ⟨S, hS⟩ := exists_subalgebra_of_residue_constants O R
  haveI : IsLocalRing S := residue_constant_subalgebra_isLocalRing O R S hS
  haveI : Module.Finite S R := residue_constant_subalgebra_module_finite O R S hS
  haveI : IsNoetherian S R := residue_constant_subalgebra_isNoetherian O R S hS
  haveI : IsNoetherian S S :=
    isNoetherian_of_injective (Algebra.linearMap S R) Subtype.val_injective
  haveI : IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal S) S := isAdicComplete_residue_constant_subalgebra O R S hS
  refine ⟨S, hS, inferInstance,
    { complete := inferInstance, isLocalHom := ?_, res_surj := ?_ },
    fun N c => residue_constant_subalgebra_mem_maximalIdeal_pow_iff O R S hS N c⟩
  · refine ⟨fun a ha => ?_⟩
    have h1 : IsUnit (algebraMap O R a) := by
      have := ha.map S.val
      simpa using this
    exact IsUnit.of_map _ _ h1
  · intro x
    obtain ⟨a, ha⟩ := (hS x).1 x.2
    exact ⟨a, (residue_constant_subalgebra_mem_maximalIdeal_iff O R S hS _).2 (by simpa using ha)⟩


[ACP_SRC_SPACE] NRep, exact ordered products, IsLift, supplied Space, weight-zero algebra, FinLam and core
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/LeAdicClosure.lean#L9-L221
def w (n : ℕ) (i : Fin n) : ℤ := if n = 3 then 1 - (i : ℕ) else (n : ℤ) - 1 - 2 * (i : ℕ)

variable {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {k : Type} [Field k] (F : Frame l k) (n : ℕ)

def ordProd {H K : Type} [CommRing K] {n : ℕ} (r : H → Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) K) (ψ : Fin n → H → K) (g : Fin n → H) :
    Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) K := (List.ofFn fun i => r (g i) - ψ i (g i) • (1 : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) K)).prod

theorem ordProd_map {H K K' : Type} [CommRing K] [CommRing K'] {n : ℕ} (f : K →+* K')
    (r : H → Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) K) (ψ : Fin n → H → K) (g : Fin n → H) :
    ordProd (fun s => f.mapMatrix (r s)) (fun i s => f (ψ i s)) g = f.mapMatrix (ordProd r ψ g) := by
  simp only [ordProd, map_list_prod, List.map_ofFn]
  congr 1
  refine congrArg List.ofFn (funext fun i => ?_)
  simp only [Function.comp_apply, map_sub]
  congr 1
  ext a b
  by_cases h : a = b <;> simp [h, Matrix.one_apply]

@[ext] structure NRep (K : Type) [CommRing K] where
  r : F.G0 →* Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) K
  ψ : ∀ v, Fin n → F.Gv v →* Kˣ
  pol : ∀ g, r (F.star g) = Jmat K n * (r g)ᵀ * Jmat K n
  diag : ∀ i j, i ≠ j → (r F.g0 - r (F.star F.g0)) i j = 0
  ord : ∀ v g, ordProd (fun s => r (F.loc0 v s)) (fun i s => (ψ v i s : K)) g = 0

namespace NRep

variable {F n} {K K' : Type} [CommRing K] [CommRing K']

def map (f : K →+* K') (ρ : NRep F n K) : NRep F n K' where
  r := (f.mapMatrix : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) K →+* _).toMonoidHom.comp ρ.r
  ψ v i := (Units.map f.toMonoidHom).comp (ρ.ψ v i)
  pol g := by
    show f.mapMatrix (ρ.r (F.star g)) = Jmat K' n * (f.mapMatrix (ρ.r g))ᵀ * Jmat K' n
    rw [ρ.pol, map_mul, map_mul]
    simp only [RingHom.mapMatrix_apply, Jmat_map, Matrix.transpose_map]
  diag i j h := by
    show f (ρ.r F.g0 i j) - f (ρ.r (F.star F.g0) i j) = 0
    rw [← map_sub]
    exact (congrArg f (ρ.diag i j h)).trans (map_zero f)
  ord v g := (ordProd_map f _ _ g).trans (by rw [ρ.ord, map_zero])

@[simp] theorem map_r (f : K →+* K') (ρ : NRep F n K) (g : F.G0) (i j : Fin n) :
    (ρ.map f).r g i j = f (ρ.r g i j) := rfl

@[simp] theorem map_ψ (f : K →+* K') (ρ : NRep F n K) (v : F.V) (i : Fin n) (g : F.Gv v) :
    ((ρ.map f).ψ v i g : K') = f (ρ.ψ v i g) := rfl

theorem map_map {K'' : Type} [CommRing K''] (f : K →+* K') (f' : K' →+* K'') (ρ : NRep F n K) :
    (ρ.map f).map f' = ρ.map (f'.comp f) := rfl

def restrict {O : Type} [CommRing O] [Algebra O K] (ρ : NRep F n K) (A : Subalgebra O K)
    (h1 : ∀ g i j, ρ.r g i j ∈ A) (h2 : ∀ v i g, (ρ.ψ v i g : K) ∈ A) : NRep F n A where
  r :=
    { toFun := fun g => Matrix.of fun i j => ⟨ρ.r g i j, h1 g i j⟩
      map_one' := by
        ext i j
        by_cases h : i = j <;> simp [h, Matrix.one_apply]
      map_mul' := fun g h => by
        ext i j
        simp [Matrix.mul_apply] }
  ψ v i :=
    { toFun := fun g => ⟨⟨ρ.ψ v i g, h2 v i g⟩, ⟨ρ.ψ v i g⁻¹, h2 v i g⁻¹⟩, Subtype.ext (by simp), Subtype.ext (by simp)⟩
      map_one' := by ext; simp
      map_mul' := fun g h => by ext; simp }
  pol g := by
    refine Matrix.map_injective (f := (A.val : A →+* K)) Subtype.val_injective ?_
    show _ = (A.val : A →+* K).mapMatrix _
    rw [map_mul, map_mul]
    simp only [RingHom.mapMatrix_apply, Jmat_map]
    exact ρ.pol g
  diag i j h := Subtype.ext (ρ.diag i j h)
  ord v g := by
    refine Matrix.map_injective (f := (A.val : A →+* K)) Subtype.val_injective ?_
    show (A.val : A →+* K).mapMatrix _ = (A.val : A →+* K).mapMatrix _
    rw [← ordProd_map, map_zero]
    exact ρ.ord v g

def Cont (I : Ideal K) (ρ : NRep F n K) : Prop :=
  (∀ i j, AdicCont I fun g => ρ.r g i j) ∧ ∀ v i, AdicCont I fun g => (ρ.ψ v i g : K)

def IsBar (f : k →+* K) (ρ : NRep F n K) : Prop :=
  (∀ g i, ρ.r g i i = f ((F.Ψ g ^ w n i : kˣ) : k)) ∧ ∀ v i g, (ρ.ψ v i g : K) = f ((F.Ψ (F.loc v g) ^ w n i : kˣ) : k)

def IsDiag (ρ : NRep F n K) : Prop := ∀ g i j, i ≠ j → ρ.r g i j = 0

def triP (ρ : NRep F n K) : Ideal K := Ideal.span {x | ∃ g i j, j < i ∧ x = ρ.r g i j}

def triM (ρ : NRep F n K) : Ideal K := Ideal.span {x | ∃ g i j, i < j ∧ x = ρ.r g i j}

def ee (ρ : NRep F n K) : Ideal K := Ideal.span {x | ∃ g i j, Odd (w n i - w n j) ∧ x = ρ.r g i j}

def OffTri (ρ : NRep F n K) (p : Ideal K) : Prop := ¬ ρ.triP ≤ p ∧ ¬ ρ.triM ≤ p

def TI (ρ : NRep F n K) (p : Ideal K) : Prop := ρ.OffTri p ∧ (n = 3 → ¬ ρ.ee ≤ p)

def TE (ρ : NRep F n K) (p : Ideal K) : Prop := ρ.OffTri p ∧ n = 3 ∧ ρ.ee ≤ p

def Coin (ρ : NRep F n K) (p : Ideal K) (v : F.V) (i j : Fin n) : Prop :=
  ∀ g ∈ F.Nv v, (ρ.ψ v i g : K) - ρ.ψ v j g ∈ p

def mon (ρ : NRep F n K) (j : ℤ) : Set K :=
  {x | ∃ L : List (F.G0 × Fin n × Fin n), (L.map fun s => w n s.2.1 - w n s.2.2).sum = j ∧
    x = (L.map fun s => ρ.r s.1 s.2.1 s.2.2).prod}

end NRep

def Irr {H K : Type} [Field K] {ι : Type} [Fintype ι] (r : H → Matrix ι ι K) : Prop :=
  ∀ U : Submodule K (ι → K), (∀ g, ∀ u ∈ U, r g *ᵥ u ∈ U) → U = ⊥ ∨ U = ⊤

def adicClosure {O R : Type} [CommRing O] [CommRing R] [IsLocalRing R] [Algebra O R] (S : Subalgebra O R) :
    Subalgebra O R where
  carrier := {x | ∀ N : ℕ, ∃ y ∈ S, x - y ∈ maximalIdeal R ^ N}
  mul_mem' {a b} ha hb N := by
    obtain ⟨x, hx, h1⟩ := ha N
    obtain ⟨y, hy, h2⟩ := hb N
    refine ⟨x * y, mul_mem hx hy, ?_⟩
    have : a * b - x * y = (a - x) * b + x * (b - y) := by ring
    rw [this]
    exact add_mem (Ideal.mul_mem_right _ _ h1) (Ideal.mul_mem_left _ _ h2)
  add_mem' {a b} ha hb N := by
    obtain ⟨x, hx, h1⟩ := ha N
    obtain ⟨y, hy, h2⟩ := hb N
    refine ⟨x + y, add_mem hx hy, ?_⟩
    rw [add_sub_add_comm]
    exact add_mem h1 h2
  algebraMap_mem' c N := ⟨_, S.algebraMap_mem c, by simp⟩

theorem le_adicClosure {O R : Type} [CommRing O] [CommRing R] [IsLocalRing R] [Algebra O R] (S : Subalgebra O R) :
    S ≤ adicClosure S := fun x hx _ => ⟨x, hx, by simp⟩

class IsCoef (l : outParam ℕ) (O : Type) [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [IsDiscreteValuationRing O] : Prop where
  complete : IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal O) O
  fin : Finite (ResidueField O)
  char : CharP (ResidueField O) l

attribute [instance] IsCoef.complete IsCoef.fin IsCoef.char

section Space

variable {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] (O : Type) [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] (F : Frame l (ResidueField O)) (n : ℕ)

variable {F n} in

def IsLift {R : Type} [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] [IsCO O R] (ρ : NRep F n R) : Prop :=
  ρ.Cont (maximalIdeal R) ∧ (ρ.map (res O R : R →+* ResidueField O)).IsBar (RingHom.id _) ∧
    (ρ.map (res O R : R →+* ResidueField O)).IsDiag

structure Space : Type 1 where
  R : Type
  [cr : CommRing R]
  [al : Algebra O R]
  [co : IsCO O R]
  ρ : NRep F n R
  lift : IsLift O ρ
  univ : ∀ (C : Type) [CommRing C] [Algebra O C] [IsCO O C] (ρ' : NRep F n C), IsLift O ρ' →
    ∃! f : R →ₐ[O] C, ρ.map (f : R →+* C) = ρ'

attribute [instance] Space.cr Space.al Space.co

namespace Space

variable {O F n} (S : Space O F n)

def B : Subalgebra O S.R :=
  adicClosure (Algebra.adjoin O (S.ρ.mon 0 ∪ {x | ∃ v i g, x = (S.ρ.ψ v i g : S.R)}))

def A : Subalgebra O S.R := Algebra.adjoin O ((S.B : Set S.R) ∪ {x | ∃ g i j, x = S.ρ.r g i j})

theorem B_le_A : S.B ≤ S.A := fun _ hx => Algebra.subset_adjoin (Or.inl hx)

theorem ψ_mem (v : F.V) (i : Fin n) (g : F.Gv v) : (S.ρ.ψ v i g : S.R) ∈ S.B :=
  le_adicClosure _ (Algebra.subset_adjoin (Or.inr ⟨v, i, g, rfl⟩))

theorem mon_mem {x : S.R} (hx : x ∈ S.ρ.mon 0) : x ∈ S.B := le_adicClosure _ (Algebra.subset_adjoin (Or.inl hx))

theorem r_mem (g : F.G0) (i j : Fin n) : S.ρ.r g i j ∈ S.A := Algebra.subset_adjoin (Or.inr ⟨g, i, j, rfl⟩)

def inc : S.B →ₐ[O] S.A := Subalgebra.inclusion S.B_le_A

instance : Algebra S.B S.A := (S.inc : S.B →+* S.A).toAlgebra

instance : IsScalarTower O S.B S.A := IsScalarTower.of_algebraMap_eq fun _ => rfl

def u : NRep F n S.A := S.ρ.restrict S.A S.r_mem fun v i g => S.B_le_A (S.ψ_mem v i g)

def ψB (v : F.V) (i : Fin n) (g : F.Gv v) : S.B := ⟨S.ρ.ψ v i g, S.ψ_mem v i g⟩

def aB (g : F.G0) (i : Fin n) : S.B := ⟨S.ρ.r g i i, S.mon_mem ⟨[(g, i, i)], by simp, by simp⟩⟩

def trB (g : F.G0) : S.B := ∑ i, S.aB g i

def mB : Ideal S.B := (maximalIdeal S.R).comap (S.B.val : S.B →+* S.R)

def π (p : Ideal S.A) : Ideal S.B := p.comap (S.inc : S.B →+* S.A)

def gr (j : ℤ) : Submodule S.B S.A := Submodule.span S.B (S.u.mon j)

def IsHom (I : Ideal S.A) : Prop := I = Ideal.span {x | x ∈ I ∧ ∃ j, x ∈ S.gr j}

def lamId : Ideal S.B :=
  (maximalIdeal O).map (algebraMap O S.B) ⊔
    Ideal.span {x | ∃ v i g, g ∈ F.Iv v ∧ F.Ψ (F.loc v g) = 1 ∧ x = S.ψB v i g - 1}

def FinLam (I : Ideal S.B) : Prop := S.mB ≤ (I ⊔ S.lamId).radical

def BB : Set (PrimeSpectrum S.A) := {p | S.mB ≤ S.π p.asIdeal ∧ ¬ S.u.triP ≤ p.asIdeal}

structure Loc (x : Ideal S.A) : Type 1 where
  O' : Type
  [cO : CommRing O']
  [dO : IsDomain O']
  [vO : IsDiscreteValuationRing O']

section Cone

variable {k G W V : Type} [CommRing k] [Group G] [TopologicalSpace G] [AddCommGroup W] [Module k W]
  {Gv : V → Type} [∀ v, Group (Gv v)] [∀ v, TopologicalSpace (Gv v)]
  (loc : ∀ v, Gv v →* G) (ρ : Representation k G W) (b : V → Submodule k W)

def coneH0 : Submodule k W where
  carrier := {X | (∀ g, ρ g X = X) ∧ ∀ v, X ∈ b v}
  add_mem' := by
    rintro x y ⟨a1, a2⟩ ⟨b1, b2⟩
    exact ⟨fun g => by rw [map_add, a1, b1], fun v => add_mem (a2 v) (b2 v)⟩
  zero_mem' := ⟨fun g => map_zero _, fun v => zero_mem _⟩
  smul_mem' := by
    rintro c x ⟨a1, a2⟩
    exact ⟨fun g => by rw [map_smul, a1], fun v => Submodule.smul_mem _ c (a2 v)⟩

def Z1C : Submodule k ((G → W) × (V → W)) where
  carrier := {x | IsLocallyConstant x.1 ∧ (∀ s t, x.1 (s * t) = x.1 s + ρ s (x.1 t)) ∧
    ∀ v s, x.1 (loc v s) - (ρ (loc v s) (x.2 v) - x.2 v) ∈ b v}
  add_mem' := by
    rintro x y ⟨a1, a2, a3⟩ ⟨b1, b2, b3⟩
    refine ⟨a1.add b1, fun s t => ?_, fun v s => ?_⟩
    · simp only [Prod.fst_add, Pi.add_apply, a2, b2, map_add]; abel
    · convert add_mem (a3 v s) (b3 v s) using 1
      simp only [Prod.fst_add, Prod.snd_add, Pi.add_apply, map_add]; abel
  zero_mem' := ⟨IsLocallyConstant.const 0, by simp, by simp⟩
  smul_mem' := by
    rintro c x ⟨a1, a2, a3⟩
    refine ⟨a1.comp (c • ·), fun s t => by simp [a2], fun v s => ?_⟩
    convert Submodule.smul_mem _ c (a3 v s) using 1
    simp [smul_sub]

def continuousCoboundaries1 : Submodule k ((G → W) × (V → W)) where
  carrier := {x | ∃ X : W, (∀ s, x.1 s = ρ s X - X) ∧ ∀ v, x.2 v - X ∈ b v}
  add_mem' := by
    rintro x y ⟨X, a1, a2⟩ ⟨Y, b1, b2⟩
    refine ⟨X + Y, fun s => ?_, fun v => ?_⟩
    · simp only [Prod.fst_add, Pi.add_apply, a1, b1, map_add]; abel
    · convert add_mem (a2 v) (b2 v) using 1
      simp only [Prod.snd_add, Pi.add_apply]; abel
  zero_mem' := ⟨0, by simp, by simp⟩
  smul_mem' := by
    rintro c x ⟨X, a1, a2⟩
    refine ⟨c • X, fun s => by simp [a1, smul_sub], fun v => ?_⟩
    convert Submodule.smul_mem _ c (a2 v) using 1
    simp [smul_sub]

abbrev H1C : Type := Z1C loc ρ b ⧸ (continuousCoboundaries1 ρ b).comap (Z1C loc ρ b).subtype

def continuousCocycles2 : Submodule k ((G × G → W) × (∀ v, Gv v → W)) where
  carrier := {x | IsLocallyConstant x.1 ∧ (∀ v, IsLocallyConstant (x.2 v)) ∧
    (∀ s t u, ρ s (x.1 (t, u)) + x.1 (s, t * u) = x.1 (s * t, u) + x.1 (s, t)) ∧
    ∀ v s t, x.1 (loc v s, loc v t) - dOne (ρ.comp (loc v)) (x.2 v) (s, t) ∈ b v}
  add_mem' := by
    rintro x y ⟨a1, a2, a3, a4⟩ ⟨b1, b2, b3, b4⟩
    refine ⟨a1.add b1, fun v => (a2 v).add (b2 v), fun s t u => ?_, fun v s t => ?_⟩
    · simp only [Prod.fst_add, Pi.add_apply, map_add]
      rw [add_add_add_comm, a3, b3, add_add_add_comm]
    · convert add_mem (a4 v s t) (b4 v s t) using 1
      simp only [Prod.fst_add, Prod.snd_add, Pi.add_apply, dOne, map_add]; abel
  zero_mem' := ⟨IsLocallyConstant.const 0, fun _ => IsLocallyConstant.const 0, by simp, by simp [dOne]⟩
  smul_mem' := by
    rintro c x ⟨a1, a2, a3, a4⟩
    refine ⟨a1.comp (c • ·), fun v => (a2 v).comp (c • ·), fun s t u => ?_, fun v s t => ?_⟩
    · simp only [Prod.smul_fst, Pi.smul_apply, map_smul, ← smul_add, a3]
    · convert Submodule.smul_mem _ c (a4 v s t) using 1
      simp [dOne, smul_sub]

def continuousCoboundaries2 : Submodule k ((G × G → W) × (∀ v, Gv v → W)) where
  carrier := {x | ∃ (β : G → W) (Y : V → W), IsLocallyConstant β ∧ x.1 = dOne ρ β ∧
    ∀ v s, x.2 v s - (β (loc v s) - (ρ (loc v s) (Y v) - Y v)) ∈ b v}
  add_mem' := by
    rintro x y ⟨β, Y, a1, a2, a3⟩ ⟨β', Y', b1, b2, b3⟩
    refine ⟨β + β', Y + Y', a1.add b1, ?_, fun v s => ?_⟩
    · funext p
      simp only [Prod.fst_add, Pi.add_apply, a2, b2, dOne, map_add]; abel
    · convert add_mem (a3 v s) (b3 v s) using 1
      simp only [Prod.snd_add, Pi.add_apply, map_add]; abel
  zero_mem' := ⟨0, 0, IsLocallyConstant.const 0, by funext p; simp [dOne], by simp⟩
  smul_mem' := by
    rintro c x ⟨β, Y, a1, a2, a3⟩
    refine ⟨c • β, c • Y, a1.comp (c • ·), ?_, fun v s => ?_⟩
    · funext p
      simp [a2, dOne, smul_sub]
    · convert Submodule.smul_mem _ c (a3 v s) using 1
      simp [smul_sub]

abbrev coneH2 : Type := continuousCocycles2 loc ρ b ⧸ (continuousCoboundaries2 loc ρ b).comap (continuousCocycles2 loc ρ b).subtype

end Cone

def chRep {k G : Type} [CommRing k] [Group G] (χ : G →* kˣ) : Representation k G k where
  toFun g := (χ g : k) • LinearMap.id
  map_one' := by ext; simp
  map_mul' g h := by ext; simp [mul_comm]

@[simp] theorem chRep_apply {k G : Type} [CommRing k] [Group G] (χ : G →* kˣ) (g : G) (x : k) :
    chRep χ g x = χ g * x := rfl

structure Frame (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (k : Type) [Field k] : Type 1 where
  Γ : Type
  [grp : Group Γ]
  [top : TopologicalSpace Γ]
  [tg : IsTopologicalGroup Γ]
  [cpt : CompactSpace Γ]
  [t2 : T2Space Γ]
  [td : TotallyDisconnectedSpace Γ]
  del : Γ →* ℤˣ
  c : Γ
  V : Type
  [fV : Fintype V]
  Gv : V → Type
  [gv : ∀ v, Group (Gv v)]
  [tv : ∀ v, TopologicalSpace (Gv v)]
  [tgv : ∀ v, IsTopologicalGroup (Gv v)]
  [cv : ∀ v, CompactSpace (Gv v)]
  [t2v : ∀ v, T2Space (Gv v)]
  [tdv : ∀ v, TotallyDisconnectedSpace (Gv v)]
  loc0 : ∀ v, Gv v →* del.ker
  Iv : ∀ v, Subgroup (Gv v)
  dv : V → ℕ
  Inf : Type
  [fI : Fintype Inf]
  cw : Inf → Γ
  eps : Γ →* ℤ_[l]ˣ
  Ψ : Γ →* kˣ
  g0 : del.ker

attribute [instance] Frame.grp Frame.top Frame.tg Frame.cpt Frame.t2 Frame.td Frame.fV Frame.gv Frame.tv Frame.tgv
  Frame.cv Frame.t2v Frame.tdv Frame.fI

namespace Frame

variable {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {k : Type} [Field k] (F : Frame l k)

abbrev G0 : Type := F.del.ker

def loc (v : F.V) : F.Gv v →* F.Γ := F.del.ker.subtype.comp (F.loc0 v)

def conj : F.G0 →* F.G0 := (MulAut.conjNormal F.c : MulAut F.G0).toMonoidHom

def star (g : F.G0) : F.G0 := F.conj g⁻¹

def loc2 (p : F.V × Bool) : F.Gv p.1 →* F.G0 := if p.2 then F.conj.comp (F.loc0 p.1) else F.loc0 p.1

def d : ℕ := ∑ v, F.dv v

def om (K : Type) [CommRing K] [CharP K l] : F.Γ →* Kˣ :=
  (Units.map ((ZMod.castHom (dvd_refl l) K).comp PadicInt.toZMod).toMonoidHom).comp F.eps

def delK (K : Type) [CommRing K] : F.Γ →* Kˣ := (Units.map (Int.castRingHom K).toMonoidHom).comp F.del

def Nv (v : F.V) : Subgroup (F.Gv v) := F.Iv v ⊓ (F.eps.comp (F.loc v)).ker

def ext {k' : Type} [Field k'] (f : k →+* k') : Frame l k' :=
  { F with Ψ := (Units.map f.toMonoidHom).comp F.Ψ }

def EC : Prop :=
  ∀ (K W : Type) [Field K] [Finite K] [CharP K l] [AddCommGroup W] [Module K W] [Module.Finite K W]
    (ρ : Representation K F.Γ W) (b : F.V → Submodule K W), IsOpen (ρ.ker : Set F.Γ) →
    (∀ v s, ∀ X ∈ b v, ρ (F.loc v s) X ∈ b v) →
    (∀ f : W →ₗ[K] K, (∀ g X, f (ρ g X) = F.om K g * f X) → f = 0) →
    Module.Finite K (coneH2 F.loc ρ b) ∧
    Module.finrank K (coneH2 F.loc ρ b) + Module.finrank K (coneH0 ρ b) + ∑ v, F.dv v * Module.finrank K (b v) ≤
      Module.finrank K (H1C F.loc ρ b) + ∑ w, Module.finrank K (LinearMap.ker (ρ (F.cw w) - 1))

structure Ok [CharP k l] (C₀ : ℕ) : Prop where
  l_odd : l ≠ 2
  fin : Finite k
  del_open : IsOpen (F.del.ker : Set F.Γ)
  c_sq : F.c * F.c = 1
  del_c : F.del F.c = -1
  loc_cont : ∀ v, Continuous (F.loc0 v)
  eps_cont : Continuous F.eps
  Ψ_open : IsOpen (F.Ψ.ker : Set F.Γ)
  Ψ_c : F.Ψ F.c = 1
  baseSubgroup_spec : F.Ψ F.g0 ^ 2 ≠ 1
  phi : PhiCond F.Γ l
  card_inf : Fintype.card F.Inf = F.d
  cw_sq : ∀ w, F.cw w * F.cw w = 1
  del_cw : ∀ w, F.del (F.cw w) = -1
  f2a : ∃ g : F.G0, F.om k g ≠ 1
  f2b : ∀ j : ℤ, j = 1 ∨ j = -1 → ∃ g : F.G0, F.om k g * F.Ψ g ^ j ≠ 1
  f2c : ∀ j : ℤ, j = 2 ∨ j = -2 → ∃ g, F.om k g * F.Ψ g ^ j * F.delK k g ≠ 1
  f3 : ∀ v, ∃ g, F.Ψ (F.loc v g) ^ 2 ≠ 1
  f4 : Nontrivial (H1C F.loc (chRep (F.Ψ⁻¹ * F.delK k)) fun _ => ⊥)
  f4' : Nontrivial (H1C F.loc (chRep (F.Ψ⁻¹ ^ 2 * F.delK k)) fun _ => ⊥)
  f5 : ¬ ∃ j : ℤ, ∀ g : F.G0, F.om k g = F.Ψ g ^ j
  Ψ_N : ∀ v, ∀ g ∈ F.Nv v, F.Ψ (F.loc v g) = 1

  gen : ∀ v, ∃ (u : Fin (F.dv v) → F.Gv v) (a₀ : Fin (F.dv v)), (∀ a, u a ∈ F.Iv v ∧ F.Ψ (F.loc v (u a)) = 1) ∧
    (∀ a, a ≠ a₀ → u a ∈ F.Nv v) ∧
    ∀ (A : Type) [CommGroup A] [Finite A], IsPGroup l A → ∀ χ : F.Gv v →* A, IsOpen (χ.ker : Set (F.Gv v)) →
      (∀ a, χ (u a) = 1) → ∀ g ∈ F.Iv v, χ g = 1

  cl : ∃ h : ℕ, h ≠ 0 ∧ ∀ (A : Type) [CommGroup A] [Finite A], IsPGroup l A → ∀ χ : F.G0 →* A,
    IsOpen (χ.ker : Set F.G0) → (∀ p : F.V × Bool, ∀ g ∈ F.Iv p.1, χ (F.loc2 p g) = 1) → ∀ g, χ g ^ h = 1
  ec : F.EC
  b1p : Module.rank k (H1C F.loc (chRep (F.Ψ⁻¹ ^ 2 * F.delK k)) fun _ => ⊥) ≤ C₀
  b1 : Module.rank k (H1C F.loc2 (chRep (F.Ψ⁻¹.comp F.del.ker.subtype)) fun _ => ⊥) ≤ C₀
  h1 : Module.rank k (H1C F.loc (chRep (1 : F.Γ →* kˣ)) fun _ => ⊤) ≤ 1 + C₀

def Big (C₀ : ℕ) : Prop := 3 * C₀ + 5 + 2 < F.d ∧ ∀ v, 5 < F.dv v

end Frame

end UpperTheory.V.Fr


[ACP_SRC_FLAG] Full original flag_loc
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/FlagLoc.lean#L1-L45

import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.AlgebraTools.OrdExt
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.AlgebraTools.LagTriangular

import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.AlgebraTools.CoiConjSingle

open IsLocalRing Matrix UpperTheory.V.Alg UpperTheory.V.U3 UpperTheory.V.Fr

private theorem entry_of_conj {C : Type} [CommRing C] {n : ℕ} (g : GL (Fin n) C) (f : Fin n → Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C)
    (hg : ∀ i, (g : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C) * f i * ((g⁻¹ : GL (Fin n) C) : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C) = single i i 1)
    (M : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C) (a b : Fin n) :
    ((g : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C) * M * ((g⁻¹ : GL (Fin n) C) : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C)) a b =
      ((g : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C) * (f a * M * f b) * ((g⁻¹ : GL (Fin n) C) : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C)) a b := by
  have h : (g : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C) * (f a * M * f b) * ((g⁻¹ : GL (Fin n) C) : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C) =
      single a a 1 * ((g : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C) * M * ((g⁻¹ : GL (Fin n) C) : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C)) *
        single b b 1 := by
    rw [← hg a, ← hg b]
    simp only [mul_assoc, Units.inv_mul_cancel_left]
  rw [h, Matrix.mul_single_apply_same, Matrix.single_mul_apply_same, one_mul, mul_one]

theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.flag_loc :
    ∀ {H C : Type} [Group H] [CommRing C] [IsLocalRing C] {n : ℕ} (r : H →* Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C) (ψ : Fin n → H →* Cˣ) (g₀ : H), (∀ i j, i ≠ j → IsUnit ((ψ i g₀ : C) - ψ j g₀)) → ((r g₀).map (residue C)).charpoly = ∏ i, (Polynomial.X - Polynomial.C (residue C (ψ i g₀))) → (∀ g, ordProd r (fun i s => (ψ i s : C)) g = 0) → UpperTheory.V.W.HasFlag r.toHomUnits ψ := by
  intro H C _ _ _ n r ψ g₀ hu hc ho
  have h0 := UpperTheory.V.AlgebraTools.ord_ext (R := C) r (fun i => (Units.coeHom C).comp (ψ i)) (fun g => by
    have := ho g
    unfold ordProd at this
    simpa only [Algebra.algebraMap_eq_smul_one, MonoidHom.comp_apply, Units.coeHom_apply] using this)
  obtain ⟨h1, h2, h3⟩ := UpperTheory.V.AlgebraTools.lag_triangular (MonoidAlgebra.lift C (Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) C) H r)
    (fun i => MonoidAlgebra.lift C C H ((Units.coeHom C).comp (ψ i))) (MonoidAlgebra.of C H g₀) h0
    (fun i j hij => by simpa using hu i j hij)
  simp only [MonoidAlgebra.lift_of, MonoidHom.comp_apply, Units.coeHom_apply] at h1 h2 h3
  have h4 := UpperTheory.V.AlgebraTools.lag_ne_zero (fun i => (ψ i g₀ : C)) (r g₀) hu (by rw [← Matrix.charpoly_map, hc])
  obtain ⟨g, hg⟩ := UpperTheory.V.AlgebraTools.coi_conj_single _ h1 h4
  refine ⟨g, fun s => ⟨fun i j hij => ?_, fun i => ?_⟩⟩
  · have h5 := h2 (MonoidAlgebra.of C H s) i j hij
    rw [MonoidAlgebra.lift_of] at h5
    have h6 := entry_of_conj g _ hg (r s) i j
    rw [h5, mul_zero, zero_mul] at h6
    exact h6
  · have h5 := h3 (MonoidAlgebra.of C H s) i
    rw [MonoidAlgebra.lift_of, MonoidAlgebra.lift_of] at h5
    have h6 := entry_of_conj g _ hg (r s) i i
    rw [h5, mul_smul_comm, smul_mul_assoc, hg] at h6
    refine h6.trans ?_
    simp


[ACP_SRC_LAGRANGE] Ordered-product projector calculation underlying flag_loc
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/AlgebraTools/LagTriangular.lean#L1-L109
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.AlgebraTools.EqLagIff
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.AlgebraTools.LagCoi

open Polynomial Matrix IsLocalRing TensorProduct UpperTheory.V.AlgebraTools

private theorem left_unit {R E : Type} [CommRing R] [Ring E] [Algebra R E] (f : E) (L : List E)
    (h : ∀ x ∈ L, ∃ u : R, IsUnit u ∧ f * x = u • f) :
    ∃ u : R, IsUnit u ∧ f * L.prod = u • f := by
  induction L with
  | nil => exact ⟨1, isUnit_one, by simp⟩
  | cons x L ih =>
    obtain ⟨u, hu, e⟩ := h x (by simp)
    obtain ⟨v, hv, e'⟩ := ih (fun y hy => h y (by simp [hy]))
    exact ⟨u * v, hu.mul hv, by
      rw [List.prod_cons, ← mul_assoc, e, smul_mul_assoc, e', smul_smul]⟩

private theorem right_unit {R E : Type} [CommRing R] [Ring E] [Algebra R E] (f : E) (L : List E)
    (h : ∀ x ∈ L, ∃ u : R, IsUnit u ∧ x * f = u • f) :
    ∃ u : R, IsUnit u ∧ L.prod * f = u • f := by
  induction L with
  | nil => exact ⟨1, isUnit_one, by simp⟩
  | cons x L ih =>
    obtain ⟨u, hu, e⟩ := h x (by simp)
    obtain ⟨v, hv, e'⟩ := ih (fun y hy => h y (by simp [hy]))
    exact ⟨v * u, hv.mul hu, by
      rw [List.prod_cons, mul_assoc, e', mul_smul_comm, e, smul_smul]⟩

private theorem mid_zero {R E : Type} [CommRing R] [Ring E] [Algebra R E] {n : ℕ} (f g : E)
    (F : Fin n → E) (i : Fin n)
    (hf : ∀ j, j < i → ∃ u : R, IsUnit u ∧ f * F j = u • f)
    (hg : ∀ j, i < j → ∃ u : R, IsUnit u ∧ F j * g = u • g)
    (h0 : (List.ofFn F).prod = 0) : f * F i * g = 0 := by
  have hlen : (List.ofFn F).length = n := List.length_ofFn
  have hsplit : List.ofFn F = (List.ofFn F).take i ++ F i :: (List.ofFn F).drop (i + 1) := by
    conv_lhs => rw [← List.take_append_drop i (List.ofFn F)]
    congr 1
    rw [List.drop_eq_getElem_cons (by rw [hlen]; exact i.2)]
    simp
  have hT : ∀ x ∈ (List.ofFn F).take i, ∃ u : R, IsUnit u ∧ f * x = u • f := by
    intro x hx
    obtain ⟨k, hk, rfl⟩ := List.mem_iff_getElem.1 hx
    simp only [List.length_take, hlen] at hk
    rw [List.getElem_take, List.getElem_ofFn]
    exact hf ⟨k, by omega⟩ (by rw [Fin.lt_def]; simp; omega)
  have hD : ∀ x ∈ (List.ofFn F).drop (i + 1), ∃ u : R, IsUnit u ∧ x * g = u • g := by
    intro x hx
    obtain ⟨k, hk, rfl⟩ := List.mem_iff_getElem.1 hx
    simp only [List.length_drop, hlen] at hk
    rw [List.getElem_drop, List.getElem_ofFn]
    exact hg ⟨i + 1 + k, by omega⟩ (by rw [Fin.lt_def]; simp; omega)
  obtain ⟨u, hu, hA⟩ := left_unit (R := R) f _ hT
  obtain ⟨v, hv, hB⟩ := right_unit (R := R) g _ hD
  have key : f * (List.ofFn F).prod * g = (u * v) • (f * F i * g) := by
    rw [hsplit, List.prod_append, List.prod_cons]
    calc f * (((List.ofFn F).take i).prod * (F i * ((List.ofFn F).drop (i + 1)).prod)) * g
        = (f * ((List.ofFn F).take i).prod) * F i * (((List.ofFn F).drop (i + 1)).prod * g) := by
          simp only [mul_assoc]
      _ = (u • f) * F i * (v • g) := by rw [hA, hB]
      _ = (u * v) • (f * F i * g) := by
          rw [smul_mul_assoc, smul_mul_assoc, mul_smul_comm, smul_smul]
  rw [h0, mul_zero, zero_mul] at key
  exact ((hu.mul hv).smul_eq_zero).1 key.symm

theorem UpperTheory.V.AlgebraTools.lag_triangular :
    ∀ {R Λ E : Type} [CommRing R] [Ring Λ] [Algebra R Λ] [Ring E] [Algebra R E] {n : ℕ} (ρ : Λ →ₐ[R] E) (χ : Fin n → Λ →ₐ[R] R) (z : Λ), (∀ y : Fin n → Λ, (List.ofFn fun i => ρ (y i) - algebraMap R E (χ i (y i))).prod = 0) → (∀ i j, i ≠ j → IsUnit (χ i z - χ j z)) → CompleteOrthogonalIdempotents (lag (fun i => χ i z) (ρ z)) ∧ (∀ (y : Λ) (a b : Fin n), b < a → lag (fun i => χ i z) (ρ z) a * ρ y * lag (fun i => χ i z) (ρ z) b = 0) ∧ ∀ (y : Λ) (i : Fin n), lag (fun i => χ i z) (ρ z) i * ρ y * lag (fun i => χ i z) (ρ z) i = χ i y • lag (fun i => χ i z) (ρ z) i := by
  intro R Λ E _ _ _ _ _ n ρ χ z hprod hu
  have hzero : aeval (ρ z) (∏ i, (X - C ((fun i => χ i z) i))) = 0 := by
    rw [← List.prod_ofFn, map_list_prod, List.map_ofFn]
    simpa [Function.comp_def] using hprod (fun _ => z)
  obtain ⟨hcoi, hP, -, hcomm⟩ := lag_coi (fun i => χ i z) (ρ z) hu hzero
  have hl : ∀ c j, lag (fun i => χ i z) (ρ z) c * (ρ z - algebraMap R E (χ j z)) =
      (χ c z - χ j z) • lag (fun i => χ i z) (ρ z) c := by
    intro c j
    rw [mul_sub, ← (hcomm (ρ z) c (Commute.refl _)).eq, hP, ← Algebra.commutes,
      ← Algebra.smul_def, sub_smul]
  have hr : ∀ c j, (ρ z - algebraMap R E (χ j z)) * lag (fun i => χ i z) (ρ z) c =
      (χ c z - χ j z) • lag (fun i => χ i z) (ρ z) c := by
    intro c j
    rw [sub_mul, hP, ← Algebra.smul_def, sub_smul]
  have key : ∀ (y : Λ) (c d i : Fin n), (∀ j, j < i → j ≠ c) → (∀ j, i < j → j ≠ d) →
      lag (fun i => χ i z) (ρ z) c * (ρ y - algebraMap R E (χ i y)) *
        lag (fun i => χ i z) (ρ z) d = 0 := by
    intro y c d i h1 h2
    have h := mid_zero (R := R) (lag (fun i => χ i z) (ρ z) c) (lag (fun i => χ i z) (ρ z) d)
      (fun j => ρ (Function.update (fun _ => z) i y j) -
        algebraMap R E (χ j (Function.update (fun _ => z) i y j))) i ?_ ?_ (hprod _)
    · simpa using h
    · intro j hj
      refine ⟨_, hu c j (h1 j hj).symm, ?_⟩
      simp only [Function.update_apply, if_neg (ne_of_lt hj)]
      exact hl c j
    · intro j hj
      refine ⟨_, hu d j (h2 j hj).symm, ?_⟩
      simp only [Function.update_apply, if_neg (ne_of_gt hj)]
      exact hr d j
  have hmid : ∀ (r : R) (c d : Fin n), lag (fun i => χ i z) (ρ z) c * algebraMap R E r *
      lag (fun i => χ i z) (ρ z) d =
        r • (lag (fun i => χ i z) (ρ z) c * lag (fun i => χ i z) (ρ z) d) := by
    intro r c d
    rw [← Algebra.commutes, ← Algebra.smul_def, smul_mul_assoc]
  refine ⟨hcoi, ?_, ?_⟩
  · intro y a b hba
    have h := key y a b a (fun j hj => ne_of_lt hj) (fun j hj => ne_of_gt (hba.trans hj))
    rw [mul_sub, sub_mul, hmid, hcoi.ortho (ne_of_gt hba), smul_zero, sub_zero] at h
    exact h
  · intro y i
    have h := key y i i i (fun j hj => ne_of_lt hj) (fun j hj => ne_of_gt hj)
    rw [mul_sub, sub_mul, hmid, (hcoi.idem i).eq, sub_eq_zero] at h
    exact h


[ACP_SRC_O1] Full original o1 local characteristic-polynomial identity
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/CharpolyFlag.lean#L1-L64
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.FrobeniusSmoothVectors.Integral
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.FrobeniusSmoothVectors.FlagLoc

open IsLocalRing Matrix UpperTheory.V.Alg UpperTheory.V.U3 UpperTheory.V.Fr

private theorem zpow_w_ne' {k : Type} [Field k] {n : ℕ} (hn : n = 2 ∨ n = 3) (a : kˣ) (ha : a ^ 2 ≠ 1)
    (i j : Fin n) (hij : i ≠ j) : a ^ w n i ≠ a ^ w n j := by
  have h1 : a ≠ 1 := fun h => ha (by rw [h, one_pow])
  have h2 : a ≠ a⁻¹ := fun h => ha (by rw [sq]; nth_rewrite 2 [h]; exact mul_inv_cancel a)
  have h3 : (1 : kˣ) ≠ a⁻¹ := fun h => h1 (by rw [← inv_inv a, ← h, inv_one])
  rcases hn with rfl | rfl
  · fin_cases i <;> fin_cases j <;> simp_all [UpperTheory.V.Fr.w] <;> simp_all [eq_comm]
  · fin_cases i <;> fin_cases j <;> simp_all [UpperTheory.V.Fr.w] <;> simp_all [eq_comm]

private theorem residue_eq {O R : Type} [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] [IsCO O R]
    {x y : R} (h : res O R x = res O R y) : residue R x = residue R y := by
  rw [← sub_eq_zero, ← map_sub, residue_eq_zero_iff, ← res_eq_zero_iff O R, map_sub, h, sub_self]

private theorem charpoly_flag' {H k : Type} [Group H] [CommRing k] {n : ℕ} (r : H →* Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) k)
    (κ : Fin n → H →* kˣ) (h : UpperTheory.V.W.HasFlag r.toHomUnits κ) (s : H) :
    (r s).charpoly = ∏ i, (Polynomial.X - Polynomial.C (κ i s : k)) := by
  obtain ⟨g, hg⟩ := h
  have e : ((g * r.toHomUnits s * g⁻¹ : GL (Fin n) k) : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) k)
      = (g : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) k) * r s * (g : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) k)⁻¹ := by
    simp [Matrix.coe_units_inv]
  have hc := Matrix.charpoly_units_conj g (r s)
  rw [← hc, ← e, Matrix.charpoly_of_isUpperTriangular]
  · refine Finset.prod_congr rfl fun i _ => ?_
    rw [(hg s).2 i]
  · intro i j hij
    exact (hg s).1 i j hij

theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.o1 :
    ∀ {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [IsDiscreteValuationRing O] [IsCoef l O] {F : Frame l (ResidueField O)} {C₀ : ℕ} {n : ℕ}, F.Ok C₀ → (n = 2 ∨ n = 3) → ∀ S : Space O F n, ∀ v g, (S.u.r (F.loc0 v g)).charpoly = ∏ i, (Polynomial.X - Polynomial.C (S.u.ψ v i g : S.A)) := by
  intro l _ O _ _ _ _ F C₀ n hF hn S v g
  obtain ⟨g₀, hg⟩ := hF.f3 v
  have h4 : ∀ i, res O S.R (S.ρ.ψ v i g₀) = ((F.Ψ (F.loc v g₀) ^ w n i : (ResidueField O)ˣ) : ResidueField O) :=
    fun i => S.lift.2.1.2 v i g₀
  have hfl : UpperTheory.V.W.HasFlag (S.ρ.r.comp (F.loc0 v)).toHomUnits (S.ρ.ψ v) := by
    refine flag_loc _ _ g₀ (fun i j hij => ?_) ?_ (fun s => S.ρ.ord v s)
    · by_contra hu
      have h2 : (S.ρ.ψ v i g₀ : S.R) - S.ρ.ψ v j g₀ ∈ maximalIdeal S.R := hu
      have h3 := (res_eq_zero_iff O S.R _).2 h2
      rw [map_sub, h4, h4, sub_eq_zero] at h3
      exact zpow_w_ne' hn _ hg i j hij (Units.ext h3)
    · have hd : ((S.ρ.r.comp (F.loc0 v)) g₀).map (residue S.R) =
          Matrix.diagonal fun i => residue S.R (S.ρ.ψ v i g₀) := by
        ext i j
        by_cases hij : i = j
        · subst hij
          rw [Matrix.diagonal_apply_eq]
          refine residue_eq (O := O) ?_
          rw [h4]
          exact S.lift.2.1.1 (F.loc0 v g₀) i
        · rw [Matrix.diagonal_apply_ne _ hij, ← map_zero (residue S.R)]
          refine residue_eq (O := O) ?_
          rw [map_zero]
          exact S.lift.2.2 (F.loc0 v g₀) i j hij
      rw [hd, Matrix.charpoly_diagonal]
  have h := charpoly_flag' _ _ hfl g
  refine Polynomial.map_injective (S.A.val : S.A →+* S.R) Subtype.val_injective ?_
  rw [← Matrix.charpoly_map, Polynomial.map_prod]
  simp only [Polynomial.map_sub, Polynomial.map_X, Polynomial.map_C]
  exact h


[ACP_SRC_TORSION] Full original oneUnitTF
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/CurveDVRSpecialization/SelfTwistEigenvalues/OneUnitTF.lean#L1-L40
import Mathlib

open Polynomial Matrix

theorem UpperTheory.V.Pr.T.oneUnitTF :
    ∀ {R : Type} [CommRing R] [IsDomain R] [IsLocalRing R] (p : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime] [CharP R p] (x : R) (n : ℕ),
      n ≠ 0 → x - 1 ∈ IsLocalRing.maximalIdeal R → x ^ n = 1 → x = 1 := by
  intro R _ _ _ p _ _ x n hn hx hxn
  have hp : p.Prime := Fact.out
  obtain ⟨k, m, hpm, rfl⟩ := Nat.exists_eq_pow_mul_and_not_dvd hn p hp.ne_one
  have hy1 : x ^ p ^ k - 1 = (x - 1) ^ p ^ k := by rw [sub_pow_char_pow, one_pow]
  have hym : (x ^ p ^ k) ^ m = 1 := by rw [← pow_mul]; exact hxn
  have hyM : x ^ p ^ k - 1 ∈ IsLocalRing.maximalIdeal R := by
    rw [hy1]; exact Ideal.pow_mem_of_mem _ hx _ (pow_pos hp.pos _)
  have hmu : IsUnit (m : R) := by
    have h0 : (m : ZMod p) ≠ 0 := by rwa [Ne, ZMod.natCast_eq_zero_iff]
    have h := (Ne.isUnit h0).map (ZMod.castHom (dvd_refl p) R)
    simpa using h
  have hs : (∑ i ∈ Finset.range m, (x ^ p ^ k) ^ i) - m ∈ IsLocalRing.maximalIdeal R := by
    have h : (∑ i ∈ Finset.range m, (x ^ p ^ k) ^ i) - m
        = ∑ i ∈ Finset.range m, ((x ^ p ^ k) ^ i - 1) := by
      simp [Finset.sum_sub_distrib]
    rw [h]
    refine Ideal.sum_mem _ fun i _ => ?_
    obtain ⟨c, hc⟩ := sub_dvd_pow_sub_pow (x ^ p ^ k) 1 i
    rw [one_pow] at hc
    rw [hc]
    exact Ideal.mul_mem_right _ _ hyM
  have hsu : IsUnit (∑ i ∈ Finset.range m, (x ^ p ^ k) ^ i) := by
    by_contra h
    have h' := (IsLocalRing.mem_maximalIdeal _).2 h
    have h2 := Ideal.sub_mem _ h' hs
    rw [sub_sub_cancel] at h2
    exact (IsLocalRing.mem_maximalIdeal _).1 h2 hmu
  have hy0 : x ^ p ^ k - 1 = 0 := by
    have h := geom_sum_mul (x ^ p ^ k) m
    rw [hym, sub_self] at h
    exact hsu.mul_right_eq_zero.1 h
  rw [hy1] at hy0
  exact sub_eq_zero.1 ((pow_eq_zero_iff (pow_ne_zero _ hp.ne_zero)).1 hy0)


[ACP_SRC_CYCLE] charpoly_of_cyc over an arbitrary commutative ring
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/CurveDVRSpecialization/FinPtOf.lean#L13-L28
private theorem charpoly_of_cyc {A : Type} [CommRing A] (M : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) A)
    (h01 : M 0 1 * M 1 0 = 0) (h02 : M 0 2 * M 2 0 = 0) (h12 : M 1 2 * M 2 1 = 0)
    (h012 : M 0 1 * (M 1 2 * M 2 0) = 0) (h021 : M 0 2 * (M 2 1 * M 1 0) = 0) :
    M.charpoly = ∏ i, (Polynomial.X - Polynomial.C (M i i)) := by
  have e : ∀ a b : A, a * b = 0 → Polynomial.C a * Polynomial.C b = 0 := fun a b h => by
    rw [← Polynomial.C_mul, h, Polynomial.C_0]
  have e3 : ∀ a b c : A, a * (b * c) = 0 → Polynomial.C a * (Polynomial.C b * Polynomial.C c) = 0 :=
    fun a b c h => by rw [← Polynomial.C_mul, ← Polynomial.C_mul, h, Polynomial.C_0]
  rw [Matrix.charpoly, Matrix.det_fin_three, Fin.prod_univ_three]
  simp only [Matrix.charmatrix_apply_eq, ne_eq, Fin.reduceEq, not_false_eq_true,
    Matrix.charmatrix_apply_ne]
  linear_combination (-(Polynomial.X - Polynomial.C (M 0 0))) * e _ _ h12
    + (-(Polynomial.X - Polynomial.C (M 2 2))) * e _ _ h01
    + (-(Polynomial.X - Polynomial.C (M 1 1))) * e _ _ h02
    - e3 _ _ _ h012 - e3 _ _ _ h021



[ACP_SRC_CHAR] Full finite-character rigidity argument char_eq
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/CurveDVRSpecialization/FinPtOf.lean#L29-L108
private theorem char_eq {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {G A : Type} [Group G] [TopologicalSpace G] [IsTopologicalGroup G]
    [CommRing A] [IsDomain A] [IsLocalRing A] [IsNoetherianRing A] [Finite (ResidueField A)] [CharP A l]
    (T : Set G) (h : ℕ) (h0 : h ≠ 0)
    (hcl : ∀ (B : Type) [CommGroup B] [Finite B], IsPGroup l B → ∀ χ : G →* B,
      IsOpen (χ.ker : Set G) → (∀ g ∈ T, χ g = 1) → ∀ g, χ g ^ h = 1)
    (a b : G →* Aˣ) (ha : AdicCont (maximalIdeal A) fun g => (a g : A)) (hb : IsOpen (b.ker : Set G))
    (hab : ∀ g, (a g : A) - b g ∈ maximalIdeal A) (hT : ∀ g ∈ T, a g = b g) (g : G) : a g = b g := by
  set m := maximalIdeal A with hm
  set χ : G →* Aˣ := a * b⁻¹ with hχ
  have hχg : ∀ g, χ g = a g * (b g)⁻¹ := fun g => rfl
  have hm1 : ∀ g, ((χ g : Aˣ) : A) - 1 ∈ m := by
    intro g
    have : ((χ g : Aˣ) : A) - 1 = ((a g : A) - b g) * ((b g)⁻¹ : Aˣ) := by
      rw [hχg, Units.val_mul, sub_mul, Units.mul_inv]
    rw [this]
    exact Ideal.mul_mem_right _ _ (hab g)
  have hfinA : Finite (A ⧸ m) := inferInstanceAs (Finite (ResidueField A))
  have hpowN : ∀ (N : ℕ) g, ((χ g : Aˣ) : A) ^ (l ^ N) - 1 ∈ m ^ N := by
    intro N g
    have hx : ((χ g : Aˣ) : A) = 1 + (((χ g : Aˣ) : A) - 1) := by ring
    rw [hx, add_pow_char_pow, one_pow, add_sub_cancel_left]
    exact Ideal.pow_le_pow_right (Nat.lt_pow_self (Fact.out : l.Prime).one_lt).le
      (Ideal.pow_mem_pow (hm1 g) _)
  have key : ∀ (N : ℕ) g, ((χ g : Aˣ) : A) ^ h - 1 ∈ m ^ N := by
    intro N
    haveI : Finite (A ⧸ m ^ N) := Ideal.finite_quotient_pow (IsNoetherian.noetherian m) N
    let q := Ideal.Quotient.mk (m ^ N)
    let χN : G →* (A ⧸ m ^ N)ˣ := (Units.map (q : A →* A ⧸ m ^ N)).comp χ
    let S : Subgroup (A ⧸ m ^ N)ˣ := (powMonoidHom (l ^ N) : (A ⧸ m ^ N)ˣ →* (A ⧸ m ^ N)ˣ).ker
    have hS : ∀ g, χN g ∈ S := by
      intro g
      rw [MonoidHom.mem_ker, powMonoidHom_apply]
      ext
      simp only [χN, MonoidHom.comp_apply, Units.val_pow_eq_pow_val, Units.coe_map, Units.val_one,
        MonoidHom.coe_coe]
      rw [← map_pow, ← map_one q, Ideal.Quotient.eq]
      exact hpowN N g
    let χ' : G →* S := χN.codRestrict S hS
    have hp : IsPGroup l S := fun x => ⟨N, Subtype.ext (by
      have := x.2
      rw [MonoidHom.mem_ker, powMonoidHom_apply] at this
      simpa using this)⟩
    have hval : ∀ g, χ' g = 1 ↔ q ((χ g : Aˣ) : A) = 1 := by
      intro g
      constructor
      · intro e
        have := congrArg (fun x : S => ((x : (A ⧸ m ^ N)ˣ) : A ⧸ m ^ N)) e
        simpa [χ', χN] using this
      · intro e
        apply Subtype.ext
        apply Units.ext
        simpa [χ', χN] using e
    have hopen : IsOpen (χ'.ker : Set G) := by
      apply Subgroup.isOpen_of_mem_nhds _ (g := 1)
      have hU : IsOpen ({x | q (a x : A) = q (a 1 : A)} ∩ (b.ker : Set G)) :=
        ((ha N).isOpen_fiber _).inter hb
      refine Filter.mem_of_superset (hU.mem_nhds ⟨rfl, b.ker.one_mem⟩) ?_
      rintro x ⟨hx1, hx2⟩
      show χ' x = 1
      rw [hval]
      have hbx : b x = 1 := hx2
      simp only [Set.mem_setOf_eq, map_one, Units.val_one] at hx1
      rw [hχg, hbx, inv_one, mul_one]
      exact hx1
    have hT' : ∀ g ∈ T, χ' g = 1 := by
      intro g hg
      rw [hval, hχg, hT g hg, mul_inv_cancel, Units.val_one, map_one]
    intro g
    have := hcl S hp χ' hopen hT' g
    rw [← map_pow, hval] at this
    rw [← Ideal.Quotient.eq, map_one]
    simpa using this
  have hbot : ((χ g : Aˣ) : A) ^ h - 1 ∈ (⨅ N : ℕ, m ^ N) := Ideal.mem_iInf.2 (fun N => key N g)
  rw [Ideal.iInf_pow_eq_bot_of_isLocalRing m (Ideal.IsPrime.ne_top inferInstance), Ideal.mem_bot,
    sub_eq_zero] at hbot
  have h1 := UpperTheory.V.Pr.T.oneUnitTF l _ h h0 (hm1 g) hbot
  have h2 : χ g = 1 := Units.ext h1
  rw [hχg, mul_inv_eq_one] at h2
  exact h2



[ACP_SRC_DIAG] Frame gen/cl diagonal rigidity diag_eq
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/CurveDVRSpecialization/FinPtOf.lean#L109-L182
private theorem diag_eq {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {k : Type} [Field k] [CharP k l] {F : Frame l k} {C₀ : ℕ}
    (hF : F.Ok C₀) {A : Type} [CommRing A] [IsDomain A] [IsLocalRing A] [IsNoetherianRing A]
    [Finite (ResidueField A)] (j : k →+* A) (ρ : NRep F 3 A) (hc : ρ.Cont (maximalIdeal A))
    (hbar : ∀ g i, ρ.r g i i - j ((F.Ψ g ^ w 3 i : kˣ) : k) ∈ maximalIdeal A)
    (hmul : ∀ g h i, ρ.r (g * h) i i = ρ.r g i i * ρ.r h i i)
    (h1 : ∀ v, ∀ g ∈ F.Iv v, F.Ψ (F.loc v g) = 1 → ∀ i, ρ.r (F.loc0 v g) i i = 1) (g : F.G0) (i : Fin 3) :
    ρ.r g i i = j ((F.Ψ g ^ w 3 i : kˣ) : k) := by
  haveI : CharP A l := charP_of_injective_ringHom j.injective l
  obtain ⟨h, h0, hcl⟩ := hF.cl
  choose u a₀ hu _ hgen using hF.gen
  have hstar := UpperTheory.V.Fr.star hF
  have hconj : ∀ x : F.G0, F.conj x = F.star x⁻¹ := by
    intro x; simp [Frame.star]
  have hconjc : Continuous F.conj := by
    have : (F.conj : F.G0 → F.G0) = F.star ∘ (fun x => x⁻¹) := funext hconj
    rw [this]
    exact hstar.2.2.2.1.comp continuous_inv
  have hinv1 : ∀ (x : F.G0) (i' : Fin 3), ρ.r x i' i' = 1 → ρ.r x⁻¹ i' i' = 1 := by
    intro x i' hx
    have := hmul x⁻¹ x i'
    rw [inv_mul_cancel, map_one, Matrix.one_apply_eq, hx, mul_one] at this
    exact this.symm
  let T : Set F.G0 := {x | ∃ (p : F.V × Bool) (a : Fin (F.dv p.1)), x = F.loc2 p (u p.1 a)}
  have hcl' : ∀ (B : Type) [CommGroup B] [Finite B], IsPGroup l B → ∀ χ : F.G0 →* B,
      IsOpen (χ.ker : Set F.G0) → (∀ g ∈ T, χ g = 1) → ∀ g, χ g ^ h = 1 := by
    intro B _ _ hp χ hχ hT
    apply hcl B hp χ hχ
    intro p g hg
    have hcont : Continuous (F.loc2 p) := by
      obtain ⟨v, bb⟩ := p
      cases bb
      · simpa [Frame.loc2] using hF.loc_cont v
      · simpa [Frame.loc2] using hconjc.comp (hF.loc_cont v)
    have hop : IsOpen ((χ.comp (F.loc2 p)).ker : Set (F.Gv p.1)) := by
      rw [← MonoidHom.comap_ker, Subgroup.coe_comap]
      exact hχ.preimage hcont
    exact hgen p.1 B hp (χ.comp (F.loc2 p)) hop (fun a => hT _ ⟨p, a, rfl⟩) g hg
  let a : F.G0 →* Aˣ :=
    { toFun := fun g => ⟨ρ.r g i i, ρ.r g⁻¹ i i,
        by rw [← hmul, mul_inv_cancel, map_one, Matrix.one_apply_eq],
        by rw [← hmul, inv_mul_cancel, map_one, Matrix.one_apply_eq]⟩
      map_one' := Units.ext (by simp)
      map_mul' := fun g g' => Units.ext (by simp [hmul]) }
  let b : F.G0 →* Aˣ :=
    (Units.map (j : k →* A)).comp ((zpowGroupHom (w 3 i)).comp (F.Ψ.comp F.del.ker.subtype))
  have hb : IsOpen (b.ker : Set F.G0) := by
    refine Subgroup.isOpen_mono (H₁ := F.Ψ.ker.comap F.del.ker.subtype) ?_ ?_
    · intro x hx
      rw [Subgroup.mem_comap, MonoidHom.mem_ker] at hx
      rw [MonoidHom.mem_ker]
      show Units.map (j : k →* A) ((F.Ψ (F.del.ker.subtype x)) ^ (w 3 i)) = 1
      rw [hx, _root_.one_zpow, map_one]
    · rw [Subgroup.coe_comap]
      exact hF.Ψ_open.preimage continuous_subtype_val
  have ha : AdicCont (maximalIdeal A) fun g => ((a g : Aˣ) : A) := hc.1 i i
  have hab : ∀ g, ((a g : Aˣ) : A) - b g ∈ maximalIdeal A := fun g => hbar g i
  have hT : ∀ g ∈ T, a g = b g := by
    rintro x ⟨⟨v, bb⟩, a', rfl⟩
    obtain ⟨hIv, hΨ⟩ := hu v a'
    have hΨ0 : F.Ψ ((F.loc0 v (u v a') : F.G0) : F.Γ) = 1 := hΨ
    apply Units.ext
    cases bb
    · have e1 := h1 v _ hIv hΨ i
      simp [a, b, Frame.loc2, e1, hΨ0]
    · have e1 := h1 v _ hIv hΨ i.rev
      have e2 : ρ.r (F.conj (F.loc0 v (u v a'))) i i = 1 := by
        rw [hconj, hstar.2.2.2.2]
        exact hinv1 _ _ e1
      have e3 : F.Ψ ((F.conj (F.loc0 v (u v a')) : F.G0) : F.Γ) = 1 := by
        rw [hconj, hstar.2.2.1, Subgroup.coe_inv, map_inv, hΨ0, inv_one, inv_one]
      simp [a, b, Frame.loc2, e2, e3]
  have := char_eq T h h0 hcl' a b ha hb hab hT g
  exact congrArg Units.val this



[ACP_SRC_FINPT] Full original finPtOf, both triangular branches and all IsCD tests
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/CurveDVRSpecialization/FinPtOf.lean#L183-L344
theorem finPtOf : FinPtOf := by
  intro l _ O _ _ _ _ F C₀ hF S hoff A _ _ _ _ _ ρ hρ hψ g
  haveI : IsLocalHom (algebraMap O A) := UpperTheory.V.Pr.IsCD.isLocalHom
  haveI : Finite (IsLocalRing.ResidueField A) := UpperTheory.V.Pr.IsCD.fin (O := O)
  haveI : IsNoetherianRing A := PrincipalIdealRing.isNoetherianRing
  have hCL : UpperTheory.V.CA.IsCL O A :=
    ⟨inferInstance, UpperTheory.V.Pr.IsCD.complete (O := O), inferInstance, Module.Finite.of_finite⟩
  have hres : ∀ x : ResidueField O,
      IsLocalRing.ResidueField.map (algebraMap O A) x
        = IsLocalRing.residue A (algebraMap (ResidueField O) A x) := by
    intro x
    obtain ⟨o, rfl⟩ := IsLocalRing.residue_surjective x
    rw [IsLocalRing.ResidueField.map_residue]
    congr 1
    rw [IsScalarTower.algebraMap_apply O (ResidueField O) A o, IsLocalRing.ResidueField.algebraMap_eq]
  obtain ⟨ξ, hξ, -⟩ := UpperTheory.V.Fr.point_loc hF (Or.inr rfl) S A hCL
    (IsLocalRing.ResidueField.map (algebraMap O A))
    (fun a => IsLocalRing.ResidueField.map_residue _ a) ρ hρ.cont hρ.bar
  have hent : ∀ g i j, ρ.r g i j = ξ (S.u.r g i j) := by
    intro g i j; rw [← hξ]; rfl
  have hψent : ∀ v i x, ((ρ.ψ v i x : Aˣ) : A) = ξ ((S.u.ψ v i x : (↥S.A)ˣ) : S.A) := by
    intro v i x; rw [← hξ]; rfl
  suffices key : (∀ g : F.G0, ρ.r g 0 1 * ρ.r g 1 0 = 0 ∧ ρ.r g 0 2 * ρ.r g 2 0 = 0 ∧
      ρ.r g 1 2 * ρ.r g 2 1 = 0 ∧ ρ.r g 0 1 * (ρ.r g 1 2 * ρ.r g 2 0) = 0 ∧
      ρ.r g 0 2 * (ρ.r g 2 1 * ρ.r g 1 0) = 0) ∧
      (∀ (g : F.G0) (i : Fin 3), ρ.r g i i
        = algebraMap (ResidueField O) A ((F.Ψ (g : F.Γ) ^ w 3 i : (ResidueField O)ˣ) : ResidueField O)) by
    obtain ⟨hc, hd⟩ := key
    obtain ⟨c1, c2, c3, c4, c5⟩ := hc g
    rw [charpoly_of_cyc _ c1 c2 c3 c4 c5]
    exact Finset.prod_congr rfl (fun i _ => by rw [hd])
  by_cases hOT : S.u.OffTri (RingHom.ker (ξ : S.A →+* A))
  · obtain ⟨I, hI, hIle⟩ := hoff
    have hIP := hIle _ (RingHom.ker_isPrime _) hOT
    let φ : S.B →+* A := (ξ : S.A →+* A).comp (S.inc : S.B →+* S.A)
    have hP : (RingHom.ker φ).IsPrime := RingHom.ker_isPrime φ
    have hlam : S.lamId ≤ RingHom.ker φ := by
      refine sup_le ?_ ?_
      · rw [Ideal.map_le_iff_le_comap]
        intro o ho
        rw [Ideal.mem_comap, RingHom.mem_ker]
        show ξ (S.inc (algebraMap O S.B o)) = 0
        rw [AlgHom.commutes, AlgHom.commutes, IsScalarTower.algebraMap_apply O (ResidueField O) A,
          IsLocalRing.ResidueField.algebraMap_eq, (IsLocalRing.residue_eq_zero_iff o).2 ho, map_zero]
      · rw [Ideal.span_le]
        rintro x ⟨v, i, y, hy, hΨ, rfl⟩
        rw [SetLike.mem_coe, RingHom.mem_ker, map_sub, map_one, sub_eq_zero]
        have e : S.inc (S.ψB v i y) = ((S.u.ψ v i y : (↥S.A)ˣ) : S.A) := rfl
        show ξ (S.inc (S.ψB v i y)) = 1
        rw [e, ← hψent, hψ v i y hy hΨ, Units.val_one]
    have hIφ : I ≤ RingHom.ker φ := fun x hx => hIP hx
    have hmB : S.mB ≤ RingHom.ker φ :=
      hI.trans ((Ideal.IsPrime.radical_le_iff hP).2 (sup_le hIφ hlam))
    have hval : ∀ c : S.B, φ c = algebraMap (ResidueField O) A (res O S.R (c : S.R)) := by
      intro c
      obtain ⟨o, ho⟩ := IsLocalRing.residue_surjective (res O S.R (c : S.R))
      have hk : φ (c - algebraMap O S.B o) = 0 := by
        apply hmB
        show ((c - algebraMap O S.B o : S.B) : S.R) ∈ maximalIdeal S.R
        rw [← res_eq_zero_iff O S.R]
        rw [Subalgebra.coe_sub, Subalgebra.coe_algebraMap, map_sub, AlgHom.commutes,
          IsLocalRing.ResidueField.algebraMap_eq, ho, sub_self]
      rw [map_sub, sub_eq_zero] at hk
      rw [hk, ← ho]
      show ξ (S.inc (algebraMap O S.B o)) = _
      rw [AlgHom.commutes, AlgHom.commutes, IsScalarTower.algebraMap_apply O (ResidueField O) A,
        IsLocalRing.ResidueField.algebraMap_eq]
    have hprod : ∀ L : List (F.G0 × Fin 3 × Fin 3),
        (L.map (fun s => w 3 s.2.1 - w 3 s.2.2)).sum = 0 → (∃ s ∈ L, s.2.1 ≠ s.2.2) →
        (L.map (fun s => ρ.r s.1 s.2.1 s.2.2)).prod = 0 := by
      intro L hL hne
      have hx : (L.map (fun s => S.ρ.r s.1 s.2.1 s.2.2)).prod ∈ S.B := S.mon_mem ⟨L, hL, rfl⟩
      have e1 : (L.map (fun s => ρ.r s.1 s.2.1 s.2.2)).prod = φ ⟨_, hx⟩ := by
        have e2 : S.inc ⟨_, hx⟩ = (L.map (fun s => S.u.r s.1 s.2.1 s.2.2)).prod := by
          apply Subtype.ext
          show (L.map (fun s => S.ρ.r s.1 s.2.1 s.2.2)).prod = ((S.A.val) (L.map (fun s => S.u.r s.1 s.2.1 s.2.2)).prod)
          rw [map_list_prod, List.map_map]
          rfl
        show _ = ξ (S.inc ⟨_, hx⟩)
        rw [e2, map_list_prod, List.map_map]
        congr 1
        apply List.map_congr_left
        intro s _
        exact hent _ _ _
      rw [e1, hval]
      show algebraMap (ResidueField O) A (res O S.R (L.map (fun s => S.ρ.r s.1 s.2.1 s.2.2)).prod) = 0
      rw [map_list_prod, List.map_map]
      obtain ⟨s, hs, hs'⟩ := hne
      rw [List.prod_eq_zero (List.mem_map.2 ⟨s, hs, S.lift.2.2 s.1 s.2.1 s.2.2 hs'⟩), map_zero]
    refine ⟨fun g => ⟨?_, ?_, ?_, ?_, ?_⟩, fun g i => ?_⟩
    · simpa using hprod [(g, 0, 1), (g, 1, 0)] (by simp [w]) ⟨(g, 0, 1), by simp, by simp⟩
    · simpa using hprod [(g, 0, 2), (g, 2, 0)] (by simp [w]) ⟨(g, 0, 2), by simp, by simp⟩
    · simpa using hprod [(g, 1, 2), (g, 2, 1)] (by simp [w]) ⟨(g, 1, 2), by simp, by simp⟩
    · simpa using hprod [(g, 0, 1), (g, 1, 2), (g, 2, 0)] (by simp [w]) ⟨(g, 0, 1), by simp, by simp⟩
    · simpa using hprod [(g, 0, 2), (g, 2, 1), (g, 1, 0)] (by simp [w]) ⟨(g, 0, 2), by simp, by simp⟩
    · have e : S.inc (S.aB g i) = S.u.r g i i := rfl
      rw [hent, ← e]
      show φ (S.aB g i) = _
      rw [hval]
      congr 1
      exact S.lift.2.1.1 g i
  · have htri : (∀ (g : F.G0) (i j : Fin 3), j < i → ρ.r g i j = 0) ∨
        (∀ (g : F.G0) (i j : Fin 3), i < j → ρ.r g i j = 0) := by
      unfold NRep.OffTri at hOT
      rw [not_and_or, not_not, not_not] at hOT
      rcases hOT with h | h
      · left
        intro g i j hji
        rw [hent]
        exact h (Ideal.subset_span ⟨g, i, j, hji, rfl⟩)
      · right
        intro g i j hij
        rw [hent]
        exact h (Ideal.subset_span ⟨g, i, j, hij, rfl⟩)
    have hcyc : ∀ g : F.G0, ρ.r g 0 1 * ρ.r g 1 0 = 0 ∧ ρ.r g 0 2 * ρ.r g 2 0 = 0 ∧
        ρ.r g 1 2 * ρ.r g 2 1 = 0 ∧ ρ.r g 0 1 * (ρ.r g 1 2 * ρ.r g 2 0) = 0 ∧
        ρ.r g 0 2 * (ρ.r g 2 1 * ρ.r g 1 0) = 0 := by
      intro g
      rcases htri with h | h
      · simp [h g 1 0 (by decide), h g 2 0 (by decide), h g 2 1 (by decide)]
      · simp [h g 0 1 (by decide), h g 0 2 (by decide), h g 1 2 (by decide)]
    refine ⟨hcyc, fun g i => ?_⟩
    have hmul : ∀ (g h : F.G0) (i : Fin 3), ρ.r (g * h) i i = ρ.r g i i * ρ.r h i i := by
      intro g h i
      rw [map_mul, Matrix.mul_apply]
      refine Finset.sum_eq_single i (fun b _ hb => ?_) (fun hi => absurd (Finset.mem_univ i) hi)
      rcases htri with ht | ht <;> rcases lt_or_gt_of_ne hb with hb' | hb'
      · rw [ht g i b hb', zero_mul]
      · rw [ht h b i hb', mul_zero]
      · rw [ht h b i hb', mul_zero]
      · rw [ht g i b hb', zero_mul]
    have hbar : ∀ (g : F.G0) (i : Fin 3), ρ.r g i i
        - algebraMap (ResidueField O) A ((F.Ψ (g : F.Γ) ^ w 3 i : (ResidueField O)ˣ) : ResidueField O)
        ∈ maximalIdeal A := by
      intro g i
      refine Ideal.Quotient.eq.1 ?_
      have hb := hρ.bar.1 g i
      change IsLocalRing.residue A (ρ.r g i i) = _ at hb
      change IsLocalRing.residue A (ρ.r g i i) = IsLocalRing.residue A _
      rw [hb, hres]
    have h1 : ∀ v, ∀ x ∈ F.Iv v, F.Ψ (F.loc v x) = 1 → ∀ i, ρ.r (F.loc0 v x) i i = 1 := by
      intro v x hx hΨ i
      have hcp := UpperTheory.V.Fr.o1 hF (Or.inr rfl) S v x
      have hm : (S.u.r (F.loc0 v x)).map (ξ : S.A →+* A) = ρ.r (F.loc0 v x) := by
        ext a b; rw [hent]; rfl
      have := congrArg (Polynomial.map (ξ : S.A →+* A)) hcp
      rw [← Matrix.charpoly_map, hm, Polynomial.map_prod] at this
      obtain ⟨c1, c2, c3, c4, c5⟩ := hcyc (F.loc0 v x)
      rw [charpoly_of_cyc _ c1 c2 c3 c4 c5] at this
      have hev := congrArg (Polynomial.eval (ρ.r (F.loc0 v x) i i)) this
      rw [Polynomial.eval_prod, Polynomial.eval_prod,
        Finset.prod_eq_zero (Finset.mem_univ i) (by simp)] at hev
      have hone : ∀ j, (ξ : S.A →+* A) ((S.u.ψ v j x : (↥S.A)ˣ) : S.A) = 1 := by
        intro j
        rw [RingHom.coe_coe, ← hψent, hψ v j x hx hΨ, Units.val_one]
      simp only [Polynomial.map_sub, Polynomial.map_X, Polynomial.map_C, hone,
        Polynomial.eval_sub, Polynomial.eval_X, Polynomial.eval_C] at hev
      obtain ⟨j, -, hj⟩ := Finset.prod_eq_zero_iff.1 hev.symm
      exact sub_eq_zero.1 hj
    exact diag_eq hF (algebraMap (ResidueField O) A) ρ hρ.cont hbar hmul h1 g i

end UpperTheory.V.Pr


[ACP_SRC_POINTLOC] Broader triangular-residual point_loc; explicitly not imported by ACP2
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/PointLoc.lean#L1-L349
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.FrobeniusSmoothVectors.AlgVring
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.FrobeniusSmoothVectors.Imports
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.FrobeniusSmoothVectors.CoB
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.CommutativeAlgebraHeight.ClSub

open IsLocalRing Matrix UpperTheory.V.Alg UpperTheory.V.U3 UpperTheory.V.Fr

private theorem cont_sub {Γ S C : Type} [TopologicalSpace Γ] [CommRing S] [IsNoetherianRing S] [CommRing C]
    [Algebra S C] [Module.Finite S C] (I : Ideal S) (J : Ideal C)
    (h : ∀ N, ∀ c ∈ J ^ N, c ∈ (I ^ N • ⊤ : Submodule S C)) (a : Γ → C) (ha : AdicCont J a) (N : ℕ) :
    IsLocallyConstant fun g => (I ^ N • Submodule.span S (Set.range a)).mkQ (a g) := by
  classical
  obtain ⟨k, hk⟩ := Ideal.exists_pow_inf_eq_pow_smul I (Submodule.span S (Set.range a))
  set M := Submodule.span S (Set.range a)
  let φ : Γ → C ⧸ J ^ (N + k) := fun g => Ideal.Quotient.mk (J ^ (N + k)) (a g)
  let f : Γ → C ⧸ (I ^ N • M) := fun g => (I ^ N • M).mkQ (a g)
  have key : ∀ g g', φ g = φ g' → f g = f g' := by
    intro g g' hgg
    have h1 : a g - a g' ∈ J ^ (N + k) := Ideal.Quotient.eq.1 hgg
    have h2 : a g - a g' ∈ (I ^ (N + k) • ⊤ : Submodule S C) ⊓ M :=
      ⟨h _ _ h1, M.sub_mem (Submodule.subset_span ⟨g, rfl⟩) (Submodule.subset_span ⟨g', rfl⟩)⟩
    rw [hk (N + k) (by omega), Nat.add_sub_cancel] at h2
    have h3 : a g - a g' ∈ I ^ N • M := Submodule.smul_mono le_rfl inf_le_right h2
    exact (Submodule.Quotient.eq _).2 h3
  have hf : f = (fun x => if hx : ∃ g, φ g = x then f hx.choose else 0) ∘ φ := by
    funext g
    have hx : ∃ g', φ g' = φ g := ⟨g, rfl⟩
    simp only [Function.comp_apply, dif_pos hx]
    exact key _ _ hx.choose_spec.symm
  change IsLocallyConstant f
  rw [hf]
  exact (ha (N + k)).comp _

private theorem two_zero {K : Type} [Field K] {x y s t : K} (h : s ≠ t) (h1 : x + y = 0) (h2 : s * x + t * y = 0) :
    x = 0 ∧ y = 0 := by
  have hy : y = -x := by linear_combination h1
  subst hy
  have hx : (s - t) * x = 0 := by linear_combination h2
  rcases mul_eq_zero.1 hx with h3 | h3
  · exact absurd (sub_eq_zero.1 h3) h
  · exact ⟨h3, by simp [h3]⟩

private theorem or_zero {G K : Type} [Field K] {a b : G → K} (h : ∀ g g', a g * b g' = 0) :
    (∀ g, a g = 0) ∨ ∀ g, b g = 0 := by
  by_contra hc
  push_neg at hc
  obtain ⟨⟨g, hg⟩, ⟨g', hg'⟩⟩ := hc
  exact mul_ne_zero hg hg' (h g g')

private theorem tri_of_bar {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {k : Type} [Field k] [CharP k l] {F : Frame l k} {C₀ n : ℕ}
    (hF : F.Ok C₀) (hn : n = 2 ∨ n = 3) {K : Type} [Field K] (f : k →+* K) (ρ : NRep F n K) (hρ : ρ.IsBar f) :
    (∀ g i j, j < i → ρ.r g i j = 0) ∨ ∀ g i j, i < j → ρ.r g i j = 0 := by
  have hst := UpperTheory.V.Fr.star hF
  have hd : ∀ g h : F.G0, ∀ i, ρ.r (g * h) i i = ρ.r g i i * ρ.r h i i := by
    intro g h i
    rw [hρ.1, hρ.1, hρ.1]
    simp [mul_zpow, map_mul]
  rcases hn with rfl | rfl
  · have hE : ∀ g h : F.G0, ρ.r g 0 1 * ρ.r h 1 0 = 0 := by
      intro g h
      have e := hd g h 0
      rw [map_mul, Matrix.mul_apply, Fin.sum_univ_two] at e
      linear_combination e
    rcases or_zero hE with h01 | h10
    · right
      intro g i j hij
      fin_cases i <;> fin_cases j <;> simp at hij
      exact h01 g
    · left
      intro g i j hij
      fin_cases i <;> fin_cases j <;> simp at hij
      exact h10 g
  · set X := ρ.r F.g0 - ρ.r (F.star F.g0) with hXdef
    have hX : ∀ a b : Fin 3, a ≠ b → X a b = 0 := fun a b hab => ρ.diag a b hab
    have hXm : ∀ g h : F.G0, ρ.r (g * F.g0 * h) - ρ.r (g * F.star F.g0 * h) = ρ.r g * X * ρ.r h := by
      intro g h
      simp only [map_mul, hXdef, Matrix.mul_sub, Matrix.sub_mul]
    have hT : ∀ g h : F.G0, ∀ i : Fin 3, (ρ.r g * X * ρ.r h) i i = ρ.r g i i * X i i * ρ.r h i i := by
      intro g h i
      rw [← hXm, Matrix.sub_apply, hd, hd, hd, hd, hXdef, Matrix.sub_apply]
      ring
    have hE : ∀ g h : F.G0, ∀ i : Fin 3, (ρ.r g * ρ.r h) i i = ρ.r g i i * ρ.r h i i := by
      intro g h i
      rw [← map_mul, hd]
    have hp : (F.Ψ ↑F.g0 : kˣ) ^ 2 ≠ 1 := hF.baseSubgroup_spec
    set p := F.Ψ ↑F.g0 with hpdef
    have hw0 : w 3 0 = 1 := by simp [w]
    have hw1 : w 3 1 = 0 := by simp [w]
    have hw2 : w 3 2 = -1 := by simp [w]
    have hs : F.Ψ ↑(F.star F.g0) = p⁻¹ := hst.2.2.1 F.g0
    have hX0 : X 0 0 = f ↑p - f ↑p⁻¹ := by
      rw [hXdef, Matrix.sub_apply, hρ.1, hρ.1, hw0, hs]
      simp [← hpdef]
    have hX1 : X 1 1 = 0 := by
      rw [hXdef, Matrix.sub_apply, hρ.1, hρ.1, hw1]
      simp
    have hX2 : X 2 2 = f ↑p⁻¹ - f ↑p := by
      rw [hXdef, Matrix.sub_apply, hρ.1, hρ.1, hw2, hs]
      simp [← hpdef]
    have hq : f ↑p - f ↑p⁻¹ ≠ 0 := by
      intro e
      apply hp
      have e2 : f ((p : k) * p) = f ((p : k) * ↑p⁻¹) := by
        rw [map_mul, map_mul, sub_eq_zero.1 e]
      rw [Units.mul_inv] at e2
      have e3 : (p : k) * p = 1 := f.injective (by rw [e2, map_one])
      ext
      simpa [pow_two] using e3
    have h2 : (2 : K) ≠ 0 := by
      have hk2 : (2 : k) ≠ 0 := by
        intro e
        have : (l : ℕ) ∣ 2 := by
          have := (CharP.cast_eq_zero_iff k l 2).1 (by exact_mod_cast e)
          exact this
        have hl := (Fact.out : l.Prime)
        rcases (Nat.le_of_dvd (by norm_num) this).lt_or_eq with h | h
        · have := hl.two_le
          omega
        · exact hF.l_odd h
      intro e
      apply hk2
      apply f.injective
      rw [map_ofNat, e, map_zero]
    have hA : ∀ g h : F.G0, ρ.r g 0 1 * ρ.r h 1 0 = 0 ∧ ρ.r g 0 2 * ρ.r h 2 0 = 0 := by
      intro g h
      have e1 := hE g h 0
      have e2 := hT g h 0
      simp only [Matrix.mul_apply, Fin.sum_univ_three, hX 0 1 (by decide), hX 0 2 (by decide),
        hX 1 0 (by decide), hX 1 2 (by decide), hX 2 0 (by decide), hX 2 1 (by decide)] at e1 e2
      refine two_zero (s := X 1 1) (t := X 2 2) ?_ ?_ ?_
      · rw [hX1, hX2]
        intro e
        exact hq (by linear_combination e)
      · linear_combination e1
      · linear_combination e2
    have hB : ∀ g h : F.G0, ρ.r g 1 0 * ρ.r h 0 1 = 0 ∧ ρ.r g 1 2 * ρ.r h 2 1 = 0 := by
      intro g h
      have e1 := hE g h 1
      have e2 := hT g h 1
      simp only [Matrix.mul_apply, Fin.sum_univ_three, hX 0 1 (by decide), hX 0 2 (by decide),
        hX 1 0 (by decide), hX 1 2 (by decide), hX 2 0 (by decide), hX 2 1 (by decide)] at e1 e2
      refine two_zero (s := X 0 0) (t := X 2 2) ?_ ?_ ?_
      · rw [hX0, hX2]
        intro e
        apply h2
        have : (2 : K) * (f ↑p - f ↑p⁻¹) = 0 := by linear_combination e
        exact (mul_eq_zero.1 this).resolve_right hq
      · linear_combination e1
      · linear_combination e2
    have s01 : ∀ g, ρ.r (F.star g) 0 1 = ρ.r g 1 2 := fun g => hst.2.2.2.2 ρ g 0 1
    have s10 : ∀ g, ρ.r (F.star g) 1 0 = ρ.r g 2 1 := fun g => hst.2.2.2.2 ρ g 1 0
    have t12 : (∀ g : F.G0, ρ.r g 0 1 = 0) → ∀ g : F.G0, ρ.r g 1 2 = 0 := fun h g => by
      rw [← s01]; exact h _
    have t21 : (∀ g : F.G0, ρ.r g 1 0 = 0) → ∀ g : F.G0, ρ.r g 2 1 = 0 := fun h g => by
      rw [← s10]; exact h _
    have t10 : (∀ g : F.G0, ρ.r g 2 1 = 0) → ∀ g : F.G0, ρ.r g 1 0 = 0 := fun h g => by
      rw [← hst.1 g, s10]; exact h _
    have hP : ∀ g h : F.G0, (ρ.r g * ρ.r h) = ρ.r (g * h) := fun g h => (map_mul _ _ _).symm
    have hL : (∀ g : F.G0, ρ.r g 1 0 = 0) → (∀ g : F.G0, ρ.r g 2 0 = 0) → (∀ g : F.G0, ρ.r g 2 1 = 0) →
        (∀ (g : F.G0) (i j : Fin 3), j < i → ρ.r g i j = 0) := by
      intro a b c g i j hij
      fin_cases i <;> fin_cases j <;> simp at hij
      · exact a g
      · exact b g
      · exact c g
    have hR : (∀ g : F.G0, ρ.r g 0 1 = 0) → (∀ g : F.G0, ρ.r g 0 2 = 0) → (∀ g : F.G0, ρ.r g 1 2 = 0) →
        (∀ (g : F.G0) (i j : Fin 3), i < j → ρ.r g i j = 0) := by
      intro a b c g i j hij
      fin_cases i <;> fin_cases j <;> simp at hij
      · exact a g
      · exact b g
      · exact c g
    rcases or_zero (fun g h => (hA g h).1) with a01 | a10
    · have a12 := t12 a01
      rcases or_zero (fun g h => (hA g h).2) with a02 | a20
      · exact Or.inr (hR a01 a02 a12)
      · have hC : ∀ g h : F.G0, ρ.r g 0 2 * ρ.r h 2 1 = 0 := by
          intro g h
          have e := congrFun (congrFun (hP g h) 0) 1
          simp only [Matrix.mul_apply, Fin.sum_univ_three, a01, a01 h] at e
