          linear_combination e
        rcases or_zero hC with a02 | a21
        · exact Or.inr (hR a01 a02 a12)
        · exact Or.inl (hL (t10 a21) a20 a21)
    · have a21 := t21 a10
      rcases or_zero (fun g h => (hA g h).2) with a02 | a20
      · have hC : ∀ g h : F.G0, ρ.r g 2 0 * ρ.r h 0 1 = 0 := by
          intro g h
          have e := congrFun (congrFun (hP g h) 2) 1
          simp only [Matrix.mul_apply, Fin.sum_univ_three, a21, a21 h] at e
          linear_combination e
        rcases or_zero hC with a20 | a01
        · exact Or.inl (hL a10 a20 a21)
        · exact Or.inr (hR a01 a02 (t12 a01))
      · exact Or.inl (hL a10 a20 a21)

private theorem wsum {G : Type} {n : ℕ} (hn : n = 2 ∨ n = 3) (L : List (G × Fin n × Fin n))
    (h : ∀ s ∈ L, s.2.1 ≤ s.2.2) (h0 : (L.map fun s => w n s.2.1 - w n s.2.2).sum = 0) : ∀ s ∈ L, s.2.1 = s.2.2 := by
  have hw : ∀ i j : Fin n, i ≤ j → 0 ≤ w n i - w n j ∧ (w n i - w n j = 0 → i = j) := by
    intro i j hij
    have hij' : (i : ℕ) ≤ j := hij
    rcases hn with rfl | rfl
    · simp only [w]
      refine ⟨by norm_num; omega, fun e => Fin.ext ?_⟩
      norm_num at e
      omega
    · simp only [w]
      refine ⟨by norm_num; omega, fun e => Fin.ext ?_⟩
      norm_num at e
      omega
  induction L with
  | nil => simp
  | cons s L ih =>
    intro s' hs'
    have hs := hw s.2.1 s.2.2 (h s (List.mem_cons_self ..))
    have hL : ∀ t ∈ L, t.2.1 ≤ t.2.2 := fun t ht => h t (List.mem_cons_of_mem _ ht)
    have hnn : 0 ≤ (L.map fun s => w n s.2.1 - w n s.2.2).sum := by
      apply List.sum_nonneg
      intro x hx
      obtain ⟨t, ht, rfl⟩ := List.mem_map.1 hx
      exact (hw _ _ (hL t ht)).1
    rw [List.map_cons, List.sum_cons] at h0
    have e1 : w n s.2.1 - w n s.2.2 = 0 := by linarith [hs.1]
    have e2 : (L.map fun s => w n s.2.1 - w n s.2.2).sum = 0 := by linarith [hs.1]
    rcases List.mem_cons.1 hs' with rfl | hs'
    · exact hs.2 e1
    · exact ih hL e2 s' hs'

private theorem cyc_mem {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {k : Type} [Field k] [CharP k l] {F : Frame l k} {C₀ n : ℕ}
    (hF : F.Ok C₀) (hn : n = 2 ∨ n = 3) {C : Type} [CommRing C] [IsLocalRing C] (f : k →+* ResidueField C)
    (ρ : NRep F n C) (hρ : (ρ.map (residue C)).IsBar f) (L : List (F.G0 × Fin n × Fin n))
    (h0 : (L.map fun s => w n s.2.1 - w n s.2.2).sum = 0) (h1 : ∃ s ∈ L, s.2.1 ≠ s.2.2) :
    (L.map fun s => ρ.r s.1 s.2.1 s.2.2).prod ∈ maximalIdeal C := by
  have hr : ∀ g i j, (ρ.map (residue C)).r g i j = residue C (ρ.r g i j) := fun _ _ _ => rfl
  rw [← IsLocalRing.residue_eq_zero_iff, map_list_prod, List.map_map]
  apply List.prod_eq_zero
  rcases tri_of_bar hF hn f (ρ.map (residue C)) hρ with ht | ht
  · by_cases hex : ∃ s ∈ L, s.2.2 < s.2.1
    · obtain ⟨s, hs, hlt⟩ := hex
      refine List.mem_map.2 ⟨s, hs, ?_⟩
      simp only [Function.comp_apply]
      rw [← hr]
      exact ht _ _ _ hlt
    · push_neg at hex
      exfalso
      obtain ⟨s, hs, hne⟩ := h1
      exact hne (wsum hn L hex h0 s hs)
  · by_cases hex : ∃ s ∈ L, s.2.1 < s.2.2
    · obtain ⟨s, hs, hlt⟩ := hex
      refine List.mem_map.2 ⟨s, hs, ?_⟩
      simp only [Function.comp_apply]
      rw [← hr]
      exact ht _ _ _ hlt
    · push_neg at hex
      exfalso
      obtain ⟨s, hs, hne⟩ := h1
      have hL' : ∀ t ∈ L.map (fun s => (s.1, s.2.2, s.2.1)), t.2.1 ≤ t.2.2 := by
        intro t ht
        obtain ⟨s, hs, rfl⟩ := List.mem_map.1 ht
        exact hex s hs
      have h0' : ((L.map (fun s => (s.1, s.2.2, s.2.1))).map fun s => w n s.2.1 - w n s.2.2).sum = 0 := by
        have e : ((L.map (fun s => (s.1, s.2.2, s.2.1))).map fun s => w n s.2.1 - w n s.2.2) =
            (L.map fun s => w n s.2.1 - w n s.2.2).map Neg.neg := by
          simp [List.map_map, Function.comp_def]
        have hneg : ∀ m : List ℤ, (m.map Neg.neg).sum = -m.sum := by
          intro m
          induction m with
          | nil => simp
          | cons a m ih => simp only [List.map_cons, List.sum_cons, ih]; ring
        rw [e, hneg, h0, neg_zero]
      exact hne (wsum hn _ hL' h0' (s.1, s.2.2, s.2.1) (List.mem_map.2 ⟨s, hs, rfl⟩)).symm

private theorem lc_lemma {X A B : Type} [TopologicalSpace X] (φ : X → A) (g : X → B)
    (hφ : IsLocallyConstant φ) (h : ∀ x y, φ x = φ y → g x = g y) : IsLocallyConstant g := by
  classical
  rcases isEmpty_or_nonempty X with hX | ⟨⟨x₀⟩⟩
  · exact IsLocallyConstant.of_constant _ (fun x => (hX.false x).elim)
  have e : g = (fun a => if ha : ∃ x, φ x = a then g ha.choose else g x₀) ∘ φ := by
    funext x
    have ha : ∃ y, φ y = φ x := ⟨x, rfl⟩
    simp only [Function.comp_apply, dif_pos ha]
    exact h _ _ ha.choose_spec.symm
  rw [e]
  exact hφ.comp _

theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.point_loc :
    ∀ {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [IsDiscreteValuationRing O] [IsCoef l O] {F : Frame l (ResidueField O)} {C₀ : ℕ} {n : ℕ}, F.Ok C₀ → (n = 2 ∨ n = 3) → ∀ S : Space O F n, ∀ (C : Type) [CommRing C] [IsLocalRing C] [Algebra O C], UpperTheory.V.CA.IsCL O C → ∀ (f : ResidueField O →+* ResidueField C), IsResMap O C f → ∀ ρ : NRep F n C, ρ.Cont (maximalIdeal C) → (ρ.map (residue C)).IsBar f → ∃ ξ : S.A →ₐ[O] C, S.u.map (ξ : S.A →+* C) = ρ ∧ S.x0 ≤ (maximalIdeal C).comap (ξ : S.A →+* C) := by
  intro l _ O _ _ _ _ F C₀ n hF hn S C _ _ _ hCL f hf ρ hρc hρ
  obtain ⟨S', hmem, hfin, hCO, hpow⟩ := UpperTheory.V.CA.cl_sub O C hCL
  obtain ⟨hBco, hmB⟩ := co_B hF hn S
  have hJ : ∀ c ∈ maximalIdeal C, c ∈ S' := fun c hc => (hmem c).2 ⟨0, by simpa using hc⟩
  have hmax : ∀ c : S', c ∈ maximalIdeal S' ↔ (c : C) ∈ maximalIdeal C := by
    intro c
    simpa using hpow 1 c
  have hin : ∀ (c : C) (t : ResidueField O), residue C c = f t → c ∈ S' := by
    intro c t h
    obtain ⟨a, rfl⟩ := IsLocalRing.residue_surjective t
    refine (hmem c).2 ⟨a, ?_⟩
    rw [← IsLocalRing.residue_eq_zero_iff, map_sub, h, hf a, sub_self]
  have hres : ∀ (c : S') (t : ResidueField O), residue C (c : C) = f t → res O S' c = t := by
    intro c t h
    obtain ⟨a, rfl⟩ := IsLocalRing.residue_surjective t
    have d : c - algebraMap O S' a ∈ maximalIdeal S' := by
      rw [hmax]
      rw [← IsLocalRing.residue_eq_zero_iff]
      simp only [Subalgebra.coe_sub, Subalgebra.coe_algebraMap, map_sub, h, hf a, sub_self]
    have d2 := (res_eq_zero_iff O S' _).2 d
    rw [map_sub, AlgHom.commutes, sub_eq_zero] at d2
    rw [d2, IsLocalRing.ResidueField.algebraMap_eq]
  have hJN : ∀ N, ∀ c ∈ maximalIdeal C ^ N, c ∈ ((maximalIdeal S' ^ N • ⊤ : Submodule S' C)) := by
    intro N c hc
    have hle : maximalIdeal C ≤ Ideal.map (algebraMap S' C) (maximalIdeal S') := by
      intro c hc
      exact Ideal.mem_map_of_mem (algebraMap S' C) ((hmax ⟨c, hJ c hc⟩).2 hc)
    have hc' := Ideal.pow_right_mono hle N hc
    rw [← Ideal.map_pow] at hc'
    rw [Ideal.smul_top_eq_map]
    exact hc'
  have cone : Cone O S' ρ := by
    refine ⟨?_, ?_, ?_, ?_, ?_⟩
    · intro g i
      exact ⟨⟨ρ.r g i i, hin _ _ (hρ.1 g i)⟩, rfl, hres ⟨ρ.r g i i, hin _ _ (hρ.1 g i)⟩ _ (hρ.1 g i)⟩
    · intro L h0 h1
      have hp := cyc_mem hF hn f ρ hρ L h0 h1
      exact ⟨⟨_, hJ _ hp⟩, (hmax _).2 hp, rfl⟩
    · intro i j
      haveI : IsNoetherian S' C := isNoetherian_of_isNoetherianRing_of_finite S' C
      exact IsNoetherian.noetherian _
    · intro i j N
      exact cont_sub (maximalIdeal S') (maximalIdeal C) hJN (fun g => ρ.r g i j) (hρc.1 i j) N
    · refine ⟨fun v i g => ⟨((ρ.ψ v i g : Cˣ) : C), hin _ _ (hρ.2 v i g)⟩, ?_, fun v i g => rfl⟩
      intro v i
      refine ⟨?_, fun g => hres _ _ (hρ.2 v i g)⟩
      intro N
      refine lc_lemma _ _ (hρc.2 v i N) ?_
      intro x y hxy
      apply Ideal.Quotient.eq.2
      rw [hpow]
      exact Ideal.Quotient.eq.1 hxy
  obtain ⟨ξ, β, h1, h2⟩ := alg hF hn S S' C Subtype.val_injective ρ cone
  refine ⟨ξ, h1, ?_⟩
  rw [Space.x0, Ideal.map_le_iff_le_comap]
  intro b hb
  rw [← hmB] at hb
  show ξ (S.inc b) ∈ maximalIdeal C
  rw [h2]
  apply (hmax _).1
  rw [← res_eq_zero_iff O S', res_map]
  exact (res_eq_zero_iff O S.B b).2 hb


[ACP_SRC_POINTDEFS] Literal P1', P23', SeedIn, AllGood, OffFin, IsLiftA, FinPt and FinPtOf
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/CurveDVRSpecialization/PropagationTheoremGL2.lean#L31-L84

def P1' (S : Space O F n) (M : Ideal S.B) : Prop :=
  ∀ (x : Ideal S.A) (L : S.Loc x) (p : Ideal L.R), Nice L.ρ p → L.ρ.TI p → M ≤ L.π p →
    ∀ P : Ideal L.R, P.IsPrime → P ≤ p → M ≤ L.π P

def P23' (S : Space O F 3) (M : Ideal S.B) : Prop :=
  ∀ (x : Ideal S.A) (L : S.Loc x) (p q : Ideal L.R), Nice L.ρ p → L.ρ.TE p → Relation.ReflTransGen (ThickLink L.ρ) p q →
    (∃ P : Ideal L.R, P.IsPrime ∧ P ≤ p ∧ L.ρ.TI P ∧ M ≤ L.π P) →
    ∀ P' : Ideal L.R, P'.IsPrime → P' ≤ q → L.ρ.TI P' → M ≤ L.π P'

def SeedIn (S : Space O F n) (M : Ideal S.B) : Prop :=
  ∃ (ξ : Ideal S.A) (Z : Ideal S.B), ξ.IsPrime ∧ S.u.TI ξ ∧ Z.IsPrime ∧ M ≤ Z ∧ Z ≤ S.π ξ ∧
    qdim Z = ((1 + n * F.d : ℕ) : WithBot ℕ∞)

def AllGood (S : Space O F n) (M : Ideal S.B) : Prop := ∀ p : Ideal S.A, p.IsPrime → S.u.TI p → M ≤ S.π p

def OffFin (S : Space O F n) : Prop := ∃ I : Ideal S.B, S.FinLam I ∧ ∀ p : Ideal S.A, p.IsPrime → S.u.OffTri p → I ≤ S.π p

end Input

def PropagationTheoremGL2 : Prop :=
  ∀ (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (O : Type) [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [IsDiscreteValuationRing O] [IsCoef l O]
    (F : Frame l (ResidueField O)) (C₀ : ℕ), F.Ok C₀ → F.Big C₀ → ∀ (S : Space O F 2) (M : Ideal S.B),
    S.FinLam M → P1' S M → SeedIn S M → AllGood S M

def PropagationTheoremGL3 : Prop :=
  ∀ (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (O : Type) [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [IsDiscreteValuationRing O] [IsCoef l O]
    (F : Frame l (ResidueField O)) (C₀ : ℕ), F.Ok C₀ → F.Big C₀ → ∀ (S₂ : Space O F 2) (M₂ : Ideal S₂.B),
    S₂.FinLam M₂ → AllGood S₂ M₂ → ∀ (S : Space O F 3) (M : Ideal S.B),
    S.FinLam M → P1' S M → P23' S M → SeedIn S M → AllGood S M ∧ OffFin S

section Points

variable {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] (O : Type) [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] {F : Frame l (ResidueField O)} {n : ℕ}

structure IsLiftA {A : Type} [CommRing A] [IsLocalRing A] [Algebra O A] [IsLocalHom (algebraMap O A)]
    (ρ : NRep F n A) : Prop where
  cont : ρ.Cont (maximalIdeal A)
  bar : (ρ.map (residue A)).IsBar (ResidueField.map (algebraMap O A))
  diag : (ρ.map (residue A)).IsDiag

variable (F n) in

def FinPt : Prop :=
  ∀ (A : Type) [CommRing A] [Algebra O A] [IsCD O A] [Algebra (ResidueField O) A] [IsScalarTower O (ResidueField O) A]
    (ρ : NRep F n A), IsLiftA O ρ → (∀ v i, ∀ g ∈ F.Iv v, F.Ψ (F.loc v g) = 1 → ρ.ψ v i g = 1) →
    ∀ g, (ρ.r g).charpoly =
      ∏ i, (Polynomial.X - Polynomial.C (algebraMap (ResidueField O) A ((F.Ψ g ^ w n i : (ResidueField O)ˣ) : ResidueField O)))

end Points

def FinPtOf : Prop :=
  ∀ (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (O : Type) [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [IsDiscreteValuationRing O] [IsCoef l O]
    (F : Frame l (ResidueField O)) (C₀ : ℕ), F.Ok C₀ → ∀ S : Space O F 3, OffFin S → FinPt O F 3


[ACP_SRC_NICE_DEFS] Full original NicePrimeEx contract
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/CurveDVRSpecialization/FlipTwoThree.lean#L7-L14
noncomputable abbrev qdim {R : Type} [CommRing R] (I : Ideal R) : WithBot ℕ∞ := ringKrullDim (R ⧸ I)

def NicePrimeEx : Prop :=
  ∀ (R : Type) [CommRing R] [IsNoetherianRing R] [IsLocalRing R] (ϖ : R), ϖ ∈ maximalIdeal R →
    ∀ (N : ℕ), 1 ≤ N → ∀ (ι : Type) [Finite ι] (a : ι → Ideal R), (∀ i, qdim (a i) < (N : ℕ)) →
    ∀ I : Ideal R, ((N + 1 : ℕ) : WithBot ℕ∞) ≤ qdim I →
    ∃ p : Ideal R, p.IsPrime ∧ I ≤ p ∧ qdim p = 1 ∧ ϖ ∈ p ∧ ∀ i, ¬ a i ≤ p



[ACP_SRC_LOCAL_DEFS] All ten LocData.Ok fields and full LocProp
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/CurveDVRSpecialization/FlipTwoThree.lean#L15-L59
structure LocData (R : Type) [CommRing R] where
  D1 : ℕ
  TI : Set (PrimeSpectrum R)
  TE : Set (PrimeSpectrum R)
  Good : Set (PrimeSpectrum R)
  Nice : Set (PrimeSpectrum R)
  Thick : Set (PrimeSpectrum R)
  sharp : Ideal R

namespace LocData

variable {R : Type} [CommRing R] (L : LocData R)

def Comp (P : PrimeSpectrum R) : Prop := P.asIdeal ∈ L.sharp.minimalPrimes

def Adj (P Q : PrimeSpectrum R) : Prop :=
  L.Comp P ∧ L.Comp Q ∧ (L.D1 : WithBot ℕ∞) ≤ qdim (P.asIdeal ⊔ Q.asIdeal)

def Link (p q : PrimeSpectrum R) : Prop :=
  p ∈ L.Nice ∧ p ∈ L.TE ∧ q ∈ L.Nice ∧ q ∈ L.TE ∧ ∃ e ∈ L.Thick, L.Comp e ∧ e ≤ p ∧ e ≤ q

def P1 : Prop := ∀ p ∈ L.Nice, p ∈ L.TI → p ∈ L.Good → ∀ P ≤ p, P ∈ L.Good

def flipTwoThree : Prop := ∀ p ∈ L.Nice, p ∈ L.TE → ∀ q, Relation.ReflTransGen L.Link p q →
  (∃ P ≤ p, P ∈ L.TI ∧ P ∈ L.Good) → ∀ P' ≤ q, P' ∈ L.TI → P' ∈ L.Good

structure Ok : Prop where
  good_up : ∀ P Q, P ≤ Q → P ∈ L.Good → Q ∈ L.Good
  ti_down : ∀ P Q, P ≤ Q → Q ∈ L.TI → P ∈ L.TI
  nice_type : ∀ p ∈ L.Nice, p ∈ L.TI ∨ p ∈ L.TE
  nice_good : ∀ I ∈ L.Good, (L.D1 : WithBot ℕ∞) ≤ qdim I.asIdeal → ∃ p ∈ L.Nice, I ≤ p
  nice_thick : ∀ e ∈ L.Thick, L.Comp e → ∀ I, e ≤ I → (L.D1 : WithBot ℕ∞) ≤ qdim I.asIdeal →
    ∃ p ∈ L.Nice, p ∈ L.TE ∧ I ≤ p
  comp : ∀ P ∈ L.TI, P.asIdeal ∈ minimalPrimes R → L.Comp P
  chain : ∀ P Q, L.Comp P → L.Comp Q → Relation.ReflTransGen L.Adj P Q
  dich : ∀ P, L.Comp P → P ∈ L.TI ∨ P ∈ L.Thick
  p1 : L.P1
  p23 : L.flipTwoThree

def Concl : Prop := ∀ I ∈ L.Good, I ∈ L.TI → (L.D1 : WithBot ℕ∞) ≤ qdim I.asIdeal →
  ∀ P ∈ L.TI, P.asIdeal ∈ minimalPrimes R → P ∈ L.Good

end LocData

def LocProp : Prop := ∀ (R : Type) [CommRing R] [IsNoetherianRing R] (L : LocData R), L.Ok → L.Concl


[ACP_SRC_GLOBAL_DEFS] All eight GlobData.Ok fields and full GlobProp
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/CurveDVRSpecialization/FlipTwoThree.lean#L61-L87
structure GlobData (X : Type) where
  TI : Set X
  Good : Set X
  Core : Set X
  Cl : Set X
  EE : Set X

namespace GlobData

variable {X : Type} [TopologicalSpace X] (M : GlobData X)

def CompI (Y : Set X) : Prop := Y ∈ irreducibleComponents X ∧ (Y ∩ M.TI).Nonempty

structure Ok : Prop where
  cl_sub : M.Cl ⊆ M.Core
  conn : IsPreconnected M.Core
  jac : ∀ C, IsClosed C → (C ∩ M.Core).Nonempty → (C ∩ M.Cl).Nonempty
  dense : M.Core ⊆ closure (M.Cl \ M.EE)
  meets : ∀ Y, M.CompI Y → (Y ∩ M.Core).Nonempty
  loc : ∀ x ∈ M.Cl, ∀ Y Y', M.CompI Y → M.CompI Y' → x ∈ Y → x ∈ Y' → Y ⊆ M.Good → Y' ⊆ M.Good
  off : ∀ x ∈ M.Cl \ M.EE, ∃ Y, M.CompI Y ∧ x ∈ Y
  seed : ∃ Y, M.CompI Y ∧ Y ⊆ M.Good

end GlobData

def GlobProp : Prop := ∀ (X : Type) [TopologicalSpace X] [TopologicalSpace.NoetherianSpace X] (M : GlobData X),
  M.Ok → M.TI ⊆ M.Good


[ACP_SRC_CHART_DEFS] Full Chart, ChartOfFlat and GlobOfCharts contracts
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/CurveDVRSpecialization/FlipTwoThree.lean#L89-L108
structure Chart {X : Type} [TopologicalSpace X] (x : X) {R : Type} [CommRing R] [IsLocalRing R]
    (f : PrimeSpectrum R → X) : Prop where
  cont : Continuous f
  pt : f (closedPoint R) = x
  min : ∀ P : PrimeSpectrum R, P.asIdeal ∈ minimalPrimes R → closure {f P} ∈ irreducibleComponents X
  onto : ∀ Y ∈ irreducibleComponents X, x ∈ Y → ∃ P : PrimeSpectrum R, P.asIdeal ∈ minimalPrimes R ∧ closure {f P} = Y

def ChartOfFlat : Prop :=
  ∀ (X : Type) [TopologicalSpace X] (C : Type) [CommRing C] (j : PrimeSpectrum C → X), Topology.IsOpenEmbedding j →
    ∀ (R : Type) [CommRing R] [IsLocalRing R] [Algebra C R] [Module.Flat C R],
      Chart (j (PrimeSpectrum.comap (algebraMap C R) (closedPoint R))) (j ∘ PrimeSpectrum.comap (algebraMap C R))

def GlobOfCharts : Prop :=
  ∀ (X : Type) [TopologicalSpace X] (M : GlobData X), IsOpen M.TI → IsClosed M.Good →
    ∀ (x : X) (R : Type) [CommRing R] [IsNoetherianRing R] [IsLocalRing R] (f : PrimeSpectrum R → X), Chart x f →
    ∀ L : LocData R, L.TI = f ⁻¹' M.TI → L.Good = f ⁻¹' M.Good → L.Concl →
      ((∀ P ∈ minimalPrimes R, (L.D1 : WithBot ℕ∞) ≤ qdim P) →
        ∀ Y Y', M.CompI Y → M.CompI Y' → x ∈ Y → x ∈ Y' → Y ⊆ M.Good → Y' ⊆ M.Good) ∧
      ((∀ P : PrimeSpectrum R, P.asIdeal ∈ minimalPrimes R → P ∈ L.TI) → ∃ Y, M.CompI Y ∧ x ∈ Y) ∧
      ((∃ I ∈ L.Good, I ∈ L.TI ∧ (L.D1 : WithBot ℕ∞) ≤ qdim I.asIdeal) → ∃ Y, M.CompI Y ∧ Y ⊆ M.Good)


[ACP_SRC_NICE] One-dimensional specialization and full nicePrimeEx
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/CurveDVRSpecialization/NicePrimeEx.lean#L1-L125
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.CurveDVRSpecialization.FlipTwoThree

open IsLocalRing

private theorem UpperTheory.V.Pr.npe_height_le_one {B : Type*} [CommRing B] [IsDomain B] [IsNoetherianRing B]
    (m : Ideal B) [m.IsPrime] {f : B} (hf : f ≠ 0)
    (h : ∀ p : Ideal B, p.IsPrime → p ≠ ⊥ → p ≤ m → f ∈ p) : m.height ≤ 1 := by
  by_cases hm : m = ⊥
  · subst hm
    simp
  have hfin := (Ideal.span {f}).finite_minimalPrimes_of_isNoetherianRing
  have hsub : (m : Set B) ⊆ ⋃ P ∈ (Ideal.span {f}).minimalPrimes, ((id P : Ideal B) : Set B) := by
    intro x hx
    simp only [Set.mem_iUnion, id, SetLike.mem_coe]
    by_cases hx0 : x = 0
    · obtain ⟨P, hP, -⟩ := Ideal.exists_minimalPrimes_le (I := Ideal.span {f}) (J := m)
        ((Ideal.span_singleton_le_iff_mem _).mpr (h m inferInstance hm le_rfl))
      exact ⟨P, hP, hx0 ▸ P.zero_mem⟩
    obtain ⟨P, hP, hPm⟩ := Ideal.exists_minimalPrimes_le (I := Ideal.span {x}) (J := m)
        ((Ideal.span_singleton_le_iff_mem _).mpr hx)
    have hPp : P.IsPrime := hP.1.1
    have hxP : x ∈ P := (Ideal.span_singleton_le_iff_mem _).mp hP.1.2
    have hP1 := Ideal.height_le_one_of_isPrincipal_of_mem_minimalPrimes _ P hP
    have hfP : f ∈ P := h P hPp (fun e => hx0 (by simpa [e] using hxP)) hPm
    obtain ⟨Q, hQ, hQP⟩ := Ideal.exists_minimalPrimes_le (I := Ideal.span {f}) (J := P)
        ((Ideal.span_singleton_le_iff_mem _).mpr hfP)
    have hQp : Q.IsPrime := hQ.1.1
    have hfQ : f ∈ Q := (Ideal.span_singleton_le_iff_mem _).mp hQ.1.2
    have hQ0 : ⊥ < Q := bot_lt_iff_ne_bot.mpr fun e => hf (by simpa [e] using hfQ)
    refine ⟨Q, hQ, ?_⟩
    rcases hQP.eq_or_lt with e | hlt
    · exact e ▸ hxP
    · exfalso
      have h1 := Ideal.height_add_one_le_of_lt_of_isPrime hQ0
      have h2 := Ideal.height_add_one_le_of_lt_of_isPrime hlt
      have h3 : (⊥ : Ideal B).height + 1 + 1 ≤ 1 := (add_le_add h1 le_rfl).trans (h2.trans hP1)
      simp at h3
      exact absurd h3 (by decide)
  obtain ⟨P, hP, hmP⟩ := (Ideal.subset_union_prime_finite hfin (f := id) ⊥ ⊥
    (fun i hi _ _ => hi.1.1)).mp hsub
  have hPp : P.IsPrime := hP.1.1
  exact (Ideal.height_mono hmP).trans
    (Ideal.height_le_one_of_isPrincipal_of_mem_minimalPrimes _ P hP)

private theorem UpperTheory.V.Pr.npe_dim_one {R : Type} [CommRing R] [IsNoetherianRing R] [IsLocalRing R]
    (Q : Ideal R) [Q.IsPrime] {f : R} (hfm : f ∈ maximalIdeal R) (hf : f ∉ Q) :
    ∃ p : Ideal R, p.IsPrime ∧ Q ≤ p ∧ f ∉ p ∧ ringKrullDim (R ⧸ p) = 1 := by
  obtain ⟨p, ⟨hp, hQp, hfp⟩, hmax⟩ := set_has_maximal_iff_noetherian.mpr
    (inferInstance : IsNoetherian R R) {p : Ideal R | p.IsPrime ∧ Q ≤ p ∧ f ∉ p}
    ⟨Q, inferInstance, le_rfl, hf⟩
  refine ⟨p, hp, hQp, hfp, ?_⟩
  haveI : Nontrivial (R ⧸ p) := Ideal.Quotient.nontrivial_iff.mpr hp.ne_top
  haveI : IsLocalRing (R ⧸ p) :=
    IsLocalRing.of_surjective' (Ideal.Quotient.mk p) Ideal.Quotient.mk_surjective
  have hf0 : Ideal.Quotient.mk p f ≠ 0 := fun h => hfp (Ideal.Quotient.eq_zero_iff_mem.mp h)
  have hh : (maximalIdeal (R ⧸ p)).height ≤ 1 := by
    refine UpperTheory.V.Pr.npe_height_le_one _ hf0 fun P hP hP0 _ => ?_
    by_contra hfP
    have hle : p ≤ P.comap (Ideal.Quotient.mk p) := fun x hx => by
      rw [Ideal.mem_comap, Ideal.Quotient.eq_zero_iff_mem.mpr hx]; exact P.zero_mem
    refine hmax (P.comap (Ideal.Quotient.mk p))
      ⟨Ideal.comap_isPrime _ _, hQp.trans hle, hfP⟩ (lt_of_le_of_ne hle fun e => hP0 ?_)
    rw [eq_bot_iff]
    intro y hy
    obtain ⟨x, rfl⟩ := Ideal.Quotient.mk_surjective y
    have : x ∈ p := by rw [e]; exact hy
    exact Ideal.mem_bot.mpr (Ideal.Quotient.eq_zero_iff_mem.mpr this)
  have hfm' : Ideal.Quotient.mk p f ∈ maximalIdeal (R ⧸ p) := by
    haveI := IsLocalHom.of_surjective (Ideal.Quotient.mk p) Ideal.Quotient.mk_surjective
    exact map_nonunit _ f hfm
  have hlt : (⊥ : Ideal (R ⧸ p)) < maximalIdeal (R ⧸ p) :=
    bot_lt_iff_ne_bot.mpr fun e => hf0 (by simpa [e] using hfm')
  have h1 := Ideal.height_add_one_le_of_lt_of_isPrime hlt
  have h2 : (maximalIdeal (R ⧸ p)).height = 1 := le_antisymm hh (le_trans le_add_self h1)
  rw [← IsLocalRing.maximalIdeal_height_eq_ringKrullDim, h2]
  rfl

private theorem UpperTheory.V.Pr.npe_chain {R : Type} [CommRing R] (q : LTSeries (PrimeSpectrum R))
    (J : Ideal R) (N : ℕ) (hN : N + 1 ≤ q.length) (hJ : J ≤ (q ⟨1, by omega⟩).asIdeal) :
    (N : WithBot ℕ∞) ≤ ringKrullDim (R ⧸ J) := by
  rw [ringKrullDim_quotient, Order.le_krullDim_iff]
  exact ⟨{ length := N
           toFun := fun i => ⟨q ⟨i + 1, by omega⟩,
             hJ.trans (q.monotone (a := ⟨1, by omega⟩) (b := ⟨i + 1, by omega⟩) (by simp [Fin.le_def]))⟩
           step := fun i => q.strictMono (by simp [Fin.lt_def]) }, rfl⟩

theorem UpperTheory.V.Pr.nicePrimeEx :
    UpperTheory.V.Pr.NicePrimeEx := by
  classical
  intro R _ _ _ ϖ hϖ N hN ι _ a ha I hI
  rw [UpperTheory.V.Pr.qdim, ringKrullDim_quotient, Order.le_krullDim_iff] at hI
  obtain ⟨l, hl⟩ := hI
  obtain ⟨s, hs, hI0, hlast⟩ : ∃ s : LTSeries (PrimeSpectrum R), N + 1 ≤ s.length ∧
      I ≤ s.head.asIdeal ∧ ϖ ∈ s.last.asIdeal := by
    let t : LTSeries (PrimeSpectrum R) := l.map Subtype.val (fun _ _ h => h)
    have ht : t.length = N + 1 := hl
    have hh : I ≤ t.head.asIdeal := l.head.2
    have hle : t.last ≤ closedPoint R := le_maximalIdeal t.last.2.ne_top
    rcases hle.eq_or_lt with e | hlt
    · exact ⟨t, ht.ge, hh, by rw [e]; exact hϖ⟩
    · exact ⟨t.snoc _ hlt, by simp [ht], by simpa using hh, by rw [RelSeries.last_snoc]; exact hϖ⟩
  obtain ⟨q, hq1, hqlen, hqh, -⟩ := PrimeSpectrum.exist_ltSeries_mem_one_of_mem_last s hlast
  have hq : N + 1 ≤ q.length := hqlen ▸ hs
  have e1 : (1 : Fin (q.length + 1)) = ⟨1, by omega⟩ := by
    ext; simp [Nat.mod_eq_of_lt (show 1 < q.length + 1 by omega)]
  rw [e1] at hq1
  set Q := q ⟨1, by omega⟩ with hQ
  have hIQ : I ≤ Q.asIdeal := by
    rw [hqh] at hI0
    exact hI0.trans (q.monotone (a := 0) (Fin.zero_le _))
  have hQ2 : Q < q ⟨2, by omega⟩ := q.strictMono (by simp [Fin.lt_def])
  obtain ⟨g, hg, hgQ⟩ := SetLike.exists_of_lt (show Q.asIdeal < (q ⟨2, by omega⟩).asIdeal from hQ2)
  have hgm : g ∈ maximalIdeal R := le_maximalIdeal (q ⟨2, by omega⟩).2.ne_top hg
  have hna : ∀ i, ∃ x ∈ a i, x ∉ Q.asIdeal := fun i =>
    SetLike.not_le_iff_exists.mp fun h => (UpperTheory.V.Pr.npe_chain q (a i) N hq h).not_gt (ha i)
  choose x hxa hxQ using hna
  haveI := Fintype.ofFinite ι
  have hf : g * ∏ i, x i ∉ Q.asIdeal := fun h => by
    rcases Q.2.mem_or_mem h with h | h
    · exact hgQ h
    · obtain ⟨i, -, hi⟩ := Ideal.IsPrime.prod_mem_iff.mp h
      exact hxQ i hi
  obtain ⟨p, hp, hQp, hfp, hd⟩ := UpperTheory.V.Pr.npe_dim_one Q.asIdeal (Ideal.mul_mem_right _ _ hgm) hf
  refine ⟨p, hp, hIQ.trans hQp, hd, hQp hq1, fun i hi => hfp (Ideal.mul_mem_left _ _ ?_)⟩
  exact Ideal.mem_of_dvd _ (Finset.dvd_prod_of_mem x (Finset.mem_univ i)) (hi (hxa i))


[ACP_SRC_LOCAL] Full original locProp
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/CurveDVRSpecialization/LocProp.lean#L1-L75
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.CurveDVRSpecialization.FlipTwoThree

open UpperTheory.V.Pr in
private theorem UpperTheory.V.Pr.exists_prime_qdim {R : Type} [CommRing R] (J : Ideal R) (n : ℕ)
    (h : (n : WithBot ℕ∞) ≤ qdim J) :
    ∃ I : PrimeSpectrum R, J ≤ I.asIdeal ∧ (n : WithBot ℕ∞) ≤ qdim I.asIdeal := by
  rw [qdim, ringKrullDim_quotient] at h
  obtain ⟨l, hl⟩ := Order.le_krullDim_iff.mp h
  refine ⟨(l 0).1, fun y hy => (l 0).2 hy, ?_⟩
  rw [qdim, ringKrullDim_quotient]
  apply Order.le_krullDim_iff.mpr
  refine ⟨{ length := l.length, toFun := fun i => ⟨(l i).1, ?_⟩, step := fun i => ?_ }, hl⟩
  · rw [PrimeSpectrum.mem_zeroLocus]
    exact (show (l 0).1.asIdeal ≤ (l i).1.asIdeal from l.monotone (Fin.zero_le i))
  · exact l.step i

theorem UpperTheory.V.Pr.locProp :
    UpperTheory.V.Pr.LocProp := by
  intro R _ _ L hL I hIg hIti hIbig P hPti hPmin
  have hW : ∀ p ∈ L.Nice, (∃ P ≤ p, P ∈ L.TI ∧ P ∈ L.Good) →
      ∀ P' ≤ p, P' ∈ L.TI → P' ∈ L.Good := by
    intro p hp hS
    rcases hL.nice_type p hp with hpt | hpt
    · obtain ⟨Q, hQp, _, hQg⟩ := hS
      have hpg := hL.good_up Q p hQp hQg
      intro P' hP' _
      exact hL.p1 p hp hpt hpg P' hP'
    · exact hL.p23 p hp hpt p Relation.ReflTransGen.refl hS
  let Inv : PrimeSpectrum R → Prop := fun Q =>
    (Q ∈ L.TI → Q ∈ L.Good) ∧ ((Q ∈ L.TI ∧ Q ∈ L.Good) ∨
      (Q ∈ L.Thick ∧ ∃ p0 ∈ L.Nice, p0 ∈ L.TE ∧ (∃ P ≤ p0, P ∈ L.TI ∧ P ∈ L.Good) ∧
        ∃ p ∈ L.Nice, p ∈ L.TE ∧ Q ≤ p ∧ Relation.ReflTransGen L.Link p0 p))
  have K : ∀ Q, L.Comp Q → Inv Q → ∀ M : PrimeSpectrum R, Q ≤ M →
      (L.D1 : WithBot ℕ∞) ≤ qdim M.asIdeal →
      ∃ p ∈ L.Nice, M ≤ p ∧ ∀ P' ≤ p, P' ∈ L.TI → P' ∈ L.Good := by
    intro Q hQc hQ M hQM hM
    rcases hQ.2 with ⟨hti, hg⟩ | ⟨hth, p0, hp0n, hp0e, hS, p, hpn, hpe, hQp, hrt⟩
    · have hMg := hL.good_up Q M hQM hg
      obtain ⟨p, hpn, hMp⟩ := hL.nice_good M hMg hM
      exact ⟨p, hpn, hMp, hW p hpn ⟨Q, le_trans hQM hMp, hti, hg⟩⟩
    · obtain ⟨p', hp'n, hp'e, hMp'⟩ := hL.nice_thick Q hth hQc M hQM hM
      exact ⟨p', hp'n, hMp', hL.p23 p0 hp0n hp0e p'
        (hrt.tail ⟨hpn, hpe, hp'n, hp'e, Q, hth, hQc, hQp, le_trans hQM hMp'⟩) hS⟩
  have step : ∀ Q Q', L.Adj Q Q' → Inv Q → Inv Q' := by
    intro Q Q' hadj hQ
    obtain ⟨hQc, hQ'c, hbig⟩ := hadj
    obtain ⟨M, hJM, hM⟩ := UpperTheory.V.Pr.exists_prime_qdim _ _ hbig
    have hQM : Q ≤ M := show Q.asIdeal ≤ M.asIdeal from le_trans le_sup_left hJM
    have hQ'M : Q' ≤ M := show Q'.asIdeal ≤ M.asIdeal from le_trans le_sup_right hJM
    obtain ⟨p, hpn, hMp, hWp⟩ := K Q hQc hQ M hQM hM
    by_cases hQ'ti : Q' ∈ L.TI
    · have hg := hWp Q' (le_trans hQ'M hMp) hQ'ti
      exact ⟨fun _ => hg, Or.inl ⟨hQ'ti, hg⟩⟩
    · have hth : Q' ∈ L.Thick := (hL.dich Q' hQ'c).resolve_left hQ'ti
      refine ⟨fun h => absurd h hQ'ti, Or.inr ⟨hth, ?_⟩⟩
      obtain ⟨p', hp'n, hp'e, hMp'⟩ := hL.nice_thick Q' hth hQ'c M hQ'M hM
      rcases hQ.2 with ⟨hti, hg⟩ | ⟨hQth, p0, hp0n, hp0e, hS, p1, hp1n, hp1e, hQp1, hrt⟩
      · exact ⟨p', hp'n, hp'e, ⟨Q, le_trans hQM hMp', hti, hg⟩, p', hp'n, hp'e,
          le_trans hQ'M hMp', Relation.ReflTransGen.refl⟩
      · exact ⟨p0, hp0n, hp0e, hS, p', hp'n, hp'e, le_trans hQ'M hMp',
          hrt.tail ⟨hp1n, hp1e, hp'n, hp'e, Q, hQth, hQc, hQp1, le_trans hQM hMp'⟩⟩
  obtain ⟨q, hq, hqI⟩ := Ideal.exists_minimalPrimes_le (I := ⊥) (J := I.asIdeal) bot_le
  let P0 : PrimeSpectrum R := ⟨q, hq.1.1⟩
  have hP0I : P0 ≤ I := show q ≤ I.asIdeal from hqI
  have hP0ti : P0 ∈ L.TI := hL.ti_down P0 I hP0I hIti
  have hP0c : L.Comp P0 := hL.comp P0 hP0ti hq
  obtain ⟨p, hpn, hIp⟩ := hL.nice_good I hIg hIbig
  have hP0g := hW p hpn ⟨I, hIp, hIti, hIg⟩ P0 (le_trans hP0I hIp) hP0ti
  have hP0 : Inv P0 := ⟨fun _ => hP0g, Or.inl ⟨hP0ti, hP0g⟩⟩
  have hall : ∀ Q, Relation.ReflTransGen L.Adj P0 Q → Inv Q := by
    intro Q hch
    induction hch with
    | refl => exact hP0
    | tail _ hadj ih => exact step _ _ hadj ih
  exact (hall P (hL.chain P0 P hP0c (hL.comp P hPti hPmin))).1 hPti


[ACP_SRC_GLOBAL] Full original globProp
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/CurveDVRSpecialization/GlobProp.lean#L1-L46
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.CurveDVRSpecialization.FlipTwoThree

theorem UpperTheory.V.Pr.globProp :
    UpperTheory.V.Pr.GlobProp := by
  intro X _ _ M hM
  classical
  have hfin := TopologicalSpace.NoetherianSpace.finite_irreducibleComponents (α := X)
  have key : ∀ Y, M.CompI Y → Y ⊆ M.Good := by
    by_contra hne
    push_neg at hne
    obtain ⟨Yb, hYb, hYbn⟩ := hne
    set G : Set X := ⋃ Y ∈ {Y | M.CompI Y ∧ Y ⊆ M.Good}, Y with hG
    set B : Set X := ⋃ Y ∈ {Y | M.CompI Y ∧ ¬ Y ⊆ M.Good}, Y with hB
    have hGc : IsClosed G :=
      (hfin.subset (fun Y hY => hY.1.1)).isClosed_biUnion
        (fun Y hY => isClosed_of_mem_irreducibleComponents Y hY.1.1)
    have hBc : IsClosed B :=
      (hfin.subset (fun Y hY => hY.1.1)).isClosed_biUnion
        (fun Y hY => isClosed_of_mem_irreducibleComponents Y hY.1.1)
    have hcov : M.Core ⊆ G ∪ B := by
      intro x hx
      have h1 : x ∈ closure (M.Cl \ M.EE) := hM.dense hx
      have h2 : M.Cl \ M.EE ⊆ G ∪ B := by
        intro y hy
        obtain ⟨Y, hY, hyY⟩ := hM.off y hy
        by_cases hg : Y ⊆ M.Good
        · exact Or.inl (Set.mem_biUnion (show Y ∈ {Y | M.CompI Y ∧ Y ⊆ M.Good} from ⟨hY, hg⟩) hyY)
        · exact Or.inr (Set.mem_biUnion (show Y ∈ {Y | M.CompI Y ∧ ¬ Y ⊆ M.Good} from ⟨hY, hg⟩) hyY)
      exact (hGc.union hBc).closure_subset_iff.mpr h2 h1
    obtain ⟨Ys, hYs, hYsg⟩ := hM.seed
    have hG' : (M.Core ∩ G).Nonempty := by
      obtain ⟨x, hxY, hxC⟩ := hM.meets Ys hYs
      exact ⟨x, hxC, Set.mem_biUnion (show Ys ∈ {Y | M.CompI Y ∧ Y ⊆ M.Good} from ⟨hYs, hYsg⟩) hxY⟩
    have hB' : (M.Core ∩ B).Nonempty := by
      obtain ⟨x, hxY, hxC⟩ := hM.meets Yb hYb
      exact ⟨x, hxC, Set.mem_biUnion (show Yb ∈ {Y | M.CompI Y ∧ ¬ Y ⊆ M.Good} from ⟨hYb, hYbn⟩) hxY⟩
    obtain ⟨x, hxC, hxG, hxB⟩ := isPreconnected_closed_iff.mp hM.conn G B hGc hBc hcov hG' hB'
    obtain ⟨z, hzGB, hzCl⟩ := hM.jac (G ∩ B) (hGc.inter hBc) ⟨x, ⟨hxG, hxB⟩, hxC⟩
    rw [hG, hB] at hzGB
    simp only [Set.mem_inter_iff, Set.mem_iUnion, Set.mem_setOf_eq, exists_prop] at hzGB
    obtain ⟨⟨Y, ⟨hY, hYg⟩, hzY⟩, ⟨Y', ⟨hY', hY'n⟩, hzY'⟩⟩ := hzGB
    exact hY'n (hM.loc z hzCl Y Y' hY hY' hzY hzY' hYg)
  intro x hx
  exact key (irreducibleComponent x)
    ⟨irreducibleComponent_mem_irreducibleComponents x, ⟨x, mem_irreducibleComponent, hx⟩⟩
    mem_irreducibleComponent


[ACP_SRC_CHART] Full original chartOfFlat, without Noetherian assumptions
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/CurveDVRSpecialization/ChartOfFlat.lean#L1-L48
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.CurveDVRSpecialization.FlipTwoThree

open PrimeSpectrum in
private theorem UpperTheory.V.Pr.exp_product_chart_local {X : Type} [TopologicalSpace X] {C : Type} [CommRing C]
    {j : PrimeSpectrum C → X} (hj : Topology.IsOpenEmbedding j) {Z : Set X} (hZ : IsIrreducible Z)
    {c : PrimeSpectrum C} (hc : j c ∈ Z) :
    ∃ q : PrimeSpectrum C, q ⤳ c ∧ Z ⊆ closure {j q} := by
  have h1 : IsIrreducible (j ⁻¹' Z) := ⟨⟨c, hc⟩, hZ.isPreirreducible.preimage hj⟩
  obtain ⟨q, hq⟩ := QuasiSober.sober h1.closure isClosed_closure
  refine ⟨q, hq.specializes (subset_closure hc), ?_⟩
  have h2 : Z ⊆ closure (Z ∩ Set.range j) :=
    subset_closure_inter_of_isPreirreducible_of_isOpen hZ.isPreirreducible hj.isOpen_range
      ⟨j c, hc, c, rfl⟩
  refine h2.trans (closure_minimal ?_ isClosed_closure)
  rintro _ ⟨hy, y, rfl⟩
  have h3 : q ⤳ y := hq.specializes (subset_closure hy)
  exact specializes_iff_mem_closure.mp (h3.map hj.continuous)

open PrimeSpectrum in
theorem UpperTheory.V.Pr.chartOfFlat :
    UpperTheory.V.Pr.ChartOfFlat := by
  intro X _ C _ j hj R _ _ _ _
  have hg : GeneralizingMap (PrimeSpectrum.comap (algebraMap C R)) :=
    Algebra.HasGoingDown.iff_generalizingMap_primeSpectrumComap.mp inferInstance
  have hc : Continuous (j ∘ PrimeSpectrum.comap (algebraMap C R)) :=
    hj.continuous.comp (PrimeSpectrum.continuous_comap _)
  refine ⟨hc, rfl, ?_, ?_⟩
  · intro P hP
    refine ⟨isIrreducible_singleton.closure, ?_⟩
    intro Z hZ hle
    have hmem : j (PrimeSpectrum.comap (algebraMap C R) P) ∈ Z := hle (subset_closure rfl)
    obtain ⟨q, hq, hZq⟩ := UpperTheory.V.Pr.exp_product_chart_local hj hZ hmem
    obtain ⟨P', hP', rfl⟩ := hg hq
    have h1 : P' ≤ P := (PrimeSpectrum.le_iff_specializes _ _).mpr hP'
    have h2 : P.asIdeal ≤ P'.asIdeal := hP.2 ⟨P'.isPrime, bot_le⟩ h1
    have h3 : P' = P := le_antisymm h1 h2
    subst h3
    exact hZq
  · intro Y hY hx
    obtain ⟨q, hq, hYq⟩ := UpperTheory.V.Pr.exp_product_chart_local hj hY.1 hx
    obtain ⟨P', -, rfl⟩ := hg hq
    obtain ⟨p, hp, hle⟩ := Ideal.exists_minimalPrimes_le (I := (⊥ : Ideal R)) (J := P'.asIdeal) bot_le
    refine ⟨⟨p, hp.1.1⟩, hp, ?_⟩
    have h1 : (⟨p, hp.1.1⟩ : PrimeSpectrum R) ⤳ P' := (PrimeSpectrum.le_iff_specializes _ _).mp hle
    have h2 : Y ⊆ closure {(j ∘ PrimeSpectrum.comap (algebraMap C R)) ⟨p, hp.1.1⟩} :=
      hYq.trans (closure_minimal
        (Set.singleton_subset_iff.mpr (specializes_iff_mem_closure.mp (h1.map hc))) isClosed_closure)
    exact Set.Subset.antisymm (hY.2 isIrreducible_singleton.closure h2) h2


[ACP_SRC_CHARTS] Full original globOfCharts with all three conclusions
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/CurveDVRSpecialization/GlobOfCharts.lean#L1-L51
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.CurveDVRSpecialization.FlipTwoThree

open IsLocalRing in
theorem UpperTheory.V.Pr.globOfCharts :
    UpperTheory.V.Pr.GlobOfCharts := by
  intro X _ M hTI hG x R _ _ _ f hC L hLT hLG hConcl
  have eG : ∀ P, P ∈ L.Good ↔ f P ∈ M.Good := fun P => by rw [hLG]; rfl
  have eT : ∀ P, P ∈ L.TI ↔ f P ∈ M.TI := fun P => by rw [hLT]; rfl
  have hsub : ∀ P : PrimeSpectrum R, f P ∈ M.Good → closure {f P} ⊆ M.Good :=
    fun P h => hG.closure_subset_iff.mpr (Set.singleton_subset_iff.mpr h)
  have hmeet : ∀ P : PrimeSpectrum R, (closure {f P} ∩ M.TI).Nonempty → f P ∈ M.TI := by
    rintro P ⟨y, hy, hyT⟩
    obtain ⟨z, hzT, hz⟩ := mem_closure_iff.mp hy _ hTI hyT
    rw [Set.mem_singleton_iff] at hz
    rw [← hz]
    exact hzT
  refine ⟨?_, ?_, ?_⟩
  · intro hD Y Y' hY hY' hxY hxY' hYg
    obtain ⟨P, hPm, rfl⟩ := hC.onto Y hY.1 hxY
    obtain ⟨P', hP'm, rfl⟩ := hC.onto Y' hY'.1 hxY'
    have hPG : P ∈ L.Good := (eG P).mpr (hYg (subset_closure (Set.mem_singleton _)))
    have hPT : P ∈ L.TI := (eT P).mpr (hmeet P hY.2)
    have h' := hConcl P hPG hPT (hD P hPm) P' ((eT P').mpr (hmeet P' hY'.2)) hP'm
    exact hsub P' ((eG P').mp h')
  · intro hall
    refine ⟨irreducibleComponent x, ⟨irreducibleComponent_mem_irreducibleComponents x, ?_⟩,
      mem_irreducibleComponent⟩
    obtain ⟨P, hPm, hPY⟩ :=
      hC.onto _ (irreducibleComponent_mem_irreducibleComponents x) mem_irreducibleComponent
    refine ⟨f P, ?_, (eT P).mp (hall P hPm)⟩
    rw [← hPY]
    exact subset_closure (Set.mem_singleton _)
  · rintro ⟨I, hIG, hIT, hID⟩
    obtain ⟨p, hpm, hpI⟩ :=
      Ideal.exists_minimalPrimes_le (J := I.asIdeal) (bot_le : (⊥ : Ideal R) ≤ I.asIdeal)
    haveI hpp : p.IsPrime := hpm.1.1
    let P : PrimeSpectrum R := ⟨p, hpp⟩
    have hPmin : P.asIdeal ∈ minimalPrimes R := hpm
    have hopen : IsOpen L.TI := by
      rw [hLT]
      exact hTI.preimage hC.cont
    have hPT : P ∈ L.TI := by
      have hcl : I ∈ closure ({P} : Set (PrimeSpectrum R)) :=
        (PrimeSpectrum.le_iff_mem_closure P I).mp hpI
      obtain ⟨z, hzT, hz⟩ := mem_closure_iff.mp hcl _ hopen hIT
      rw [Set.mem_singleton_iff] at hz
      rw [← hz]
      exact hzT
    have hPG := hConcl I hIG hIT hID P hPT hPmin
    exact ⟨closure {f P}, ⟨hC.min P hPmin, f P, subset_closure (Set.mem_singleton _), (eT P).mp hPT⟩,
      hsub P ((eG P).mp hPG)⟩


[ACP_SRC_FINEND] Already reconstructed AFE20: same-frame FinPt to FinEnd transport
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/CurveDVRSpecialization/FinEndOfFinPt.lean#L1-L48
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.CurveDVRSpecialization.RatTorsionFree
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.CurveDVRSpecialization.HostFrameEx
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.CurveDVRSpecialization.PropagationTheoremGL2
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.CurveDVRSpecialization.ExistsNrep3
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.GaloisLibrary.SelmerTransport.GalEmbTop

open NumberField IsLocalRing Formalism.ConjugateSelfDual ReciprocityTheory.Gal GlobalTheory.GalOfCusp UpperTheory.V.HV UpperTheory.V.U3 UpperTheory.V.Aut UpperTheory.V.Pr UpperTheory.V

theorem UpperTheory.V.Pr.finEnd_of_finPt :
    ∀ {Ω : Type} [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] {η : G ℚ Ω →* ℤˣ} {Ψ : G ℚ Ω →* Oˣ} {σ g₁ : G ℚ Ω} {B : ValuationSubring Ω} {M : ℕ}
      {L : Type} [Field L] [NumberField L] [Algebra L Ω] {Φ : Fr.Frame l (ResidueField O)} (k : GalLink η Ψ σ g₁ B M L Φ) [CharP (ResidueField O) l] {C₀ : ℕ}, Φ.Ok C₀ → l ∣ M → σ * σ = 1 →
    FinPt O Φ 3 → FinEnd l η Ψ σ g₁ B := by
  intro Ω _ _ _ l _ O _ _ η Ψ σ g₁ B M L _ _ _ Φ k _ C₀ hok hl hσ hF
  haveI : Module.IsTorsionFree L Ω := DivisionSemiring.to_moduleIsTorsionFree
  haveI : IsAlgClosure L Ω := ⟨IsAlgClosure.isAlgClosed ℚ, Algebra.IsAlgebraic.tower_top (K := ℚ) L⟩
  have hidx : (Base.galOf ℚ L Ω).index = Module.finrank ℚ L := (Base.galEmbTop ℚ L Ω).2.2
  refine ⟨Base.galOf ℚ L Ω, ⟨by rw [hidx]; exact Module.finrank_pos.ne'⟩, ?_⟩
  intro A _ _ _ h0 r hr g hg _
  haveI : IsNoetherianRing A := PrincipalIdealRing.isNoetherianRing
  let f : ResidueField O →+* A := Ideal.Quotient.lift (maximalIdeal O) (algebraMap O A) h0
  have hf : ∀ a : O, f (residue O a) = algebraMap O A a := fun a => rfl
  letI : Algebra (ResidueField O) A := f.toAlgebra
  haveI : IsScalarTower O (ResidueField O) A := IsScalarTower.of_algebraMap_eq fun a => (hf a).symm
  obtain ⟨ρ, h1, h2, h3⟩ := exists_nrep3 k hok hl hσ r hr
  have hpow : ∀ (s : G ℚ Ω) (n : ℤ), algebraMap O A ((Ψ s ^ n : Oˣ) : O) =
      f ((barChar O Ψ s ^ n : (ResidueField O)ˣ) : ResidueField O) := by
    intro s n
    rw [← hf]
    congr 1
    exact congrArg Units.val (map_zpow (Units.map (residue O).toMonoidHom) (Ψ s) n)
  have key := hF A ρ h1 (by
    intro v i g hg h
    apply Units.ext
    rw [h3 v i g hg, UnitaryThreeInertia.psi, hpow]
    rw [k.j_loc, k.psi] at h
    rw [h]; simp)
  obtain ⟨s, rfl⟩ : ∃ s, Base.galEmb ℚ L Ω s = g := (Base.range_galEmb ℚ L Ω).ge hg
  rw [← h2, key]
  have : ((r0 η Ψ (Base.galEmb ℚ L Ω s)).mat).map (algebraMap O A) = Matrix.diagonal fun i =>
      algebraMap O A ((Ψ (Base.galEmb ℚ L Ω s) ^ wt1 i : Oˣ) : O) := by
    rw [GG.mat, unitaryThreeReflection_left, tor_val, Matrix.diagonal_map (map_zero _)]
  rw [this, Matrix.charpoly_diagonal]
  refine Finset.prod_congr rfl fun i _ => ?_
  rw [hpow]
  have h5 : Φ.Ψ (k.p0 s) = barChar O Ψ (Base.galEmb ℚ L Ω s) := k.psi _
  have h6 : Fr.w 3 i = wt1 i := by fin_cases i <;> rfl
  rw [h5, h6]
  rfl


[ACP_SRC_ENDS] Original OrdPt, FinEnd, AutEnd2/3, HostEnd and GdEnd
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/CurveDVRSpecialization/RatTorsionFree.lean#L20-L68

structure OrdPt {A : Type} [CommRing A] [IsLocalRing A] [Algebra O A] (r : G ℚ Ω →* GG 3 A) : Prop where
  red : UnitaryThreeInertia.Red η Ψ r
  cont : UnitaryThreeInertia.AdicCont (maximalIdeal A) r
  norm : Normalised σ g₁ r
  away : ∀ B' : ValuationSubring Ω, ¬ B'.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 → ∀ s ∈ Ine (F := ℚ) B', η s = 1 → Ψ s = 1 → r s = 1
  ord : UnitaryThreeInertia.CondT (η.comp (Dec (F := ℚ) B).subtype) (Ψ.comp (Dec (F := ℚ) B).subtype)
    ((Ine (F := ℚ) B).subgroupOf (Dec (F := ℚ) B)) (r.comp (Dec (F := ℚ) B).subtype)

def FinEnd : Prop :=
  ∃ U : Subgroup (G ℚ Ω), U.FiniteIndex ∧ ∀ (A : Type) [CommRing A] [Algebra O A] [IsCD O A],
    (∀ a ∈ maximalIdeal O, algebraMap O A a = 0) → ∀ r : G ℚ Ω →* GG 3 A, OrdPt l η Ψ σ g₁ B r →
      ∀ g ∈ U, η g = 1 → (r g).mat.charpoly = (((r0 η Ψ g).mat).map (algebraMap O A)).charpoly

end Ord

variable {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] (c : Coef l) (η : G ℚ Ω →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : G ℚ Ω →* c.Oˣ) (σ g₁ : G ℚ Ω) (B : ValuationSubring Ω)

def AutEnd3 (K : Type) [Field K] [NumberField K] [IsCMField K] (ι : Qbar l ≃+* ℂ) : Prop :=
  ∀ (A : Subalgebra c.O (PadicAlgCl l)) [IsDiscreteValuationRing A] [Module.Finite c.O A] (r : G ℚ Ω →* GG 3 A),
    OrdPt l η Ψ σ g₁ B r →
    (∀ U : Subgroup (G ℚ Ω), IsOpen (U : Set (G ℚ Ω)) → U ≤ η.ker → MatIrr U (mats (UnitaryThreeInertia.push A.val r))) →
    ∀ [Algebra K Ω] (ρ : G K Ω →* GL (Fin 3) (Qbar l)),
      (∀ s, (ρ s).val = (UnitaryThreeInertia.push A.val r (Base.galEmb ℚ K Ω s)).mat) →
      Af.IsAutCsd ι (Af.tw ρ (cycBar K Ω l)⁻¹)

def AutEnd2 (e : ℕ) (ι : Qbar l ≃+* ℂ) : Prop :=
  ∀ (A : Subalgebra c.O (PadicAlgCl l)) [IsDiscreteValuationRing A] [Module.Finite c.O A] (r : G ℚ Ω →* GG 3 A),
    OrdPt l η Ψ σ g₁ B r → TypeII η (UnitaryThreeInertia.push A.val r) → (∀ g ∈ η.ker, (r g).mat 1 1 = 1) →
    ∀ W : G ℚ Ω →* GL (Fin 2) (PadicAlgCl l),
      (∀ g ∈ η.ker, (W g).val = corner (UnitaryThreeInertia.push A.val r g).mat) →
      (W σ).val.trace = 0 →
      ∃ f : Af.Newform (2 * e + 1), f.Att ι (Af.tw W (cycBar ℚ Ω l ^ (-(e : ℤ))))

def HostEnd : Prop :=
  ∀ (K : Type) [Field K] [NumberField K] [IsCMField K], IsQi K →
  ∀ (Ω : Type) [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (ι : Qbar l ≃+* ℂ) (c : Coef l)
    (N : ℕ) [NeZero N] (e : ℕ) (ψ : DirichletCharacter c.O N) (o₁ : ℕ), UnitaryThreeInertia.HostData l e ψ o₁ →
  ∀ σ : G ℚ Ω, IsCC σ → ∀ B : ValuationSubring Ω, B.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 →
  ∃ g₁, Marks (UnitaryThreeInertia.eta4 Ω) (Chr.Psi l e (Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ)) σ g₁ ∧
    FinEnd l (UnitaryThreeInertia.eta4 Ω) (Chr.Psi l e (Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ)) σ g₁ B ∧
    AutEnd3 c (UnitaryThreeInertia.eta4 Ω) (Chr.Psi l e (Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ)) σ g₁ B K ι

def GdEnd : Prop :=
  ∀ (Ω : Type) [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (K : Type) [Field K] [NumberField K] [IsCMField K] [Algebra K Ω]
    (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (c : Coef l) (N : ℕ) [NeZero N] (e : ℕ) (ψ : DirichletCharacter c.O N) (D : Dat Ω K c e ψ)
    (ι : Qbar l ≃+* ℂ) (B : ValuationSubring Ω), B.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 →
  ∃ g₁, Marks D.η D.Ψ D.cc g₁ ∧ FinEnd l D.η D.Ψ D.cc g₁ B ∧ AutEnd3 c D.η D.Ψ D.cc g₁ B K ι ∧
    AutEnd2 c D.η D.Ψ D.cc g₁ B e ι

      (∀ s, (ρ s).val = (UnitaryThreeInertia.push A.val r (Base.galEmb ℚ K Ω s)).mat) →
      _root_.UpperTheory.V.Z.Win.IsAutCsdA ι (Af.tw ρ (cycBar K Ω l)⁻¹)
def HostEndAlt : Prop :=

  ∀ (Ω : Type) [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (ι : Qbar l ≃+* ℂ) (c : Coef l)

    _root_.UpperTheory.V.Pr.AutEnd3Alt c (UnitaryThreeInertia.eta4 Ω) (Chr.Psi l e (Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ)) σ g₁ B K ι

    (ι : Qbar l ≃+* ℂ) (B : ValuationSubring Ω), B.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 →
  ∃ g₁, Marks D.η D.Ψ D.cc g₁ ∧ FinEnd l D.η D.Ψ D.cc g₁ B ∧ _root_.UpperTheory.V.Pr.AutEnd3Alt c D.η D.Ψ D.cc g₁ B K ι ∧
    _root_.UpperTheory.V.Z.Win.Dn.AutEnd2Ao c D.η D.Ψ D.cc g₁ B e ι

[ACP_SRC_AUT3] Original IsAutCsd is precisely AutCsd0
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/AutomorphicForms/BlochKatoSelmerVanishes.lean#L77-L86
def AttachedSys (ι : Qbar l ≃+* ℂ) (r : G F Ω →* GL (Fin n) (Qbar l))
    (sat : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F) → Multiset ℂˣ → Prop) : Prop :=
  IsSSRep r ∧ ∀ᶠ w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F) in Filter.cofinite, ∃ B, sat w B ∧ FrobEig ι r w (algSat n (normV w) B)

def AttC [IsTotallyComplex F] (ι : Qbar l ≃+* ℂ) (r : G F Ω →* GL (Fin n) (Qbar l)) (π : CuspSub F n) : Prop :=
  AttachedSys ι r (CG.mk π).HasSat

def IsAutCsd [IsCMField F] (ι : Qbar l ≃+* ℂ) (r : G F Ω →* GL (Fin n) (Qbar l)) : Prop :=
  ∃ π : CuspSub F n, (CG.mk π).IsRegAlg ∧ GlobalTheory.ContraPair.DualIsConj π ∧ AttC ι r π

theorem UpperTheory.V.Pr.absMain :
    UpperTheory.V.Pr.AbsMain := by
  intro l _ O _ _ _ _ _ F T₂ T₃ J₂ J₃ m₂ m₃
  obtain ⟨Φ₂, hΦ₂, hs₂⟩ := UpperTheory.V.Pr.phiEx l O F 2 T₂
  obtain ⟨Φ₃, hΦ₃, hs₃⟩ := UpperTheory.V.Pr.phiEx l O F 3 T₃
  obtain ⟨Λ₂, hΛ₂⟩ := m₂.fin
  obtain ⟨Λ₃, hΛ₃⟩ := m₃.fin
  have fl₂ := UpperTheory.V.Pr.finLamOf l O F 2 T₂ Φ₂ hΦ₂ hs₂ Λ₂ J₂ hΛ₂
  have fl₃ := UpperTheory.V.Pr.finLamOf l O F 3 T₃ Φ₃ hΦ₃ hs₃ Λ₃ J₃ hΛ₃
  have p₂ := UpperTheory.V.Pr.p1Of l O F 2 T₂ Φ₂ hΦ₂ m₂.n4 J₂ m₂.p1
  have p₃ := UpperTheory.V.Pr.p1Of l O F 3 T₃ Φ₃ hΦ₃ m₃.n4 J₃ m₃.p1
  have q₃ := UpperTheory.V.Pr.rankThree_deformationSetup_splitting_condition l O F T₃ Φ₃ hΦ₃ m₃.n4 J₃ m₃.p3
  have s₂ := UpperTheory.V.Pr.seedOf l O F 2 T₂ Φ₂ hΦ₂ J₂ m₂.unr m₂.dim m₂.seed
  have s₃ := UpperTheory.V.Pr.seedOf l O F 3 T₃ Φ₃ hΦ₃ J₃ m₃.unr m₃.dim m₃.seed
  have g₂ := UpperTheory.V.Pr.components_good_rank_two l O F T₂.C₀ T₂.ok T₂.big T₂.S _ fl₂ p₂ s₂
  obtain ⟨g₃, off⟩ := UpperTheory.V.Pr.components_good_rank_three l O F T₃.C₀ T₃.ok T₃.big T₂.S _ fl₂ g₂ T₃.S _ fl₃ p₃ q₃ s₃
  exact ⟨UpperTheory.V.Pr.goodPtOf l O F 2 T₂ Φ₂ hΦ₂ J₂ g₂,
    UpperTheory.V.Pr.goodPtOf l O F 3 T₃ Φ₃ hΦ₃ J₃ g₃,
    UpperTheory.V.Pr.finPtOf l O F T₃.C₀ T₃.ok T₃.S off⟩

private theorem comp_min {A : Type} [CommRing A] {Y : Set (PrimeSpectrum A)}
    (hY : Y ∈ irreducibleComponents (PrimeSpectrum A)) : ∃ η ∈ minimalPrimes A, ∀ p, p ∈ Y ↔ η ≤ p.asIdeal := by
  rw [← PrimeSpectrum.zeroLocus_minimalPrimes] at hY
  obtain ⟨η, hη, rfl⟩ := hY
  exact ⟨η, hη, fun _ => Iff.rfl⟩

variable {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [IsDiscreteValuationRing O] [IsCoef l O]
  {F : Frame l (ResidueField O)} {C₀ : ℕ}

private def gd (S : Space O F 2) (M : Ideal S.B) : GlobData (PrimeSpectrum S.A) where
  TI := {p | S.u.TI p.asIdeal}
  Good := {p | M ≤ S.π p.asIdeal}
  Core := S.BB
  Cl := {p | p ∈ S.BB ∧ p.asIdeal.IsMaximal}
  EE := ∅

private def ld {S : Space O F 2} (M : Ideal S.B) {x : Ideal S.A} (L : S.Loc x) : LocData L.R where
  D1 := 2 * F.d + 1
  TI := {p | L.ρ.TI p.asIdeal}
  TE := ∅
  Good := {p | M ≤ L.π p.asIdeal}
  Nice := {p | Nice L.ρ p.asIdeal}
  Thick := ∅
  sharp := ⊥

private theorem ti_down {R : Type} [CommRing R] (ρ : NRep F 2 R) {P Q : Ideal R} (h : P ≤ Q) (hQ : ρ.TI Q) :
    ρ.TI P :=
  ⟨⟨fun a => hQ.1.1 (a.trans h), fun a => hQ.1.2 (a.trans h)⟩, fun a => absurd a (by decide)⟩

private theorem ld_ok (hF : F.Ok C₀) (hB : F.Big C₀) {S : Space O F 2} {M : Ideal S.B} (hM : S.FinLam M)
    (h1 : P1' S M) {x : Ideal S.A} (hx : ¬ S.u.triP ≤ x) (L : S.Loc x) : (ld M L).Ok := by
  obtain ⟨hc, ht⟩ := sharp2 hF S x L hB hx
  refine ⟨?_, ?_, ?_, ?_, ?_, ?_, ?_, ?_, ?_, ?_⟩
  · intro P Q h hP
    exact le_trans hP (Ideal.comap_mono (Ideal.comap_mono h))
  · intro P Q h hQ
    exact ti_down _ h hQ
  · intro p hp
    exact Or.inl ⟨hp.off, fun a => absurd a (by decide)⟩
  · intro I hI hd
    obtain ⟨p, hp, hle⟩ := exists_nice_prime_above_of_large_dim l O F C₀ 2 hF hB (Or.inl rfl) S x L hx M hM I.asIdeal hI hd
    exact ⟨⟨p, hp.prime⟩, hp, hle⟩
  · intro e he
    exact absurd he (Set.notMem_empty _)
  · intro P _ hP
    exact hP
  · intro P Q hP hQ
    have key : ∀ q : Ideal L.R, Relation.ReflTransGen
        (fun P Q => P ∈ (⊥ : Ideal L.R).minimalPrimes ∧ Q ∈ (⊥ : Ideal L.R).minimalPrimes ∧
          ((2 * F.d + 1 : ℕ) : WithBot ℕ∞) ≤ ringKrullDim (L.R ⧸ (P ⊔ Q))) P.asIdeal q →
        ∀ Q : PrimeSpectrum L.R, Q.asIdeal = q → Relation.ReflTransGen (ld M L).Adj P Q := by
      intro q h
      induction h with
      | refl =>
        intro Q hQ
        rw [PrimeSpectrum.ext hQ]
      | @tail b c _ hbc ih =>
        intro Q hQ
        subst hQ
        exact (ih ⟨b, hbc.1.1.1⟩ rfl).tail hbc
    exact key _ (hc.2 _ hP _ hQ) Q rfl
  · intro P hP
    exact Or.inl (ht _ hP)
  · intro p hp hI hG P hP
    exact h1 x L p.asIdeal hp hI hG P.asIdeal P.2 hP
  · intro p _ he
    exact absurd he (Set.notMem_empty _)

private theorem ti_open (S : Space O F 2) (M : Ideal S.B) : IsOpen (gd S M).TI := by
  have : (gd S M).TI = (PrimeSpectrum.zeroLocus (S.u.triP : Set S.A))ᶜ ∩
      (PrimeSpectrum.zeroLocus (S.u.triM : Set S.A))ᶜ := by
    ext p
    exact ⟨fun h => h.1, fun h => ⟨h, fun a => absurd a (by decide)⟩⟩
  rw [this]
  exact (PrimeSpectrum.isClosed_zeroLocus _).isOpen_compl.inter (PrimeSpectrum.isClosed_zeroLocus _).isOpen_compl

private theorem good_closed (S : Space O F 2) (M : Ideal S.B) : IsClosed (gd S M).Good := by
  have : (gd S M).Good = PrimeSpectrum.zeroLocus (M.map (S.inc : S.B →+* S.A) : Set S.A) := by
    ext p
    exact Ideal.map_le_iff_le_comap.symm
  rw [this]
  exact PrimeSpectrum.isClosed_zeroLocus _

private theorem at_pt (hF : F.Ok C₀) (hB : F.Big C₀) {S : Space O F 2} {M : Ideal S.B} (hM : S.FinLam M)
    (h1 : P1' S M) {x : PrimeSpectrum S.A} (hx : ¬ S.u.triP ≤ x.asIdeal) (L : S.Loc x.asIdeal) :
    (∀ Y Y', (gd S M).CompI Y → (gd S M).CompI Y' → x ∈ Y → x ∈ Y' → Y ⊆ (gd S M).Good → Y' ⊆ (gd S M).Good) ∧
      (∃ Y, (gd S M).CompI Y ∧ x ∈ Y) ∧
      ((∃ I ∈ (ld M L).Good, I ∈ (ld M L).TI ∧ ((ld M L).D1 : WithBot ℕ∞) ≤ qdim I.asIdeal) →
        ∃ Y, (gd S M).CompI Y ∧ Y ⊆ (gd S M).Good) := by
  obtain ⟨hc, ht⟩ := sharp2 hF S x.asIdeal L hB hx
  letI : Algebra S.A L.R := (L.ι : S.A →+* L.R).toAlgebra
  haveI : Module.Flat S.A L.R := (loc_flat hF (Or.inl rfl) S x.asIdeal L).1
  have ch := chartOfFlat (PrimeSpectrum S.A) S.A id Topology.IsOpenEmbedding.id L.R
  have hpt : id (PrimeSpectrum.comap (algebraMap S.A L.R) (closedPoint L.R)) = x := PrimeSpectrum.ext L.lies
  rw [hpt] at ch
  have hr := (loc_rho hF (Or.inl rfl) S x.asIdeal L).2.2.1
  obtain ⟨a, b, c⟩ := globOfCharts _ (gd S M) (ti_open S M) (good_closed S M) x L.R _ ch (ld M L) (by
    ext p
    exact and_congr (and_congr (hr p.asIdeal).1.not (hr p.asIdeal).2.1.not)
      (imp_congr Iff.rfl (hr p.asIdeal).2.2.not)) rfl (locProp _ _ (ld_ok hF hB hM h1 hx L))
  refine ⟨a fun P hP => le_trans ?_ (hc.1 P hP), b fun P hP => ht _ hP, c⟩
  exact Nat.cast_le.2 (Nat.le_succ _)

end

theorem UpperTheory.V.Pr.components_good_rank_two :
    UpperTheory.V.Pr.PropagationTheoremGL2 := by
  intro l _ O _ _ _ _ F C₀ hF hB S M hM h1 hS
  haveI : IsNoetherianRing S.A := (gr_A hF (Or.inl rfl) S).2.2.2.2.2.1
  have hok : (gd S M).Ok := by
    refine ⟨fun p hp => hp.1, preconnected_rank_two hF S, ?_, ?_, ?_, ?_, ?_, ?_⟩
    · rintro C hC ⟨p, hp, hp'⟩
      obtain ⟨x, ⟨⟨a, -⟩, b⟩, c⟩ := jac hF (Or.inl rfl) S C hC Set.univ isOpen_univ ⟨p, ⟨hp, trivial⟩, hp'⟩
      exact ⟨x, a, b, c⟩
    · intro p hp
      rw [mem_closure_iff]
      intro U hU hpU
      obtain ⟨x, ⟨⟨-, a⟩, b⟩, c⟩ := jac hF (Or.inl rfl) S Set.univ isClosed_univ U hU ⟨p, ⟨trivial, hpU⟩, hp⟩
      exact ⟨x, a, ⟨b, c⟩, Set.notMem_empty _⟩
    · rintro Y ⟨hY, p, hp, hpI⟩
      obtain ⟨η, hη, hi⟩ := comp_min hY
      obtain ⟨x, -, b, c⟩ := l41 hF (Or.inl rfl) S η hη.1.1 (min_hom hF (Or.inl rfl) S η hη)
        fun h => hpI.1.1 (h.trans ((hi p).1 hp))
      exact ⟨x, (hi x).2 c, b⟩
    · intro x hx
      obtain ⟨L⟩ := loc_ex hF (Or.inl rfl) S x.asIdeal hx.2 hx.1.1
      exact (at_pt hF hB hM h1 hx.1.2 L).1
    · rintro x ⟨hx, -⟩
      obtain ⟨L⟩ := loc_ex hF (Or.inl rfl) S x.asIdeal hx.2 hx.1.1
      exact (at_pt hF hB hM h1 hx.1.2 L).2.1
    · obtain ⟨ξ, Z, hξ, hI, hZ, hMZ, hZξ, hd⟩ := hS
      obtain ⟨x, hm, hx, H⟩ := seedpt hF (Or.inl rfl) S Z ξ hZ hξ hI hZξ
      obtain ⟨L⟩ := loc_ex hF (Or.inl rfl) S x.asIdeal hm hx.1
      obtain ⟨P, hP, hPI, hπ, hdim⟩ := H L
      refine (at_pt hF hB hM h1 hx.2 L).2.2 ⟨⟨P, hP⟩, hMZ.trans hπ.ge, hPI, ?_⟩
      show ((2 * F.d + 1 : ℕ) : WithBot ℕ∞) ≤ ringKrullDim (L.R ⧸ P)
      rw [hdim, show ringKrullDim (S.B ⧸ Z) = _ from hd]
      exact_mod_cast (by omega : 2 * F.d + 1 ≤ 1 + 2 * F.d + 1)
  intro p hp hI
  exact globProp _ (gd S M) hok (a := ⟨p, hp⟩) hI


[ACP_SRC_GL3] Actual rank-three application and final intersection of the two finite ideals
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/CurveDVRSpecialization/PropagationTheoremGL3.lean#L1-L229

open IsLocalRing UpperTheory.V.Alg UpperTheory.V.Fr

namespace UpperTheory.V.Pr

set_option linter.overlappingInstances false

noncomputable section

variable {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [IsDiscreteValuationRing O] [IsCoef l O]
  {F : Frame l (ResidueField O)} {C₀ : ℕ}

private def gd (S : Space O F 3) (M : Ideal S.B) : GlobData (PrimeSpectrum S.A) where
  TI := {p | S.u.TI p.asIdeal}
  Good := {p | M ≤ S.π p.asIdeal}
  Core := S.BB
  Cl := {p | p ∈ S.BB ∧ p.asIdeal.IsMaximal}
  EE := {p | S.u.ee ≤ p.asIdeal}

private def ld {S : Space O F 3} (M : Ideal S.B) {x : Ideal S.A} (L : S.Loc x) (I : Ideal L.R) : LocData L.R where
  D1 := 3 * F.d + 1
  TI := {p | L.ρ.TI p.asIdeal}
  TE := {p | L.ρ.TE p.asIdeal}
  Good := {p | M ≤ L.π p.asIdeal}
  Nice := {p | Nice L.ρ p.asIdeal}
  Thick := {p | Thick L.ρ p.asIdeal}
  sharp := I

private theorem closed_le {A : Type} [CommRing A] (I : Ideal A) : IsClosed {p : PrimeSpectrum A | I ≤ p.asIdeal} :=
  PrimeSpectrum.isClosed_zeroLocus (I : Set A)

private theorem comp_eq {A : Type} [CommSemiring A] {Y : Set (PrimeSpectrum A)}
    (hY : Y ∈ irreducibleComponents (PrimeSpectrum A)) : ∃ η ∈ minimalPrimes A, Y = {p | η ≤ p.asIdeal} := by
  rw [← PrimeSpectrum.zeroLocus_minimalPrimes] at hY
  obtain ⟨η, hη, rfl⟩ := hY
  exact ⟨η, hη, rfl⟩

private theorem ti_open (S : Space O F 3) (M : Ideal S.B) : IsOpen (gd S M).TI := by
  have : (gd S M).TI = ({p : PrimeSpectrum S.A | S.u.triP ≤ p.asIdeal}ᶜ ∩ {p | S.u.triM ≤ p.asIdeal}ᶜ) ∩
      {p | S.u.ee ≤ p.asIdeal}ᶜ := by
    ext p
    exact ⟨fun h => ⟨h.1, h.2 rfl⟩, fun h => ⟨h.1, fun _ => h.2⟩⟩
  rw [this]
  exact ((closed_le _).isOpen_compl.inter (closed_le _).isOpen_compl).inter (closed_le _).isOpen_compl

private theorem good_closed (S : Space O F 3) (M : Ideal S.B) : IsClosed (gd S M).Good := by
  have : (gd S M).Good = {p : PrimeSpectrum S.A | M.map (S.inc : S.B →+* S.A) ≤ p.asIdeal} := by
    ext p
    exact Ideal.map_le_iff_le_comap.symm
  rw [this]
  exact closed_le _

private theorem π_mono {S : Space O F 3} {x : Ideal S.A} (L : S.Loc x) {P Q : Ideal L.R} (h : P ≤ Q) :
    L.π P ≤ L.π Q := Ideal.comap_mono (Ideal.comap_mono h)

private theorem comp_of_ti {S : Space O F 3} {x : Ideal S.A} {L : S.Loc x} {I : Ideal L.R} (hI : L.Sharp I)
    {P : Ideal L.R} (hP : L.ρ.TI P) (hm : P ∈ minimalPrimes L.R) : P ∈ I.minimalPrimes :=
  ⟨⟨hm.1.1, hI.off P hm.1.1 (hP.2 rfl)⟩, fun _ hQ h => hm.2 ⟨hQ.1, bot_le⟩ h⟩

private theorem ld_ok (hF : F.Ok C₀) (hB : F.Big C₀) {S : Space O F 3} {M : Ideal S.B} (hM : S.FinLam M)
    (h1 : P1' S M) (h23 : P23' S M) {I₃ : Ideal S.B} (hI₃ : S.FinLam I₃)
    (hE : ∀ ξ : Ideal S.A, ξ.IsPrime → S.u.TE ξ → I₃ ≤ S.π ξ) {x : Ideal S.A} (hx : ¬ S.u.triP ≤ x) (L : S.Loc x)
    {I : Ideal L.R} (hI : L.Sharp I) : (ld M L I).Ok where
  good_up _ _ h hP := le_trans hP (π_mono L h)
  ti_down _ _ h hQ := ⟨⟨fun h' => hQ.1.1 (h'.trans h), fun h' => hQ.1.2 (h'.trans h)⟩,
    fun e h' => hQ.2 e (h'.trans h)⟩
  nice_type p hp := by
    by_cases h : L.ρ.ee ≤ p.asIdeal
    · exact Or.inr ⟨hp.off, rfl, h⟩
    · exact Or.inl ⟨hp.off, fun _ => h⟩
  nice_good J hJ hd := by
    obtain ⟨p, hp, hle⟩ := exists_nice_prime_above_of_large_dim l O F C₀ 3 hF hB (Or.inr rfl) S x L hx M hM J.asIdeal hJ hd
    exact ⟨⟨p, hp.prime⟩, hp, hle⟩
  nice_thick e he _ J hJ hd := by
    obtain ⟨a, b, c⟩ := (loc_rho hF (Or.inr rfl) S x L).2.2.1 e.asIdeal
    have he' : L.ρ.TE e.asIdeal := he.1
    have h3 : I₃ ≤ L.π e.asIdeal := hE (L.im e.asIdeal) (Ideal.IsPrime.comap _)
      ⟨⟨fun h => he'.1.1 (a.2 h), fun h => he'.1.2 (b.2 h)⟩, rfl, c.1 he'.2.2⟩
    obtain ⟨p, hp, hle⟩ := exists_nice_prime_above_of_large_dim l O F C₀ 3 hF hB (Or.inr rfl) S x L hx I₃ hI₃ J.asIdeal
      (h3.trans (π_mono L hJ)) hd
    exact ⟨⟨p, hp.prime⟩, hp, ⟨hp.off, rfl, he'.2.2.trans (le_trans hJ hle)⟩, hle⟩
  comp P hP hm := comp_of_ti hI hP hm
  chain P Q hP hQ := by
    have key : ∀ q : Ideal L.R, Relation.ReflTransGen
        (fun P Q : Ideal L.R => P ∈ I.minimalPrimes ∧ Q ∈ I.minimalPrimes ∧
          ((3 * F.d + 1 : ℕ) : WithBot ℕ∞) ≤ ringKrullDim (L.R ⧸ (P ⊔ Q))) P.asIdeal q →
        ∀ hq : q.IsPrime, Relation.ReflTransGen (ld M L I).Adj P ⟨q, hq⟩ := by
      intro q h
      induction h with
      | refl => exact fun _ => Relation.ReflTransGen.refl
      | tail _ hab ih => exact fun _ => (ih hab.1.1.1).tail hab
    exact key _ (hI.chained.2 _ hP _ hQ) Q.isPrime
  dich P hP := hI.dich _ hP
  p1 p hp hT hG P hle := h1 x L p.asIdeal hp hT hG P.asIdeal P.isPrime hle
  p23 p hp hT q hpq hex P' hle hT' := by
    obtain ⟨P, a, b, c⟩ := hex
    refine h23 x L p.asIdeal q.asIdeal hp hT ?_ ⟨P.asIdeal, P.isPrime, a, b, c⟩ P'.asIdeal P'.isPrime hle hT'
    refine Relation.ReflTransGen.lift PrimeSpectrum.asIdeal ?_ _ _ hpq
    rintro a b ⟨a1, a2, a3, a4, e, e1, -, e2, e3⟩
    exact ⟨a1, a2, a3, a4, e.asIdeal, e.isPrime, e1, e2, e3⟩

private theorem chart_ex (hF : F.Ok C₀) {S : Space O F 3} (x : PrimeSpectrum S.A) (L : S.Loc x.asIdeal) :
    Chart x (PrimeSpectrum.comap (L.ι : S.A →+* L.R)) := by
  letI : Algebra S.A L.R := (L.ι : S.A →+* L.R).toAlgebra
  haveI : Module.Flat S.A L.R := (loc_flat hF (Or.inr rfl) S _ L).1
  have h := chartOfFlat (PrimeSpectrum S.A) S.A id Topology.IsOpenEmbedding.id L.R
  have hx : PrimeSpectrum.comap (algebraMap S.A L.R) (closedPoint L.R) = x := PrimeSpectrum.ext L.lies
  rw [hx] at h
  exact h

private theorem ld_ti (hF : F.Ok C₀) {S : Space O F 3} (M : Ideal S.B) {x : Ideal S.A} (L : S.Loc x) (I : Ideal L.R) :
    (ld M L I).TI = PrimeSpectrum.comap (L.ι : S.A →+* L.R) ⁻¹' (gd S M).TI := by
  ext P
  obtain ⟨a, b, c⟩ := (loc_rho hF (Or.inr rfl) S x L).2.2.1 P.asIdeal
  exact ⟨fun h => ⟨⟨fun h' => h.1.1 (a.2 h'), fun h' => h.1.2 (b.2 h')⟩, fun e h' => h.2 e (c.2 h')⟩,
    fun h => ⟨⟨fun h' => h.1.1 (a.1 h'), fun h' => h.1.2 (b.1 h')⟩, fun e h' => h.2 e (c.1 h')⟩⟩

private theorem gd_meets (hF : F.Ok C₀) (S : Space O F 3) (M : Ideal S.B) (Y : Set (PrimeSpectrum S.A))
    (hY : (gd S M).CompI Y) : (Y ∩ S.BB).Nonempty := by
  obtain ⟨hY, y, hy, hT⟩ := hY
  obtain ⟨η, hη, hYη⟩ := comp_eq hY
  have hT' : ¬ S.u.triP ≤ y.asIdeal := hT.1.1
  rw [hYη] at hy ⊢
  have hy' : η ≤ y.asIdeal := hy
  have hp : η.IsPrime := hη.1.1
  have hh : S.IsHom η := min_hom hF (Or.inr rfl) S η hη
  obtain ⟨x, -, hx, hle⟩ := l41 hF (Or.inr rfl) S η hp hh fun h => hT' (le_trans h hy')
  exact ⟨x, hle, hx⟩

private theorem gd_ok (hF : F.Ok C₀) (hB : F.Big C₀) {S : Space O F 3} {M : Ideal S.B} (hM : S.FinLam M)
    (h1 : P1' S M) (h23 : P23' S M) (hs : SeedIn S M) {I₃ : Ideal S.B} (hI₃ : S.FinLam I₃)
    (hE : ∀ ξ : Ideal S.A, ξ.IsPrime → S.u.TE ξ → I₃ ≤ S.π ξ) : (gd S M).Ok := by
  have h3 : (3 : ℕ) = 2 ∨ (3 : ℕ) = 3 := Or.inr rfl
  have hloc : ∀ x ∈ (gd S M).Cl, ∃ (L : S.Loc x.asIdeal) (I : Ideal L.R), L.Sharp I ∧ (ld M L I).Concl := by
    intro x hx
    obtain ⟨L⟩ := loc_ex hF h3 S x.asIdeal hx.2 hx.1.1
    obtain ⟨I, hI⟩ := sharp3 hF S _ L hB hx.1.2
    haveI : IsNoetherianRing L.R := L.co.toIsNoetherian
    exact ⟨L, I, hI, locProp _ _ (ld_ok hF hB hM h1 h23 hI₃ hE hx.1.2 L hI)⟩
  obtain ⟨-, hconn, hdense⟩ := preconnected_rank_three hF S
  refine ⟨fun _ h => h.1, hconn, fun C hC hne => ?_, ?_, fun Y hY => ?_, fun x hx Y Y' hY hY' hxY hxY' hG => ?_,
    fun x hx => ?_, ?_⟩
  · obtain ⟨x, hx, hm⟩ := jac hF h3 S C hC Set.univ isOpen_univ (by rwa [Set.inter_univ])
    exact ⟨x, hx.1.1, hx.2, hm⟩
  · refine hdense.trans (closure_minimal ?_ isClosed_closure)
    intro p hp
    rw [mem_closure_iff]
    intro U hU hpU
    obtain ⟨x, hx, hm⟩ := jac hF h3 S Set.univ isClosed_univ (U ∩ {p | S.u.ee ≤ p.asIdeal}ᶜ)
      (hU.inter (closed_le _).isOpen_compl) ⟨p, ⟨trivial, hpU, hp.2⟩, hp.1⟩
    exact ⟨x, hx.1.2.1, ⟨hx.2, hm⟩, hx.1.2.2⟩
  · exact gd_meets hF S M Y hY
  · obtain ⟨L, I, hI, hc⟩ := hloc x hx
    have hch := chart_ex hF x L
    have hgen : ∀ Z, (gd S M).CompI Z → x ∈ Z → ∃ P : PrimeSpectrum L.R, P.asIdeal ∈ minimalPrimes L.R ∧
        P ∈ (ld M L I).TI ∧ closure {PrimeSpectrum.comap (L.ι : S.A →+* L.R) P} = Z := by
      intro Z hZ hxZ
      obtain ⟨P, hP, rfl⟩ := hch.onto Z hZ.1 hxZ
      refine ⟨P, hP, ?_, rfl⟩
      rw [ld_ti hF]
      obtain ⟨y, hy, hT⟩ := hZ.2
      obtain ⟨z, hz, rfl⟩ := mem_closure_iff.1 hy _ (ti_open S M) hT
      exact hz
    obtain ⟨P, hP, hT, rfl⟩ := hgen Y hY hxY
    obtain ⟨P', hP', hT', rfl⟩ := hgen Y' hY' hxY'
    refine closure_minimal (Set.singleton_subset_iff.2 ?_) (good_closed S M)
    refine hc P (hG (subset_closure rfl)) hT (le_trans ?_ (hI.chained.1 _ (comp_of_ti hI hT hP))) P' hT' hP'
    exact Nat.cast_le.2 (Nat.le_succ _)
  · obtain ⟨L, I, hI, hc⟩ := hloc x hx.1
    haveI : IsNoetherianRing L.R := L.co.toIsNoetherian
    refine (globOfCharts _ (gd S M) (ti_open S M) (good_closed S M) x L.R _ (chart_ex hF x L) (ld M L I)
      (ld_ti hF M L I) rfl hc).2.1 fun P hP => ?_
    have hI0 : I = ⊥ := hI.triv hx.2
    refine (hI.dich P.asIdeal (hI0 ▸ hP)).resolve_right fun h => hx.2 ?_
    exact (((loc_rho hF h3 S _ L).2.2.1 P.asIdeal).2.2.1 h.1.2.2).trans
      ((Ideal.comap_mono (le_maximalIdeal P.isPrime.ne_top)).trans L.lies.le)
  · obtain ⟨ξ, Z, hξ, hT, hZ, hMZ, hle, hd⟩ := hs
    obtain ⟨x, hm, hx, h⟩ := seedpt hF h3 S Z ξ hZ hξ hT hle
    obtain ⟨L, I, hI, hc⟩ := hloc x ⟨hx, hm⟩
    haveI : IsNoetherianRing L.R := L.co.toIsNoetherian
    obtain ⟨P, hP, hPT, hπ, hdim⟩ := h L
    refine (globOfCharts _ (gd S M) (ti_open S M) (good_closed S M) x L.R _ (chart_ex hF x L) (ld M L I)
      (ld_ti hF M L I) rfl hc).2.2 ⟨⟨P, hP⟩, hMZ.trans hπ.ge, hPT, ?_⟩
    show ((3 * F.d + 1 : ℕ) : WithBot ℕ∞) ≤ ringKrullDim (L.R ⧸ P)
    rw [hdim, show ringKrullDim (S.B ⧸ Z) = _ from hd]
    have : 3 * F.d + 1 ≤ 1 + 3 * F.d + 1 := by omega
    exact_mod_cast this

end

end UpperTheory.V.Pr

theorem UpperTheory.V.Pr.components_good_rank_three :
    UpperTheory.V.Pr.PropagationTheoremGL3 := by
  intro l _ O _ _ _ _ F C₀ hF hB S₂ M₂ hM₂ hG₂ S M hM h1 h23 hs
  obtain ⟨I₃, hI₃, hE⟩ := l53 hF S₂ S M₂ hM₂ hG₂
  haveI : IsNoetherianRing S.A := (gr_A hF (Or.inr rfl) S).2.2.2.2.2.1
  have hG : AllGood S M := fun p hp hT =>
    globProp _ (gd S M) (gd_ok hF hB hM h1 h23 hs hI₃ hE) (a := ⟨p, hp⟩) hT
  refine ⟨hG, M ⊓ I₃, ?_, fun p hp ho => ?_⟩
  · unfold Space.FinLam
    rw [Ideal.radical_eq_sInf]
    refine le_sInf fun J hJ => ?_
    rcases hJ.2.inf_le.1 (le_sup_left.trans hJ.1) with h | h
    · exact hM.trans (hJ.2.radical_le_iff.2 (sup_le h (le_sup_right.trans hJ.1)))
    · exact hI₃.trans (hJ.2.radical_le_iff.2 (sup_le h (le_sup_right.trans hJ.1)))
  · rcases Classical.em (S.u.ee ≤ p) with h | h
    · exact inf_le_right.trans (hE p hp ⟨ho, rfl, h⟩)
    · exact inf_le_left.trans (hG p hp ⟨ho, fun _ => h⟩)


[ACP_SRC_NICE_APP] Exact next consumer of NicePrimeEx with triangular/coincident-locus bounds
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/CurveDVRSpecialization/ExistsNicePrimeAboveOfLargeDim.lean#L1-L69
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.CurveDVRSpecialization.PropagationTheoremGL2
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.CurveDVRSpecialization.NicePrimeEx
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.FrobeniusSmoothVectors.CharpolyCoeffsInSubalgebra
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.FrobeniusSmoothVectors.KrullDimLtOfNotLeTriangular
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.FrobeniusSmoothVectors.LocRho

theorem UpperTheory.V.Pr.exists_nice_prime_above_of_large_dim :
    ∀ (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (O : Type) [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [IsDiscreteValuationRing O] [UpperTheory.V.Fr.IsCoef l O] (F : UpperTheory.V.Fr.Frame l (IsLocalRing.ResidueField O)) (C₀ n : ℕ), F.Ok C₀ → F.Big C₀ → (n = 2 ∨ n = 3) → ∀ (S : UpperTheory.V.Fr.Space O F n) (x : Ideal S.A) (L : S.Loc x), ¬ S.u.triP ≤ x → ∀ I : Ideal S.B, S.FinLam I → ∀ P : Ideal L.R, I ≤ L.π P → ((n * F.d + 1 : ℕ) : WithBot ℕ∞) ≤ UpperTheory.V.Pr.qdim P → ∃ p, UpperTheory.V.Pr.Nice L.ρ p ∧ P ≤ p := by
  intro l _ O _ _ _ _ F C₀ n hOk hBig hn S x L hx I hI P hIP hdim
  haveI := L.co
  haveI : IsLocalHom (algebraMap L.O' L.R) := L.co.isLocalHom
  haveI : CharP (IsLocalRing.ResidueField O) l := UpperTheory.V.Fr.IsCoef.char (O := O)
  obtain ⟨_, hP⟩ := UpperTheory.V.Fr.l51 hOk hn S x L hx
  obtain ⟨hM, hC⟩ := UpperTheory.V.Fr.krull_dim_lt_of_not_le_triangular hOk hn S x L hBig hx
  obtain ⟨w, hw⟩ := IsDiscreteValuationRing.exists_irreducible O
  have hmax : IsLocalRing.maximalIdeal O = Ideal.span {w} := hw.maximalIdeal_eq
  have hwO' : algebraMap O L.O' w ∈ IsLocalRing.maximalIdeal L.O' := by
    rw [← L.unr]
    exact Ideal.mem_map_of_mem _ (hmax ▸ Ideal.subset_span rfl)
  have hwR : algebraMap O L.R w ∈ IsLocalRing.maximalIdeal L.R := by
    rw [IsScalarTower.algebraMap_apply O L.O' L.R]
    exact map_nonunit (algebraMap L.O' L.R) _ hwO'
  let a : Option (F.V × Fin n × Fin n) → Ideal L.R := fun o =>
    match o with
    | none => L.ρ.triM
    | some (v, i, j) =>
      if i = j then ⊤ else
        Ideal.map L.b I ⊔ Ideal.map (algebraMap O L.R) (IsLocalRing.maximalIdeal O) ⊔
          Ideal.span {y | ∃ g ∈ F.Nv v, y = ↑((L.ρ.ψ v i) g) - ↑((L.ρ.ψ v j) g)}
  have hN : 1 ≤ n * F.d := by
    have h1 : 1 ≤ n := by rcases hn with h | h <;> omega
    have h2 : 1 ≤ F.d := by have := hBig.1; omega
    exact Nat.one_le_iff_ne_zero.mpr (Nat.mul_ne_zero (by omega) (by omega))
  have ha : ∀ o, UpperTheory.V.Pr.qdim (a o) < ↑(n * F.d) := by
    intro o
    rcases o with _ | ⟨v, i, j⟩
    · exact hM
    · by_cases hij : i = j
      · simp only [a, hij, if_true]
        show ringKrullDim (L.R ⧸ (⊤ : Ideal L.R)) < _
        rw [ringKrullDim_eq_bot_of_subsingleton]
        exact WithBot.bot_lt_coe _
      · simp only [a, hij, if_false]
        exact hC I hI v i j hij
  obtain ⟨p, hp, hPp, hp1, hwp, hnot⟩ :=
    UpperTheory.V.Pr.nicePrimeEx L.R (algebraMap O L.R w) hwR (n * F.d) hN _ a ha P hdim
  have hmap : Ideal.map (algebraMap O L.R) (IsLocalRing.maximalIdeal O) ≤ p := by
    rw [hmax, Ideal.map_span, Set.image_singleton, Ideal.span_le, Set.singleton_subset_iff]
    exact hwp
  refine ⟨p, ⟨hp, hp1, ?_, ⟨?_, ?_⟩, ?_⟩, hPp⟩
  · have hl : ((l : ℕ) : O) ∈ IsLocalRing.maximalIdeal O := by
      rw [← IsLocalRing.residue_eq_zero_iff, map_natCast]
      exact CharP.cast_eq_zero _ l
    have := hmap (Ideal.mem_map_of_mem (algebraMap O L.R) hl)
    simpa using this
  · intro h
    rw [hP, top_le_iff] at h
    exact hp.ne_top h
  · exact hnot none
  · intro v i j hij hco
    apply hnot (some (v, i, j))
    simp only [a, hij, if_false]
    refine sup_le (sup_le ?_ hmap) ?_
    · rw [Ideal.map_le_iff_le_comap]
      intro y hy
      exact hPp (hIP hy)
    · rw [Ideal.span_le]
      rintro y ⟨g, hg, rfl⟩
      exact hco g hg


[ACP_SRC_LOCFLAT] Retained arithmetic deformation-chart flatness supplier
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/LocFlat.lean#L1-L69
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.FrobeniusSmoothVectors.Integral
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.FrobeniusSmoothVectors.IsInternalCongr
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.FrobeniusSmoothVectors.CoB
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.FrobeniusSmoothVectors.LocCpl
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.CommutativeAlgebraHeight.HighestWeightSpaceFinite

open IsLocalRing Matrix UpperTheory.V.Alg UpperTheory.V.U3 UpperTheory.V.Fr

private theorem max_of_fin {O D R : Type} [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] [Finite (ResidueField O)] [CommRing D]
    [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] [IsCO O R] (f : D →+* R) : ((maximalIdeal R).comap f).IsMaximal := by
  have hk : (maximalIdeal R).comap f = RingHom.ker ((res O R).toRingHom.comp f) := by
    ext a
    rw [Ideal.mem_comap, RingHom.mem_ker, ← res_eq_zero_iff O R]
    rfl
  have hp : ((maximalIdeal R).comap f).IsPrime := Ideal.IsPrime.comap _
  rw [hk] at hp ⊢
  haveI : Finite (D ⧸ RingHom.ker ((res O R).toRingHom.comp f)) :=
    Finite.of_injective _ (RingHom.kerLift_injective _)
  exact Ideal.Quotient.maximal_of_isField _ (Finite.isField_of_domain _)

private theorem flat_dvr {O O' : Type} [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [IsDiscreteValuationRing O] [CommRing O']
    [IsDomain O'] [IsDiscreteValuationRing O'] [Algebra O O']
    (h : (maximalIdeal O).map (algebraMap O O') = maximalIdeal O') : Module.Flat O O' := by
  have hinj : Function.Injective (algebraMap O O') := by
    rw [RingHom.injective_iff_ker_eq_bot]
    by_contra hne
    have hp : (RingHom.ker (algebraMap O O')).IsPrime := RingHom.ker_isPrime _
    have hm := eq_maximalIdeal (hp.isMaximal hne)
    refine IsDiscreteValuationRing.not_a_field O' ?_
    rw [← h, ← hm, Ideal.map_eq_bot_iff_le_ker]
  rw [Module.Flat.flat_iff_torsion_eq_bot_of_isBezout, eq_bot_iff]
  rintro z ⟨⟨a, ha⟩, hz⟩
  have h1 : algebraMap O O' a * z = 0 := by rw [← Algebra.smul_def]; exact hz
  refine (mul_eq_zero.1 h1).resolve_left fun h2 => nonZeroDivisors.ne_zero ha (hinj ?_)
  rw [h2, map_zero]

theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.loc_flat :
    ∀ {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [IsDiscreteValuationRing O] [IsCoef l O] {F : Frame l (ResidueField O)} {C₀ : ℕ} {n : ℕ}, F.Ok C₀ → (n = 2 ∨ n = 3) → ∀ S : Space O F n, ∀ (x : Ideal S.A) (L : S.Loc x), (L.ι : S.A →+* L.R).Flat ∧ x.IsMaximal ∧ S.mB ≤ S.π x := by
  intro l _ O _ _ _ _ F C₀ n hF hn S x L
  refine ⟨?_, ?_, ?_⟩
  · have hA : IsNoetherianRing S.A := (gr_A hF hn S).2.2.2.2.2.1
    have hD : IsNoetherianRing (TensorProduct O L.O' S.A) := by
      have h1 : IsNoetherianRing (TensorProduct O S.A L.O') :=
        Algebra.FiniteType.isNoetherianRing S.A _
      exact isNoetherianRing_of_ringEquiv _ (Algebra.TensorProduct.comm O S.A L.O').toRingEquiv
    let ι' : TensorProduct O L.O' S.A →ₐ[L.O'] L.R :=
      Algebra.TensorProduct.lift (Algebra.ofId _ _) L.ι fun _ _ => Commute.all _ _
    have hι : ∀ a, ι' (1 ⊗ₜ a) = L.ι a := fun a => by simp [ι']
    have hc := loc_cpl hF hn S x L ι' hι
    haveI := max_of_fin (O := L.O') ι'.toRingHom
    have h1 : ι'.toRingHom.Flat := (@UpperTheory.V.CA.c3a _ _ _ _ _ ι'.toRingHom.toAlgebra _ _ _ hc).1
    haveI : Module.Flat O L.O' := flat_dvr L.unr
    have h2 : (Algebra.TensorProduct.includeRight (R := O) (A := L.O') (B := S.A)).toRingHom.Flat := by
      have h3 : (algebraMap S.A (TensorProduct O S.A L.O')).Flat := RingHom.flat_algebraMap_iff.2 inferInstance
      have h4 := h3.comp (RingHom.Flat.of_bijective (f := (Algebra.TensorProduct.comm O S.A L.O').toRingEquiv.toRingHom)
        (Algebra.TensorProduct.comm O S.A L.O').bijective)
      exact h4
    have e : (L.ι : S.A →+* L.R) = ι'.toRingHom.comp
        (Algebra.TensorProduct.includeRight (R := O) (A := L.O') (B := S.A)).toRingHom :=
      RingHom.ext fun a => (hι a).symm
    rw [e]
    exact h2.comp h1
  · rw [← L.lies]
    exact max_of_fin (O := L.O') _
  · obtain ⟨h, hm⟩ := co_B hF hn S
    have h1 := max_of_fin (O := L.O') ((L.ι : S.A →+* L.R).comp (S.inc : S.B →+* S.A))
    rw [← Ideal.comap_comap, L.lies] at h1
    rw [← hm]
    exact (eq_maximalIdeal h1).ge


[ACP_SRC_LOCRHO] Retained arithmetic local chart and representation dictionary
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/LocRho.lean#L1-L66
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.FrobeniusSmoothVectors.Integral
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.FrobeniusSmoothVectors.MemPowSmulLe
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.FrobeniusSmoothVectors.IrrOfConn
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.FrobeniusSmoothVectors.LocFlat

open IsLocalRing Matrix UpperTheory.V.Alg UpperTheory.V.U3 UpperTheory.V.Fr

private theorem cont_map {Γ R R' : Type} [TopologicalSpace Γ] [CommRing R] [CommRing R'] (f : R →+* R')
    {I : Ideal R} {J : Ideal R'} (h : I ≤ J.comap f) {a : Γ → R} (ha : AdicCont I a) :
    AdicCont J fun g => f (a g) := fun N =>
  (ha N).comp (Ideal.quotientMap _ f (le_trans (Ideal.pow_right_mono h N) (Ideal.le_comap_pow _ N)))

private theorem span_le_iff {G R R' : Type} {n : ℕ} [CommRing R] [CommRing R'] (f : R →+* R')
    (r : G → Fin n → Fin n → R) (p : Fin n → Fin n → Prop) (P : Ideal R') :
    Ideal.span {x | ∃ g i j, p i j ∧ x = f (r g i j)} ≤ P ↔
      Ideal.span {x | ∃ g i j, p i j ∧ x = r g i j} ≤ P.comap f := by
  rw [Ideal.span_le, Ideal.span_le]
  constructor
  · rintro h _ ⟨g, i, j, hp, rfl⟩
    exact h ⟨g, i, j, hp, rfl⟩
  · rintro h _ ⟨g, i, j, hp, rfl⟩
    exact h ⟨g, i, j, hp, rfl⟩

private theorem res_b {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O]
    {F : Frame l (ResidueField O)} {n : ℕ} (S : Space O F n) (x : Ideal S.A) (L : S.Loc x) (hx : S.mB ≤ S.π x)
    (f : ResidueField O →+* ResidueField L.R) (hf : IsResMap O L.R f) (b : S.B) :
    residue L.R (L.ι (S.inc b)) = f (res O S.R b) := by
  obtain ⟨c, hc⟩ := residue_surjective (res O S.R (b : S.R))
  have h1 : b - algebraMap O S.B c ∈ S.mB := by
    show S.B.val (b - algebraMap O S.B c) ∈ maximalIdeal S.R
    rw [← res_eq_zero_iff O, map_sub, map_sub, AlgHom.commutes, AlgHom.commutes, sub_eq_zero]
    exact hc.symm
  have h2 : S.inc (b - algebraMap O S.B c) ∈ x := hx h1
  rw [← L.lies] at h2
  have h3 : L.ι (S.inc (b - algebraMap O S.B c)) ∈ maximalIdeal L.R := h2
  rw [map_sub, map_sub, AlgHom.commutes, AlgHom.commutes] at h3
  rw [← hc, hf c]
  exact Ideal.Quotient.eq.2 h3

theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.loc_rho :
    ∀ {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [IsDiscreteValuationRing O] [IsCoef l O] {F : Frame l (ResidueField O)} {C₀ : ℕ} {n : ℕ}, F.Ok C₀ → (n = 2 ∨ n = 3) → ∀ S : Space O F n, ∀ (x : Ideal S.A) (L : S.Loc x), L.ρ.Cont (maximalIdeal L.R) ∧ (∀ f, IsResMap O L.R f → (L.ρ.map (residue L.R)).IsBar f) ∧ (∀ P : Ideal L.R, (L.ρ.triP ≤ P ↔ S.u.triP ≤ L.im P) ∧ (L.ρ.triM ≤ P ↔ S.u.triM ≤ L.im P) ∧ (L.ρ.ee ≤ P ↔ S.u.ee ≤ L.im P)) ∧ (¬ S.u.triP ≤ x → L.ρ.triP = ⊤ ∧ L.ρ.triM ≤ maximalIdeal L.R) := by
  intro l _ O _ _ _ _ F C₀ n hF hn S x L
  obtain ⟨-, -, hx⟩ := UpperTheory.V.Fr.loc_flat hF hn S x L
  obtain ⟨⟨hc1, hc2⟩, -⟩ := UpperTheory.V.Fr.cont_u hF hn S
  have hx0 : S.x0 ≤ (maximalIdeal L.R).comap (L.ι : S.A →+* L.R) := by
    rw [L.lies]
    exact Ideal.map_le_iff_le_comap.2 hx
  have h3 : ∀ P : Ideal L.R, (L.ρ.triP ≤ P ↔ S.u.triP ≤ L.im P) ∧ (L.ρ.triM ≤ P ↔ S.u.triM ≤ L.im P) ∧
      (L.ρ.ee ≤ P ↔ S.u.ee ≤ L.im P) := fun P =>
    ⟨span_le_iff (L.ι : S.A →+* L.R) (fun g i j => S.u.r g i j) (fun i j => j < i) P,
      span_le_iff (L.ι : S.A →+* L.R) (fun g i j => S.u.r g i j) (fun i j => i < j) P,
      span_le_iff (L.ι : S.A →+* L.R) (fun g i j => S.u.r g i j) (fun i j => Odd (w n i - w n j)) P⟩
  have him : L.im (maximalIdeal L.R) = x := L.lies
  refine ⟨⟨fun i j => cont_map _ hx0 (hc1 i j), fun v i => cont_map _ hx0 (hc2 v i)⟩,
    fun f hf => ⟨fun g i => ?_, fun v i g => ?_⟩, h3, fun hP => ⟨?_, ?_⟩⟩
  · exact (res_b S x L hx f hf (S.aB g i)).trans (congrArg f (S.lift.2.1.1 g i))
  · exact (res_b S x L hx f hf (S.ψB v i g)).trans (congrArg f (S.lift.2.1.2 v i g))
  · by_contra hne
    exact hP (him ▸ (h3 _).1.1 (IsLocalRing.le_maximalIdeal hne))
  · have hk : RingHom.ker ((residue L.R).comp (L.ι : S.A →+* L.R)) = x := by
      rw [← RingHom.comap_ker, IsLocalRing.ker_residue]
      exact L.lies
    have := (UpperTheory.V.Fr.types hF hn S (ResidueField L.R) ((residue L.R).comp (L.ι : S.A →+* L.R))).2
    rw [hk] at this
    rw [(h3 _).2.1, him]
    exact (this hx).resolve_left hP


[ACP_SRC_LOCCPL] Retained actual arithmetic completion from the local universal property
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/LocCpl.lean#L162-L188
theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.loc_cpl :
    ∀ {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [IsDiscreteValuationRing O] [IsCoef l O] {F : Frame l (ResidueField O)} {C₀ : ℕ} {n : ℕ}, F.Ok C₀ → (n = 2 ∨ n = 3) → ∀ S : Space O F n, ∀ (x : Ideal S.A) (L : S.Loc x), ∀ ι' : TensorProduct O L.O' S.A →ₐ[L.O'] L.R, (∀ a, ι' (1 ⊗ₜ a) = L.ι a) → @UpperTheory.V.CA.IsCplAt _ _ ((maximalIdeal L.R).comap ι'.toRingHom) L.R _ ι'.toRingHom.toAlgebra _ := by
  intro l _ O _ _ _ _ F C₀ n hF hn S x L ι' hι'
  obtain ⟨-, -, -, -, -, hnoeth, -⟩ := gr_A hF hn S
  haveI : IsNoetherianRing (TensorProduct O S.A L.O') := IsNoetherianRing.of_finite S.A _
  haveI : IsNoetherianRing (TensorProduct O L.O' S.A) :=
    isNoetherianRing_of_ringEquiv _ (Algebra.TensorProduct.comm O S.A L.O').toRingEquiv
  apply cpl_of_univ ι'
  intro C _ _ _ f hf
  letI : Algebra O C := Algebra.compHom C (algebraMap O L.O')
  haveI : IsScalarTower O L.O' C := IsScalarTower.of_algebraMap_eq (fun _ => rfl)
  let f' : S.A →ₐ[O] C := (f.restrictScalars O).comp Algebra.TensorProduct.includeRight
  obtain ⟨g, hg, hu⟩ := L.univ C f' (fun a => by
    show res L.O' C (f (1 ⊗ₜ a)) = _
    rw [← hι' a]
    exact hf _)
  refine ⟨g, fun t => ?_, fun g' hg' => hu g' (fun a => by
    rw [← hι' a]
    exact hg' _)⟩
  have hcomp : g.comp ι' = f := by
    apply Algebra.TensorProduct.ext_ring
    apply AlgHom.ext
    intro a
    show g (ι' (1 ⊗ₜ a)) = f (1 ⊗ₜ a)
    rw [hι', hg]
    rfl
  exact AlgHom.congr_fun hcomp t


[ACP_SRC_GRADED] Retained generic gr_A; UCE13 with its FinA antecedent cannot replace this upstream theorem

theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.gr_A :
    ∀ {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [IsDiscreteValuationRing O] [IsCoef l O] {F : Frame l (ResidueField O)} {C₀ : ℕ} {n : ℕ}, F.Ok C₀ → (n = 2 ∨ n = 3) → ∀ S : Space O F n, DirectSum.IsInternal S.gr ∧ SetLike.GradedMonoid S.gr ∧ S.gr 0 = 1 ∧ (∀ j, (S.gr j).FG) ∧ Algebra.FiniteType S.B S.A ∧ IsNoetherianRing S.A ∧ ∀ g i j, S.u.r g i j ∈ S.gr (w n i - w n j) := by

[ACP_SRC_SHARP2] Retained rank-two sharp component-chain supplier
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/ChainedBot.lean#L1-L86
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.FrobeniusSmoothVectors.Integral
