open IsLocalRing Matrix UpperTheory.V.Alg UpperTheory.V.U3 UpperTheory.V.Fr

private theorem ringKrullDim_quotient_eq_quotient_comap_of_surjective' {A B : Type} [CommRing A] [CommRing B] (p : A →+* B)
    (hp : Function.Surjective p) (Q : Ideal B) :
    ringKrullDim (B ⧸ Q) = ringKrullDim (A ⧸ Q.comap p) := by
  have hs : Function.Surjective ((Ideal.Quotient.mk Q).comp p) :=
    Ideal.Quotient.mk_surjective.comp hp
  have hk : RingHom.ker ((Ideal.Quotient.mk Q).comp p) = Q.comap p := by
    ext a
    simp [RingHom.mem_ker, Ideal.Quotient.eq_zero_iff_mem]
  rw [ringKrullDim_eq_of_ringEquiv (RingHom.quotientKerEquivOfSurjective hs).symm,
    ringKrullDim_eq_of_ringEquiv (Ideal.quotEquivOfEq hk)]

private theorem chained_bot_of_chained_ker_of_surjective {A B : Type} [CommRing A] [CommRing B] (p : A →+* B)
    (hp : Function.Surjective p) (D : ℕ) (h : Chained (RingHom.ker p) D) :
    Chained (⊥ : Ideal B) D := by
  have hmin : (RingHom.ker p).minimalPrimes = Ideal.comap p '' (⊥ : Ideal B).minimalPrimes := by
    rw [← Ideal.comap_minimalPrimes_eq_of_surjective hp]
    rfl
  have hc : ∀ Q ∈ (⊥ : Ideal B).minimalPrimes, Q.comap p ∈ (RingHom.ker p).minimalPrimes :=
    fun Q hQ => hmin ▸ ⟨Q, hQ, rfl⟩
  obtain ⟨h1, h2⟩ := h
  refine ⟨fun Q hQ => ?_, fun P hP Q hQ => ?_⟩
  · rw [ringKrullDim_quotient_eq_quotient_comap_of_surjective' p hp]
    exact h1 _ (hc Q hQ)
  · have key := h2 _ (hc P hP) _ (hc Q hQ)
    rw [← Ideal.map_comap_of_surjective p hp P, ← Ideal.map_comap_of_surjective p hp Q]
    refine Relation.ReflTransGen.lift (Ideal.map p) (fun a b hab => ?_) _ _ key
    obtain ⟨ha, hb, hd⟩ := hab
    rw [hmin] at ha hb
    obtain ⟨a', ha', rfl⟩ := ha
    obtain ⟨b', hb', rfl⟩ := hb
    show _ ∈ _ ∧ _ ∈ _ ∧ _
    rw [Ideal.map_comap_of_surjective p hp, Ideal.map_comap_of_surjective p hp]
    refine ⟨ha', hb', ?_⟩
    have hsup : (a' ⊔ b').comap p = a'.comap p ⊔ b'.comap p := by
      conv_lhs => rw [← Ideal.map_comap_of_surjective p hp a', ← Ideal.map_comap_of_surjective p hp b',
        ← Ideal.map_sup, Ideal.comap_map_of_surjective p hp]
      exact sup_eq_left.mpr (le_trans (Ideal.comap_mono bot_le) le_sup_left)
    rw [ringKrullDim_quotient_eq_quotient_comap_of_surjective' p hp, hsup]
    exact hd

theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.sharp2 :
    ∀ {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [IsDiscreteValuationRing O] [IsCoef l O] {F : Frame l (ResidueField O)} {C₀ : ℕ}, F.Ok C₀ → ∀ S : Space O F 2, ∀ (x : Ideal S.A) (L : S.Loc x), F.Big C₀ → ¬ S.u.triP ≤ x → Chained (⊥ : Ideal L.R) (2 * F.d + 1) ∧ ∀ P ∈ minimalPrimes L.R, L.ρ.TI P := by
  intro l _ O _ _ _ _ F C₀ hok S x L hbig hx
  obtain ⟨N, N', p, f, hN, hs, hk⟩ := pres hok (Or.inl rfl) S x L
  haveI : IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal L.O') L.O' := IsCoef.complete (l := l)
  haveI : IsCO L.O' (MvPowerSeries (Fin N) L.O') := co_mvps _ _ _
  have hdim := ((UpperTheory.V.CA.cl_cm).1 L.O' N).2
  have hch : Chained (Ideal.span (Set.range f)) (2 * F.d + 1) := by
    apply c1ch _ N' (2 * F.d + 1) f
    · rw [nilradical_eq_zero, Ideal.zero_eq_bot]
      exact Ideal.bot_prime
    · exact ⟨L.O', inferInstance, inferInstance, inferInstance, inferInstance, inferInstance, inferInstance⟩
    · intro i
      have hi : f i ∈ RingHom.ker p := hk ▸ Ideal.subset_span ⟨i, rfl⟩
      exact IsLocalRing.le_maximalIdeal (RingHom.ker_ne_top p) hi
    · rw [hdim]
      exact le_of_eq (congrArg (fun n : ℕ => (n : WithBot ℕ∞)) (by omega))
  have h1 : Chained (⊥ : Ideal L.R) (2 * F.d + 1) := by
    refine chained_bot_of_chained_ker_of_surjective (p : MvPowerSeries (Fin N) L.O' →+* L.R) hs _ ?_
    rw [← hk] at hch
    exact hch
  refine ⟨h1, fun P hP => ?_⟩
  have hr := (loc_rho hok (Or.inl rfl) S x L).2.2.2 hx
  have hl := (krull_dim_lt_of_not_le_triangular hok (Or.inl rfl) S x L hbig hx).1
  have hPp : P.IsPrime := hP.1.1
  refine ⟨⟨?_, ?_⟩, fun h => absurd h (by decide)⟩
  · rw [hr.1, top_le_iff]
    exact hPp.ne_top
  · intro hle
    have hge := h1.1 P hP
    have hmono : ringKrullDim (L.R ⧸ P) ≤ ringKrullDim (L.R ⧸ L.ρ.triM) :=
      ringKrullDim_le_of_surjective (Ideal.Quotient.factor hle) (Ideal.Quotient.factor_surjective hle)
    have hlt : ((2 * F.d + 1 + 1 : ℕ) : WithBot ℕ∞) < ((2 * F.d : ℕ) : WithBot ℕ∞) :=
      lt_of_le_of_lt (hge.trans hmono) hl
    have : 2 * F.d + 1 + 1 < 2 * F.d := by exact_mod_cast hlt
    omega


[ACP_SRC_SHARP3] Retained rank-three sharp component-chain supplier
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/ChainedOfComap.lean#L1-L142

open IsLocalRing Matrix UpperTheory.V.Alg UpperTheory.V.U3 UpperTheory.V.Fr

private theorem dim_comap {A R : Type} [CommRing A] [CommRing R] (p : A →+* R) (hp : Function.Surjective p)
    (P : Ideal R) : ringKrullDim (A ⧸ P.comap p) = ringKrullDim (R ⧸ P) := by
  have h : RingHom.ker ((Ideal.Quotient.mk P).comp p) = P.comap p := by
    ext a; simp [RingHom.mem_ker, Ideal.Quotient.eq_zero_iff_mem]
  rw [← h]
  exact ringKrullDim_eq_of_ringEquiv
    (RingHom.quotientKerEquivOfSurjective (Ideal.Quotient.mk_surjective.comp hp))

private theorem chained_of_comap {A R : Type} [CommRing A] [CommRing R] (p : A →+* R) (hp : Function.Surjective p)
    (I : Ideal R) (D : ℕ) (h : Chained (I.comap p) D) : Chained I D := by
  have hm : (I.comap p).minimalPrimes = Ideal.comap p '' I.minimalPrimes :=
    Ideal.comap_minimalPrimes_eq_of_surjective hp I
  refine ⟨fun P hP => ?_, fun P hP Q hQ => ?_⟩
  · rw [← dim_comap p hp]
    exact h.1 _ (hm ▸ ⟨P, hP, rfl⟩)
  · have h1 := h.2 (P.comap p) (hm ▸ ⟨P, hP, rfl⟩) (Q.comap p) (hm ▸ ⟨Q, hQ, rfl⟩)
    have h2 : ∀ b, Relation.ReflTransGen (fun P Q : Ideal A => P ∈ (I.comap p).minimalPrimes ∧
        Q ∈ (I.comap p).minimalPrimes ∧ (D : WithBot ℕ∞) ≤ ringKrullDim (A ⧸ (P ⊔ Q))) (P.comap p) b →
        Relation.ReflTransGen (fun P Q : Ideal R => P ∈ I.minimalPrimes ∧ Q ∈ I.minimalPrimes ∧
          (D : WithBot ℕ∞) ≤ ringKrullDim (R ⧸ (P ⊔ Q))) P (b.map p) := by
      intro b hb
      induction hb with
      | refl => rw [Ideal.map_comap_of_surjective p hp]
      | tail _ hab ih =>
        refine ih.tail ?_
        obtain ⟨ha, hb, hd⟩ := hab
        rw [hm] at ha hb
        obtain ⟨a, ha, rfl⟩ := ha
        obtain ⟨b, hb, rfl⟩ := hb
        rw [Ideal.map_comap_of_surjective p hp, Ideal.map_comap_of_surjective p hp]
        refine ⟨ha, hb, ?_⟩
        have : (a ⊔ b).comap p = a.comap p ⊔ b.comap p := by
          conv_lhs => rw [← Ideal.map_comap_of_surjective p hp a, ← Ideal.map_comap_of_surjective p hp b,
            ← Ideal.map_sup, Ideal.comap_map_of_surjective p hp]
          exact sup_eq_left.2 (le_sup_of_le_left (Ideal.comap_mono bot_le))
        rwa [← dim_comap p hp, this]
    have h3 := h2 _ h1
    rwa [Ideal.map_comap_of_surjective p hp] at h3

private theorem cc_mem {σ O R : Type} [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] [CommRing R] [Nontrivial R]
    (p : MvPowerSeries σ O →+* R) (f : MvPowerSeries σ O) (hf : p f = 0) :
    MvPowerSeries.constantCoeff f ∈ maximalIdeal O := by
  intro hu
  have := (MvPowerSeries.isUnit_iff_constantCoeff.2 hu).map p
  rw [hf] at this
  exact not_isUnit_zero this

private theorem detsec' (O : Type) [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [IsDiscreteValuationRing O]
    [IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal O) O] (a b g D : ℕ) (hg : g ≠ 0) (f : Fin b → MvPowerSeries (Fin a ⊕ Fin g) O)
    (M : Matrix (Fin g) (Fin g) (MvPowerSeries (Fin a ⊕ Fin g) O)) (h : a = b + D)
    (hf : ∀ i, MvPowerSeries.constantCoeff (f i) ∈ maximalIdeal O)
    (hM : ∀ i j, MvPowerSeries.constantCoeff (M i j) ∈ maximalIdeal O) :
    Chained (Ideal.span (Set.range f ∪ Set.range (M *ᵥ fun j => MvPowerSeries.X (.inr j)) ∪ {M.det})) D := by
  obtain ⟨g, rfl⟩ := Nat.exists_eq_succ_of_ne_zero hg
  exact detsec O a b g D f M h hf hM

theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.sharp3 :
    ∀ {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [IsDiscreteValuationRing O] [IsCoef l O] {F : Frame l (ResidueField O)} {C₀ : ℕ}, F.Ok C₀ → ∀ S : Space O F 3, ∀ (x : Ideal S.A) (L : S.Loc x), F.Big C₀ → ¬ S.u.triP ≤ x → ∃ I : Ideal L.R, L.Sharp I := by
  intro l _ O _ _ _ _ F C₀ hF S x L hB hx
  obtain ⟨-, -, hcmp, htri⟩ := loc_rho hF (Or.inr rfl) S x L
  have hoff : ∀ I : Ideal L.R, Chained I (3 * F.d + 1) → ∀ P ∈ I.minimalPrimes, L.ρ.OffTri P := by
    intro I hI P hP
    refine ⟨fun h => hP.1.1.ne_top (top_le_iff.1 ((htri hx).1 ▸ h)), fun h => ?_⟩
    have h1 := (krull_dim_lt_of_not_le_triangular hF (Or.inr rfl) S x L hB hx).1
    have h2 : ringKrullDim (L.R ⧸ P) ≤ ringKrullDim (L.R ⧸ L.ρ.triM) :=
      ringKrullDim_le_of_surjective (Ideal.Quotient.factor h) (Ideal.Quotient.factor_surjective h)
    have h3 := ((hI.1 P hP).trans h2).trans_lt h1
    have h4 : 3 * F.d + 1 + 1 < 3 * F.d := by exact_mod_cast h3
    omega
  by_cases hee : S.u.ee ≤ x
  · obtain ⟨E⟩ := epres hF S x L hee
    have hG : ∀ i, E.Go i ∈ RingHom.ker E.p := fun i =>
      E.ker ▸ Ideal.subset_span (Or.inr ⟨i, rfl⟩)
    have hFe : ∀ i, E.Fe i ∈ RingHom.ker E.p := fun i =>
      E.ker ▸ Ideal.subset_span (Or.inl ⟨i, rfl⟩)
    choose M hM hGM using fun i => oddm L.O' E.a E.g (E.Go i) (E.odd i) (E.min _ (hG i))
    have hGo : E.Go = Matrix.of M *ᵥ fun j => MvPowerSeries.X (.inr j) := funext hGM
    have hch : Chained (Ideal.span {E.p (Matrix.of M).det}) (3 * F.d + 1) := by
      refine chained_of_comap E.p.toRingHom E.surj _ _ ?_
      have h1 := detsec' L.O' E.a E.b E.g _ (gpos hF S x L hx E) E.Fe (Matrix.of M) E.num
        (fun i => cc_mem E.p.toRingHom _ (hFe i)) hM
      rw [← hGo, Ideal.span_union, ← E.ker] at h1
      convert h1 using 1
      have : Ideal.span {E.p (Matrix.of M).det} = (Ideal.span {(Matrix.of M).det}).map E.p.toRingHom := by
        rw [Ideal.map_span, Set.image_singleton]; rfl
      rw [this, Ideal.comap_map_of_surjective E.p.toRingHom E.surj, sup_comm]
      rfl
    refine ⟨_, fun h => absurd hee h, fun P hP hne => ?_, hch, fun P hP => ?_⟩
    · rw [E.ee, Ideal.span_le, Set.range_subset_iff, not_forall] at hne
      obtain ⟨j, hj⟩ := hne
      rw [Ideal.span_singleton_le_iff_mem]
      refine (hP.mem_or_mem ?_).resolve_right hj
      have h1 : ((Matrix.of M).adjugate *ᵥ E.Go) j = (Matrix.of M).det * MvPowerSeries.X (.inr j) := by
        rw [hGo, Matrix.mulVec_mulVec, Matrix.adjugate_mul, Matrix.smul_mulVec, Matrix.one_mulVec]; rfl
      have h2 : ((Matrix.of M).adjugate *ᵥ E.Go) j ∈ RingHom.ker E.p := by
        rw [Matrix.mulVec, dotProduct]
        exact Ideal.sum_mem _ fun i _ => Ideal.mul_mem_left _ _ (hG i)
      rw [h1, RingHom.mem_ker, map_mul] at h2
      rw [h2]
      exact P.zero_mem
    · by_cases he : L.ρ.ee ≤ P
      · exact Or.inr (thick hF S x L E _ hGo P hP.1.1 ⟨hoff _ hch P hP, rfl, he⟩
          (hP.1.2 (Ideal.mem_span_singleton_self _)))
      · exact Or.inl ⟨hoff _ hch P hP, fun _ => he⟩
  · obtain ⟨N, N', p, f, hN, hp, hk⟩ := pres hF (Or.inr rfl) S x L
    have hch : Chained (⊥ : Ideal L.R) (3 * F.d + 1) := by
      refine chained_of_comap p.toRingHom hp _ _ ?_
      haveI hco : IsCO L.O' (MvPowerSeries (Fin N) L.O') := co_mvps L.O' L.O' (Fin N)
      haveI : IsNoetherianRing (MvPowerSeries (Fin N) L.O') := hco.toIsNoetherian
      haveI : IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal (MvPowerSeries (Fin N) L.O')) (MvPowerSeries (Fin N) L.O') :=
        hco.complete
      have h1 := c1ch (MvPowerSeries (Fin N) L.O') N' (3 * F.d + 1) f
        (by rw [nilradical_eq_zero]; exact Ideal.isPrime_bot) ⟨L.O', L.cO, L.dO, L.vO, inferInstance, _, hco⟩
        (fun i hu => by
          have h0 : f i ∈ RingHom.ker p := hk ▸ Ideal.subset_span ⟨i, rfl⟩
          have := hu.map p
          rw [RingHom.mem_ker.1 h0] at this
          exact not_isUnit_zero this)
        (by
          rw [GlobalTheory.FormalIsogeny.ringKrullDim_mvPowerSeries, IsDiscreteValuationRing.ringKrullDim_eq_one,
            Nat.card_eq_fintype_card, Fintype.card_fin, hN]
          norm_cast
          omega)
      rw [← hk] at h1
      exact h1
    refine ⟨⊥, fun _ => rfl, fun _ _ _ => bot_le, hch, fun P hP => Or.inl ⟨hoff _ hch P hP, fun _ h => hee ?_⟩⟩
    exact ((hcmp P).2.2.1 h).trans ((Ideal.comap_mono (le_maximalIdeal hP.1.1.ne_top)).trans L.lies.le)


[ACP_SRC_DIM] Retained triangular and coincident-character dimension bounds
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/KrullDimLtOfNotLeTriangular.lean#L1-L58
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.FrobeniusSmoothVectors.Integral
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.FrobeniusSmoothVectors.CharpolyCoeffsInSubalgebra
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.FrobeniusSmoothVectors.TameInertiaCondition

open IsLocalRing Matrix UpperTheory.V.Alg UpperTheory.V.U3 UpperTheory.V.Fr

private theorem dim_le_of_primes {A : Type} [CommRing A] (I : Ideal A) (c : WithBot ℕ∞)
    (h : ∀ P : Ideal A, P.IsPrime → I ≤ P → ringKrullDim (A ⧸ P) ≤ c) : ringKrullDim (A ⧸ I) ≤ c := by
  rw [ringKrullDim_quotient]
  refine iSup_le fun s => ?_
  refine le_trans ?_ (h s.head.1.asIdeal s.head.1.isPrime s.head.2)
  rw [ringKrullDim_quotient]
  let t : LTSeries (PrimeSpectrum.zeroLocus (R := A) s.head.1.asIdeal) :=
    ⟨s.length, fun i => ⟨(s i).1, (show s.head.1 ≤ (s i).1 from s.head_le i)⟩, fun i => s.step i⟩
  exact Order.LTSeries.length_le_krullDim t

private theorem dim_anti {A : Type} [CommRing A] {I J : Ideal A} (h : I ≤ J) :
    ringKrullDim (A ⧸ J) ≤ ringKrullDim (A ⧸ I) :=
  ringKrullDim_le_of_surjective (Ideal.Quotient.factor h) (Ideal.Quotient.factor_surjective h)

private theorem le_of_add_le {a : WithBot ℕ∞} {b c m : ℕ} (h : a + b ≤ (c : ℕ)) (hm : c ≤ m + b) :
    a ≤ (m : ℕ) := by
  induction a using WithBot.recBotCoe with
  | bot => exact bot_le
  | coe a =>
    induction a using ENat.recTopCoe with
    | top =>
      exfalso
      have h' : (⊤ : ℕ∞) + (b : ℕ∞) ≤ (c : ℕ∞) := by exact_mod_cast h
      simp at h'
    | coe a =>
      have h' : a + b ≤ c := by exact_mod_cast h
      have : a ≤ m := by omega
      exact_mod_cast this

theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.krull_dim_lt_of_not_le_triangular :
    ∀ {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [IsDiscreteValuationRing O] [IsCoef l O] {F : Frame l (ResidueField O)} {C₀ : ℕ} {n : ℕ}, F.Ok C₀ → (n = 2 ∨ n = 3) → ∀ S : Space O F n, ∀ (x : Ideal S.A) (L : S.Loc x), F.Big C₀ → ¬ S.u.triP ≤ x → ringKrullDim (L.R ⧸ L.ρ.triM) < (n * F.d : ℕ) ∧ ∀ I : Ideal S.B, S.FinLam I → ∀ v i j, i ≠ j → ringKrullDim (L.R ⧸ (I.map L.b ⊔ (maximalIdeal O).map (algebraMap O L.R) ⊔ Ideal.span {y | ∃ g ∈ F.Nv v, y = (L.ρ.ψ v i g : L.R) - L.ρ.ψ v j g})) < (n * F.d : ℕ) := by
  intro l _ O _ _ _ _ F C₀ n hF hn S x L hB hx
  obtain ⟨h1, h2⟩ := l51 hF hn S x L hx
  obtain ⟨hd, hdv⟩ := hB
  have hlt : ((n * F.d - 1 : ℕ) : WithBot ℕ∞) < (n * F.d : ℕ) := by
    have : n * F.d - 1 < n * F.d := by rcases hn with rfl | rfl <;> omega
    exact_mod_cast this
  have hT : ringKrullDim (L.R ⧸ L.ρ.triM) ≤ (n * F.d - 1 : ℕ) := by
    refine h1.trans ?_
    have : (n - 1) * F.d + 2 + (3 * C₀ + 5) ≤ n * F.d - 1 := by rcases hn with rfl | rfl <;> omega
    exact_mod_cast this
  refine ⟨hT.trans_lt hlt, fun I hI v i j hij => lt_of_le_of_lt (dim_le_of_primes _ _ fun P hP hle => ?_) hlt⟩
  by_cases hM : L.ρ.triM ≤ P
  · exact (dim_anti hM).trans hT
  have hoff : L.ρ.OffTri P := ⟨fun h => hP.ne_top (top_le_iff.1 (h2 ▸ h)), hM⟩
  have hIP : I ≤ L.π P :=
    (Ideal.map_le_iff_le_comap (f := L.b)).1 (le_sup_left.trans (le_sup_left.trans hle))
  have hco : L.ρ.Coin P v i j := fun g hg => (le_sup_right.trans hle) (Ideal.subset_span ⟨g, hg, rfl⟩)
  have h := (l52 hF hn S x L I hI v i j hij P hP hoff hIP hco).2 (le_sup_right.trans (le_sup_left.trans hle))
  refine le_of_add_le h ?_
  have := hdv v
  rcases hn with rfl | rfl <;> omega


[ACP_SRC_CONN] Retained preconnected core supplier
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/PreconnectedRankTwo.lean#L1-L264
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.FrobeniusSmoothVectors.Integral
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.FrobeniusSmoothVectors.StrSMul
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.FrobeniusSmoothVectors.Imports
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.FrobeniusSmoothVectors.FramePointFiberNonempty
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.FrobeniusSmoothVectors.ExistsMemStrSNeZeroOfFrameOk
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.FrobeniusSmoothVectors.SmoothHomWeightSumNeg
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.FrobeniusSmoothVectors.SmoothCharacterExtendsToOpenSubgroup
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.FrobeniusSmoothVectors.SmoothDualExact

open IsLocalRing Matrix UpperTheory.V.Alg UpperTheory.V.U3 UpperTheory.V.Fr

private theorem pol2 {Q : Type} [CommRing Q] (x y z : Q) :
    (!![z, 0; y, x] : Matrix (Fin 2) (Fin 2) Q) = Jmat Q 2 * (!![x, 0; y, z])ᵀ * Jmat Q 2 := by
  ext i j
  fin_cases i <;> fin_cases j <;>
    simp [Jmat, Matrix.mul_apply, Fin.sum_univ_two, Matrix.transpose_apply]

private theorem ord2 {H Q : Type} [CommRing Q] (r : H → Matrix (Fin 2) (Fin 2) Q) (ψ : Fin 2 → H → Q) (γ : Q)
    (h : ∀ s, r s = !![ψ 0 s, 0; (ψ 0 s - ψ 1 s) * γ, ψ 1 s]) (g : Fin 2 → H) : ordProd r ψ g = 0 := by
  have e : ∀ i : Fin 2, List.ofFn (fun i => r (g i) - ψ i (g i) • (1 : Matrix (Fin 2) (Fin 2) Q)) =
      [r (g 0) - ψ 0 (g 0) • 1, r (g 1) - ψ 1 (g 1) • 1] := fun _ => by
    simp [List.ofFn_succ]
  unfold ordProd
  rw [e 0, h, h]
  ext i j
  fin_cases i <;> fin_cases j <;>
    simp [Matrix.mul_apply, Fin.sum_univ_two] <;> ring

private theorem hom_ex {G Q : Type} [Group G] [CommRing Q] (p q : G →* Q) (a : G → Q) (a0 : a 1 = 0)
    (h : ∀ g g', a (g * g') = a g * p g' + q g * a g') :
    ∃ r : G →* Matrix (Fin 2) (Fin 2) Q, ∀ g, r g = !![p g, 0; a g, q g] := by
  refine ⟨{ toFun := fun g => !![p g, 0; a g, q g],
            map_one' := ?_, map_mul' := ?_ }, fun g => rfl⟩
  · simp only [map_one, a0]
    ext i j
    fin_cases i <;> fin_cases j <;> simp
  · intro g g'
    simp only [h]
    ext i j
    fin_cases i <;> fin_cases j <;>
      simp [Matrix.mul_apply, Fin.sum_univ_two, map_mul] <;> ring

private theorem isLocallyConstant_psi_restrict_of_isOpen_ker {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {k : Type} [Field k] {F : Frame l k}
    (hΨ : IsOpen (F.Ψ.ker : Set F.Γ)) : IsLocallyConstant fun g : F.G0 => F.Ψ (g : F.Γ) := by
  rw [IsLocallyConstant.iff_isOpen_fiber_apply]
  intro g
  have hK : IsOpen ((fun h : F.G0 => ((g⁻¹ * h : F.G0) : F.Γ)) ⁻¹' (F.Ψ.ker : Set F.Γ)) :=
    hΨ.preimage (continuous_subtype_val.comp (continuous_const.mul continuous_id))
  convert hK using 1
  ext h
  simp only [Set.mem_preimage, Set.mem_singleton_iff, SetLike.mem_coe, MonoidHom.mem_ker,
    Subgroup.coe_mul, Subgroup.coe_inv, map_mul, map_inv]
  constructor
  · intro e
    rw [e, inv_mul_cancel]
  · intro e
    exact (inv_mul_eq_one.mp e).symm

private theorem rep_ex {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {k : Type} [Field k] [CharP k l] {F : Frame l k} {C₀ : ℕ}
    (hF : F.Ok C₀) (Q : Type) [CommRing Q] [Algebra k Q] {a : F.G0 → Q} (ha : a ∈ F.strS Q) :
    ∃ ρ : NRep F 2 Q, ρ.IsBar (algebraMap k Q) ∧ ρ.triM = ⊥ ∧ (∀ i j, IsLocallyConstant fun g => ρ.r g i j) ∧
      ∀ g, ρ.r g 1 0 = a g := by
  classical
  obtain ⟨hlc, hmul, hstar, hloc⟩ := ha
  let φ : F.G0 →* kˣ := F.Ψ.comp F.del.ker.subtype
  let p : F.G0 →* Q := (algebraMap k Q).toMonoidHom.comp ((Units.coeHom k).comp φ)
  let q : F.G0 →* Q := (algebraMap k Q).toMonoidHom.comp ((Units.coeHom k).comp φ⁻¹)
  have hp : ∀ g, p g = algebraMap k Q (F.Ψ (g : F.Γ) : k) := fun g => rfl
  have hq : ∀ g, q g = algebraMap k Q ((F.Ψ (g : F.Γ))⁻¹ : kˣ) := fun g => rfl
  have hΨstar : ∀ g : F.G0, F.Ψ ((F.star g : F.G0) : F.Γ) = (F.Ψ (g : F.Γ))⁻¹ := by
    intro g
    simp only [Frame.star, Frame.conj, MulEquiv.coe_toMonoidHom, MulAut.conjNormal_apply,
      map_mul, map_inv, Subgroup.coe_inv]
    rw [mul_comm, ← mul_assoc, inv_mul_cancel, one_mul]
  have a0 : a 1 = 0 := by
    have h1 := hmul 1 1
    simp only [mul_one, map_one, Units.val_one, one_smul, inv_one] at h1
    simpa using h1
  have hmul' : ∀ g g', a (g * g') = a g * p g' + q g * a g' := by
    intro g g'
    rw [hmul, hp, hq, Algebra.smul_def, Algebra.smul_def, mul_comm (algebraMap k Q _) (a g)]
  obtain ⟨r, hr⟩ := hom_ex p q a a0 hmul'
  let χ : (v : F.V) → F.Gv v →* Qˣ := fun v => (Units.map (algebraMap k Q).toMonoidHom).comp (F.Ψ.comp (F.loc v))
  have hpstar : ∀ g, p (F.star g) = q g := fun g => by rw [hp, hq, hΨstar]
  have hqstar : ∀ g, q (F.star g) = p g := fun g => by rw [hp, hq, hΨstar, inv_inv]
  let ρ : NRep F 2 Q :=
    { r := r
      ψ := fun v => ![χ v, (χ v)⁻¹]
      pol := by
        intro g
        rw [hr, hr, hpstar, hqstar, hstar]
        exact pol2 _ _ _
      diag := by
        intro i j hij
        rw [hr, hr]
        fin_cases i <;> fin_cases j
        · exact absurd rfl hij
        · simp
        · simp [hstar]
        · exact absurd rfl hij
      ord := by
        intro v g
        obtain ⟨γ, hγ⟩ := hloc v
        refine ord2 _ _ γ (fun s => ?_) g
        rw [hr, hγ]
        simp [χ, hp, hq, Algebra.smul_def, Frame.loc] }
  refine ⟨ρ, ⟨?_, ?_⟩, ?_, ?_, ?_⟩
  · intro g i
    show r g i i = _
    rw [hr]
    fin_cases i <;> simp [w, hp, hq]
  · intro v i g
    fin_cases i <;> simp [ρ, χ, w]
  · apply Ideal.span_eq_bot.mpr
    rintro _ ⟨g, i, j, hij, rfl⟩
    show r g i j = 0
    rw [hr]
    fin_cases i <;> fin_cases j <;> simp at hij ⊢
  · have hl : IsLocallyConstant fun g : F.G0 => F.Ψ (g : F.Γ) := isLocallyConstant_psi_restrict_of_isOpen_ker hF.Ψ_open
    intro i j
    show IsLocallyConstant fun g => r g i j
    simp only [hr]
    fin_cases i <;> fin_cases j
    · exact hl.comp fun z => algebraMap k Q (z : k)
    · exact IsLocallyConstant.const _
    · exact hlc
    · exact hl.comp fun z => algebraMap k Q ((z⁻¹ : kˣ) : k)
  · intro g
    show r g 1 0 = a g
    rw [hr]
    simp

section

variable {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [IsDiscreteValuationRing O] [IsCoef l O]
  {F : Frame l (ResidueField O)} {C₀ : ℕ} (hF : F.Ok C₀) (S : Space O F 2)
include hF

private theorem pt_ex (Q : Type) [CommRing Q] [Nontrivial Q] [Algebra (ResidueField O) Q] {a : F.G0 → Q}
    (h : a ∈ F.strS Q) : ∃ ξ : S.A →+* Q, S.FPt ξ ∧ S.u.triM ≤ RingHom.ker ξ ∧ ∀ g, ξ (S.u.r g 1 0) = a g := by
  haveI : CharP (ResidueField O) l := IsCoef.char
  obtain ⟨ρ, hbar, htri, hlc, h10⟩ := rep_ex hF Q h
  obtain ⟨ξ, hξ, hmap⟩ := UpperTheory.smoothHom_weight_sum_neg hF (Or.inl rfl) S Q ρ hbar htri hlc
  refine ⟨ξ, hξ, ?_, fun g => ?_⟩
  · rw [NRep.triM, Ideal.span_le]
    rintro _ ⟨g, i, j, hij, rfl⟩
    rw [SetLike.mem_coe, RingHom.mem_ker]
    have hm : ρ.r g i j ∈ ρ.triM := Ideal.subset_span ⟨g, i, j, hij, rfl⟩
    rw [htri, Ideal.mem_bot] at hm
    rw [← hm, ← hmap, NRep.map_r]
  · rw [← h10, ← hmap, NRep.map_r]

private theorem pt_uniq {Q : Type} [CommRing Q] [Algebra (ResidueField O) Q] {ξ ξ' : S.A →+* Q}
    (h : S.FPt ξ) (h' : S.FPt ξ') (t : S.u.triM ≤ RingHom.ker ξ) (t' : S.u.triM ≤ RingHom.ker ξ')
    (e : ∀ g, ξ' (S.u.r g 1 0) = ξ (S.u.r g 1 0)) : ξ' = ξ := by
  have H := UpperTheory.smoothCharacter_extends_to_open_subgroup hF (Or.inl rfl) S Q ξ h
  have H' := UpperTheory.smoothCharacter_extends_to_open_subgroup hF (Or.inl rfl) S Q ξ' h'
  refine H.2.2 ξ' h' (fun g i j => ?_)
  have z : ∀ {ζ : S.A →+* Q}, S.u.triM ≤ RingHom.ker ζ → ζ (S.u.r g 0 1) = 0 := fun hz =>
    RingHom.mem_ker.mp (hz (Ideal.subset_span ⟨g, 0, 1, by decide, rfl⟩))
  have d : ∀ i : Fin 2, ξ' (S.u.r g i i) = ξ (S.u.r g i i) := by
    intro i
    have d1 := H.1.1 g i
    have d2 := H'.1.1 g i
    rw [NRep.map_r] at d1 d2
    rw [d1, d2]
  fin_cases i <;> fin_cases j
  · exact d 0
  · exact (z t').trans (z t).symm
  · exact e g
  · exact d 1

private theorem pt_mem {Q : Type} [CommRing Q] [Algebra (ResidueField O) Q] {ξ : S.A →+* Q}
    (h : S.FPt ξ) (t : S.u.triM ≤ RingHom.ker ξ) : (fun g => ξ (S.u.r g 1 0)) ∈ F.strS Q := by
  have H := UpperTheory.smoothCharacter_extends_to_open_subgroup hF (Or.inl rfl) S Q ξ h
  haveI : CharP (ResidueField O) l := IsCoef.char
  have htri : (NRep.map ξ S.u).triM = ⊥ := by
    apply Ideal.span_eq_bot.mpr
    rintro _ ⟨g, i, j, hij, rfl⟩
    rw [NRep.map_r]
    exact RingHom.mem_ker.mp (t (Ideal.subset_span ⟨g, i, j, hij, rfl⟩))
  have h2 := framePoint_fiber_nonempty hF Q (NRep.map ξ S.u) H.1 htri H.2.1
  simpa only [NRep.map_r] using h2

private theorem line {K : Type} [Field K] [Algebra (ResidueField O) K] {A : F.G0 → Polynomial K}
    (h : A ∈ F.strS (Polynomial K)) :
    ∃ η : PrimeSpectrum S.A, S.mB ≤ S.π η.asIdeal ∧
      ∀ (c : K) (ξ : S.A →+* K), S.FPt ξ → S.u.triM ≤ RingHom.ker ξ →
        (∀ g, (A g).eval c = ξ (S.u.r g 1 0)) → η.asIdeal ≤ RingHom.ker ξ := by
  obtain ⟨ξA, hA, tA, vA⟩ := pt_ex hF S (Polynomial K) h
  refine ⟨⟨RingHom.ker ξA, RingHom.ker_isPrime ξA⟩, ?_, ?_⟩
  · exact Ideal.le_comap_map.trans (Ideal.comap_mono hA.x0)
  · intro c ξ hξ tξ vξ
    let φ : Polynomial K →ₐ[ResidueField O] K := (Polynomial.aeval c).restrictScalars (ResidueField O)
    have hφ : S.FPt ((φ : Polynomial K →+* K).comp ξA) := hA.comp φ
    have tφ : S.u.triM ≤ RingHom.ker ((φ : Polynomial K →+* K).comp ξA) := fun x hx => by
      rw [RingHom.mem_ker, RingHom.comp_apply, RingHom.mem_ker.mp (tA hx), map_zero]
    have heq : (φ : Polynomial K →+* K).comp ξA = ξ := pt_uniq hF S hξ hφ tξ tφ (fun g => by
      rw [RingHom.comp_apply, vA g, ← vξ g]
      simp [φ, Polynomial.coe_aeval_eq_eval])
    intro x hx
    rw [RingHom.mem_ker, ← heq, RingHom.comp_apply, RingHom.mem_ker.mp hx, map_zero]

end

theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.preconnected_rank_two :
    ∀ {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [IsDiscreteValuationRing O] [IsCoef l O] {F : Frame l (ResidueField O)} {C₀ : ℕ}, F.Ok C₀ → ∀ S : Space O F 2, IsPreconnected S.BB := by
  intro l _ O _ _ _ _ F C₀ hF S
  classical
  haveI : CharP (ResidueField O) l := IsCoef.char
  obtain ⟨b, hb, hb0⟩ := UpperTheory.exists_mem_strS_ne_zero_of_frameOk hF
  obtain ⟨ξb, hbF, hbt, hbv⟩ := pt_ex hF S (ResidueField O) hb
  let qb : PrimeSpectrum S.A := ⟨RingHom.ker ξb, RingHom.ker_isPrime ξb⟩
  have hnz : ∃ g, b g ≠ 0 := by
    by_contra hc
    push_neg at hc
    exact hb0 (funext hc)
  obtain ⟨g1, hg1⟩ := hnz
  have hqb : qb ∈ S.BB := by
    refine ⟨Ideal.le_comap_map.trans (Ideal.comap_mono hbF.x0), fun hle => hg1 ?_⟩
    have hm : S.u.r g1 1 0 ∈ S.u.triP := Ideal.subset_span ⟨g1, 1, 0, by decide, rfl⟩
    rw [← hbv g1]
    exact RingHom.mem_ker.mp (hle hm)
  refine isPreconnected_of_forall qb (fun p hp => ?_)
  obtain ⟨K, _, _, ξ, hker, hξ, tξ⟩ := UpperTheory.smoothDual_exact hF (Or.inl rfl) S p hp
  have ha := pt_mem hF S hξ tξ
  let bK : F.G0 → K := fun g => algebraMap (ResidueField O) K (b g)
  have hbK : bK ∈ F.strS K := F.strS_map (Algebra.linearMap (ResidueField O) K) hb
  let Lin : K →ₗ[ResidueField O] Polynomial K :=
    (Algebra.linearMap K (Polynomial K)).restrictScalars (ResidueField O)
  let A : F.G0 → Polynomial K := fun g =>
    (1 - Polynomial.X) * Lin (ξ (S.u.r g 1 0)) + Polynomial.X * Lin (bK g)
  have hAmem : A ∈ F.strS (Polynomial K) :=
    Submodule.add_mem _ (F.strS_mul _ (F.strS_map Lin ha)) (F.strS_mul _ (F.strS_map Lin hbK))
  obtain ⟨η, hηB, hη⟩ := line hF S hAmem
  have hη0 : η.asIdeal ≤ p.asIdeal := by
    rw [← hker]
    exact hη 0 ξ hξ tξ (fun g => by simp [A, Lin])
  let ι : ResidueField O →ₐ[ResidueField O] K := Algebra.ofId _ _
  have tb' : S.u.triM ≤ RingHom.ker ((ι : ResidueField O →+* K).comp ξb) := fun x hx => by
    rw [RingHom.mem_ker, RingHom.comp_apply, RingHom.mem_ker.mp (hbt hx), map_zero]
  have hη1 : η.asIdeal ≤ qb.asIdeal := by
    have h1 := hη 1 ((ι : ResidueField O →+* K).comp ξb) (hbF.comp ι) tb'
      (fun g => by simp [A, Lin, bK, hbv, ι])
    intro x hx
    have hx1 := h1 hx
    rw [RingHom.mem_ker, RingHom.comp_apply] at hx1
    exact RingHom.mem_ker.mpr ((ι : ResidueField O →+* K).injective (by rw [hx1, map_zero]))
  have hηBB : η ∈ S.BB := ⟨hηB, fun hle => hp.2 (hle.trans hη0)⟩
  refine ⟨{η, p, qb}, ?_, by simp, by simp, ?_⟩
  · intro z hz
    simp only [Set.mem_insert_iff, Set.mem_singleton_iff] at hz
    rcases hz with rfl | rfl | rfl
    · exact hηBB
    · exact hp
    · exact hqb
  · refine (isPreconnected_singleton (x := η)).subset_closure (by simp) ?_
    intro z hz
    simp only [Set.mem_insert_iff, Set.mem_singleton_iff] at hz
    rw [← PrimeSpectrum.le_iff_mem_closure]
    rcases hz with rfl | rfl | rfl
    · exact le_rfl
    · exact hη0
    · exact hη1


[ACP_SRC_SEEDPT] Retained passage from the seed to a large local prime
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/Seedpt.lean#L1-L30
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.FrobeniusSmoothVectors.Integral
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.FrobeniusSmoothVectors.FrobeniusCornerLiftTwoToThree
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.FrobeniusSmoothVectors.MonMemPow
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.FrobeniusSmoothVectors.LocDim
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.FrobeniusSmoothVectors.LocRho

open IsLocalRing Matrix UpperTheory.V.Alg UpperTheory.V.U3 UpperTheory.V.Fr

theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.seedpt :
    ∀ {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [IsDiscreteValuationRing O] [IsCoef l O] {F : Frame l (ResidueField O)} {C₀ : ℕ} {n : ℕ}, F.Ok C₀ → (n = 2 ∨ n = 3) → ∀ S : Space O F n, ∀ (p : Ideal S.B) (ξ : Ideal S.A), p.IsPrime → ξ.IsPrime → S.u.TI ξ → p ≤ S.π ξ → ∃ x : PrimeSpectrum S.A, x.asIdeal.IsMaximal ∧ x ∈ S.BB ∧ ∀ L : S.Loc x.asIdeal, ∃ P : Ideal L.R, P.IsPrime ∧ L.ρ.TI P ∧ L.π P = p ∧ ringKrullDim (L.R ⧸ P) = ringKrullDim (S.B ⧸ p) + 1 := by
  intro l _ O _ _ _ _ F C₀ n hF hn S p ξ hp hξ hTI hpξ
  obtain ⟨η, hηp, hhom, hπ, hTIη, htd⟩ := frobenius_corner_lift_two_to_three hF hn S p ξ hp hξ hTI hpξ
  have hTIη' := hTIη
  unfold NRep.TI NRep.OffTri at hTIη'
  obtain ⟨x, hxmax, hxBB, hηx⟩ := l41 hF hn S η hηp hhom hTIη'.1.1
  refine ⟨x, hxmax, hxBB, fun L => ?_⟩
  have htd' : S.td η = ((1 : ℕ) : _) := by
    rw [htd]
    rfl
  obtain ⟨P, hP, himP, hdim⟩ := (loc_dim hF hn S x.asIdeal L).2 η hηp hηx 1 htd'
  have hloc := (loc_rho hF hn S x.asIdeal L).2.2.1 P
  rw [himP] at hloc
  refine ⟨P, hP, ?_, ?_, ?_⟩
  · unfold NRep.TI NRep.OffTri
    exact ⟨⟨fun h => hTIη'.1.1 (hloc.1.mp h), fun h => hTIη'.1.2 (hloc.2.1.mp h)⟩,
      fun h3 h => hTIη'.2 h3 (hloc.2.2.mp h)⟩
  · show S.π (L.im P) = p
    rw [himP, hπ]
  · rw [hdim, hπ]
    norm_num


[ACP_SRC_CORNER] Retained actual rank-two corner and finite exceptional-type ideal l53
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/QuotientHomNonvanishingBound.lean#L1-L236
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.FrobeniusSmoothVectors.FrobeniusCornerConeMap
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.FrobeniusSmoothVectors.ExistsFrobeniusCorner
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.FrobeniusSmoothVectors.FrobeniusCornerUnits
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.FrobeniusSmoothVectors.FrobeniusCornerMemCone
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.FrobeniusSmoothVectors.FrobeniusCornerMonomial
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.FrobeniusSmoothVectors.FrobeniusCornerMiddleMem
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.FrobeniusSmoothVectors.FrobeniusCornerMaximalIdealLe

open IsLocalRing Matrix UpperTheory.V.Alg UpperTheory.V.U3 UpperTheory.V.Fr

private theorem finite_lambda_surjective_injective_lift :
    ∀ {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [IsDiscreteValuationRing O] [IsCoef l O] {F : Frame l (ResidueField O)} {C₀ : ℕ}, F.Ok C₀ → ∀ (S₂ : Space O F 2) (S₃ : Space O F 3) (I₂ : Ideal S₂.B), S₂.FinLam I₂ → ∀ (C : Type) [CommRing C] [Algebra O C] (K : Type) [CommRing K] [Algebra O K] (p : S₃.B →ₐ[O] C) (q : S₃.A →ₐ[O] K) (j : C →ₐ[O] K), Function.Surjective p → Function.Injective j → (∀ b, q (S₃.inc b) = j (p b)) → ∀ (σ : NRep F 2 K) (ξ : S₂.A →ₐ[O] K) (β : S₂.B →ₐ[O] C), IsCorner (S₃.u.map (q : S₃.A →+* K)) σ → S₂.u.map (ξ : S₂.A →+* K) = σ → (∀ b, ξ (S₂.inc b) = j (β b)) → S₃.FinLam ((I₂.map β).comap p) := by
  intro l _ O _ _ _ _ F C₀ hF S₂ S₃ I₂ hI₂ C _ _ K _ _ p q j hp hj hpq σ ξ β hc hσ hβ
  subst hσ
  obtain ⟨hco₂, hm₂⟩ := co_B hF (Or.inl rfl) S₂
  have hI₂' : S₂.mB ≤ (I₂ ⊔ S₂.lamId).radical := hI₂
  show S₃.mB ≤ ((I₂.map β).comap p ⊔ S₃.lamId).radical
  rw [Ideal.radical_eq_sInf]
  refine le_sInf ?_
  rintro P ⟨hJP, hPp⟩
  have hker : ∀ y, p y = 0 → y ∈ P := by
    intro y hy
    apply hJP
    apply Ideal.mem_sup_left
    rw [Ideal.mem_comap, hy]
    exact zero_mem _
  have hPc : (P.map p).comap p = P := by
    rw [Ideal.comap_map_of_surjective _ hp]
    refine sup_eq_left.mpr ?_
    intro y hy
    exact hker y (by simpa using hy)
  have hpull : ∀ y, p y ∈ P.map p → y ∈ P := by
    intro y hy
    rw [← hPc]
    exact hy
  have hlam : S₃.lamId ≤ P := le_sup_right.trans hJP
  have hqeq : ∀ y y' : S₃.B, q (S₃.inc y) = q (S₃.inc y') → y - y' ∈ P := by
    intro y y' h
    rw [hpq, hpq] at h
    exact hker _ (by rw [map_sub, hj h, sub_self])
  have hψ : ∀ v i g, β (S₂.ψB v i g) = p (S₃.ψB v (cor i) g) := by
    intro v i g
    apply hj
    rw [← hβ, ← hpq]
    exact congrArg (fun u : Kˣ => (u : K)) (hc.ψ v i g)
  have hIP : I₂.map β ≤ P.map p := by
    rw [Ideal.map_le_iff_le_comap]
    intro b hb
    obtain ⟨y, hy⟩ := hp (β b)
    rw [Ideal.mem_comap, ← hy]
    apply Ideal.mem_map_of_mem
    apply hJP
    apply Ideal.mem_sup_left
    rw [Ideal.mem_comap, hy]
    exact Ideal.mem_map_of_mem _ hb
  haveI : (P.map p).IsPrime := Ideal.map_isPrime_of_surjective hp (fun y hy => hker y hy)
  have hQ : S₂.mB ≤ (P.map p).comap β := by
    refine hI₂'.trans ((Ideal.radical_mono ?_).trans_eq (Ideal.IsPrime.radical inferInstance))
    refine sup_le (Ideal.map_le_iff_le_comap.mp hIP) ?_
    unfold Space.lamId
    refine sup_le ?_ ?_
    · rw [Ideal.map_le_iff_le_comap]
      intro a ha
      rw [Ideal.mem_comap, Ideal.mem_comap, AlgHom.commutes, ← AlgHom.commutes p]
      exact Ideal.mem_map_of_mem _
        (hlam (by unfold Space.lamId; exact Ideal.mem_sup_left (Ideal.mem_map_of_mem _ ha)))
    · rw [Ideal.span_le]
      rintro _ ⟨v, i, g, hg, hΨ, rfl⟩
      rw [SetLike.mem_coe, Ideal.mem_comap, map_sub, map_one, hψ, ← map_one p, ← map_sub]
      exact Ideal.mem_map_of_mem _ (hlam (by
        unfold Space.lamId
        exact Ideal.mem_sup_right (Ideal.subset_span ⟨v, cor i, g, hg, hΨ, rfl⟩)))
  have hlift : ∀ (b : S₂.B) (y : S₃.B), β b = p y → ∃ c, y - algebraMap O S₃.B c ∈ P := by
    intro b y hy
    obtain ⟨a, ha⟩ := @IsCO.res_surj O S₂.B _ _ _ _ hco₂ b
    rw [hm₂] at ha
    refine ⟨a, hpull _ ?_⟩
    rw [map_sub, AlgHom.commutes, ← hy, ← AlgHom.commutes β, ← map_sub]
    exact hQ ha
  have hmid : ∀ g : F.G0, S₃.aB g 1 - 1 ∈ P :=
    frobenius_corner_middle_mem hF S₃ P hPp hlam
      (fun g h => hqeq _ _ (by rw [map_mul, map_mul]; exact UpperTheory.V.Fr.mid_mul _ hc.odd g h))
      (fun v g => hqeq _ _ (hc.mid v g))
  refine frobenius_corner_maximalIdeal_le hF (Or.inr rfl) S₃ P (le_sup_left.trans hlam) ?_ ?_
  · intro x hx
    obtain ⟨L, hL, hxL⟩ := hx
    have hqx : q (S₃.inc x) ∈ (S₃.u.map (q : S₃.A →+* K)).mon 0 := by
      refine ⟨L, hL, ?_⟩
      have e : S₃.inc x = (L.map (fun s => S₃.u.r s.1 s.2.1 s.2.2)).prod := by
        apply Subtype.ext
        rw [SubmonoidClass.coe_list_prod, List.map_map]
        exact hxL
      rw [e, map_list_prod, List.map_map]
      rfl
    obtain h0 | ⟨T, y, hy, hxy⟩ := frobenius_corner_monomial _ _ hc 0 _ hqx
    · refine ⟨0, ?_⟩
      simpa using hqeq x 0 (by rw [h0, map_zero, map_zero])
    · obtain ⟨L₂, hL₂, rfl⟩ := hy
      have hy₂mon : ((L₂.map (fun s => S₂.u.r s.1 s.2.1 s.2.2)).prod : S₂.R) ∈ S₂.ρ.mon 0 := by
        refine ⟨L₂, hL₂, ?_⟩
        rw [SubmonoidClass.coe_list_prod, List.map_map]
        rfl
      obtain ⟨b₂, hb₂⟩ : ∃ b₂ : S₂.B, S₂.inc b₂ = (L₂.map (fun s => S₂.u.r s.1 s.2.1 s.2.2)).prod :=
        ⟨⟨_, S₂.mon_mem hy₂mon⟩, Subtype.ext rfl⟩
      have hyval : (L₂.map fun s => (S₂.u.map (ξ : S₂.A →+* K)).r s.1 s.2.1 s.2.2).prod = j (β b₂) := by
        rw [← hβ, hb₂, map_list_prod, List.map_map]
        rfl
      obtain ⟨y₃, hy₃⟩ := hp (β b₂)
      obtain ⟨c, hc'⟩ := hlift b₂ y₃ hy₃.symm
      have ht : (T.map fun g => (S₃.u.map (q : S₃.A →+* K)).r g 1 1).prod
          = j (p (T.map fun g => S₃.aB g 1).prod) := by
        rw [← hpq, map_list_prod, map_list_prod, List.map_map, List.map_map]
        rfl
      have hmk : ∀ g, Ideal.Quotient.mk P (S₃.aB g 1) = 1 := fun g => by
        rw [← map_one (Ideal.Quotient.mk P)]
        exact Ideal.Quotient.eq.mpr (hmid g)
      have ht1 : (T.map fun g => S₃.aB g 1).prod - 1 ∈ P := by
        rw [← Ideal.Quotient.eq, map_one, map_list_prod, List.map_map]
        simp [Function.comp_def, hmk]
      have hx2 : x - (T.map fun g => S₃.aB g 1).prod * y₃ ∈ P := by
        refine hqeq _ _ ?_
        rw [hxy, hyval, ht, hpq, map_mul p, map_mul j, hy₃]
      refine ⟨c, ?_⟩
      have e : x - algebraMap O S₃.B c = (x - (T.map fun g => S₃.aB g 1).prod * y₃)
          + ((T.map fun g => S₃.aB g 1).prod - 1) * y₃ + (y₃ - algebraMap O S₃.B c) := by ring
      rw [e]
      exact P.add_mem (P.add_mem hx2 (P.mul_mem_right _ ht1)) hc'
  · intro v i g
    fin_cases i
    · exact hlift _ _ (hψ v 0 g)
    · refine ⟨1, ?_⟩
      show S₃.ψB v 1 g - algebraMap O S₃.B 1 ∈ P
      rw [map_one]
      have e : S₃.ψB v 1 g - 1
          = (S₃.aB (F.loc0 v g) 1 - 1) - (S₃.aB (F.loc0 v g) 1 - S₃.ψB v 1 g) := by ring
      rw [e]
      exact P.sub_mem (hmid _) (hqeq _ _ (hc.mid v g))
    · exact hlift _ _ (hψ v 1 g)

private theorem finite_lambda_two_three_surjection_compat {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [IsDiscreteValuationRing O]
    [IsCoef l O] {F : Frame l (ResidueField O)} {C₀ : ℕ} (hF : F.Ok C₀) (S₂ : Space O F 2) (S₃ : Space O F 3)
    (I₂ : Ideal S₂.B) (hI₂ : S₂.FinLam I₂) (hT : ∀ ξ : Ideal S₂.A, ξ.IsPrime → S₂.u.TI ξ → I₂ ≤ S₂.π ξ)
    (C : Type) [CommRing C] [Algebra O C] [Nontrivial C] (K : Type) [CommRing K] [Algebra O K]
    (p : S₃.B →ₐ[O] C) (q : S₃.A →ₐ[O] K) (j : C →ₐ[O] K) (hp : Function.Surjective p) (hq : Function.Surjective q)
    (hj : Function.Injective j) (hpq : ∀ b, q (S₃.inc b) = j (p b)) (hker : RingHom.ker q = S₃.u.ee) :
    ∃ I₃ : Ideal S₃.B, S₃.FinLam I₃ ∧ ∀ ξ : Ideal S₃.A, ξ.IsPrime → S₃.u.TE ξ → I₃ ≤ S₃.π ξ := by
  obtain ⟨hcoB, -⟩ := co_B hF (Or.inr rfl) S₃
  haveI := hcoB
  haveI hcoC : IsCO O C := co_quot O S₃.B C p hp
  letI : Algebra C K := j.toRingHom.toAlgebra
  haveI : IsScalarTower O C K := IsScalarTower.of_algebraMap_eq (fun x => (j.commutes x).symm)
  have hjinj : Function.Injective (algebraMap C K) := hj
  have hcone : Cone O C (S₃.u.map (q : S₃.A →+* K)) :=
    frobenius_corner_cone_map S₃.B S₃.A C K p (q : S₃.A →+* K) (fun c => hpq c) S₃.u (cone_u hF (Or.inr rfl) S₃).1
  have hodd : ∀ (g : F.G0) (i k : Fin 3), Odd (w 3 i - w 3 k) →
      (S₃.u.map (q : S₃.A →+* K)).r g i k = 0 := by
    intro g i k h
    show q (S₃.u.r g i k) = 0
    rw [← RingHom.mem_ker, hker]
    exact Ideal.subset_span ⟨g, i, k, h, rfl⟩
  obtain ⟨σ, hc⟩ := exists_frobenius_corner _ hodd (frobenius_corner_units hF C K _ hcone)
  obtain ⟨ξ, β, hξσ, hβ⟩ := alg hF (Or.inl rfl) S₂ C K hjinj σ (frobenius_corner_mem_cone C K _ σ hc hcone)
  refine ⟨(I₂.map β).comap p, finite_lambda_surjective_injective_lift hF S₂ S₃ I₂ hI₂ C K p q j hp hj hpq σ ξ β hc hξσ hβ, ?_⟩
  intro P hP hTE
  unfold NRep.TE NRep.OffTri at hTE
  obtain ⟨⟨hTP, hTM⟩, -, hee⟩ := hTE
  have hkerP : RingHom.ker q ≤ P := hker ▸ hee
  haveI hP' : (P.map q).IsPrime := Ideal.map_isPrime_of_surjective hq hkerP
  have hPc : (P.map q).comap q = P := by
    rw [Ideal.comap_map_of_surjective _ hq]
    exact sup_eq_left.mpr hkerP
  have hξr : ∀ g (a b : Fin 2), ξ (S₂.u.r g a b) = q (S₃.u.r g (cor a) (cor b)) := by
    intro g a b
    have e : (S₂.u.map (ξ : S₂.A →+* K)).r g a b = σ.r g a b := by rw [hξσ]
    rw [hc.r g a b] at e
    exact e
  have hTI : S₂.u.TI ((P.map q).comap ξ) := by
    unfold NRep.TI NRep.OffTri
    refine ⟨⟨?_, ?_⟩, fun h => absurd h (by decide)⟩
    · intro h
      apply hTP
      rw [NRep.triP, Ideal.span_le]
      rintro _ ⟨g, i, k, hki, rfl⟩
      fin_cases i <;> fin_cases k <;> first
        | exact absurd hki (by decide)
        | exact hee (Ideal.subset_span ⟨g, _, _, by decide, rfl⟩)
        | (rw [← hPc]
           have h1 := h (Ideal.subset_span ⟨g, 1, 0, by decide, rfl⟩)
           rw [Ideal.mem_comap, hξr] at h1
           exact h1)
    · intro h
      apply hTM
      rw [NRep.triM, Ideal.span_le]
      rintro _ ⟨g, i, k, hki, rfl⟩
      fin_cases i <;> fin_cases k <;> first
        | exact absurd hki (by decide)
        | exact hee (Ideal.subset_span ⟨g, _, _, by decide, rfl⟩)
        | (rw [← hPc]
           have h1 := h (Ideal.subset_span ⟨g, 0, 1, by decide, rfl⟩)
           rw [Ideal.mem_comap, hξr] at h1
           exact h1)
  have hT' := hT _ inferInstance hTI
  have hle : I₂.map β ≤ (P.map q).comap j := by
    rw [Ideal.map_le_iff_le_comap]
    intro b hb
    have hb' := hT' hb
    simp only [Space.π, Ideal.mem_comap] at hb' ⊢
    have e : j (β b) = ξ (S₂.inc b) := (hβ b).symm
    rw [e]
    exact hb'
  intro x hx
  rw [Ideal.mem_comap] at hx
  show S₃.inc x ∈ P
  rw [← hPc, Ideal.mem_comap, hpq]
  exact hle hx

theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.l53 :
    ∀ {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [IsDiscreteValuationRing O] [IsCoef l O] {F : Frame l (ResidueField O)} {C₀ : ℕ}, F.Ok C₀ → ∀ (S₂ : Space O F 2) (S₃ : Space O F 3) (I₂ : Ideal S₂.B), S₂.FinLam I₂ → (∀ ξ : Ideal S₂.A, ξ.IsPrime → S₂.u.TI ξ → I₂ ≤ S₂.π ξ) → ∃ I₃ : Ideal S₃.B, S₃.FinLam I₃ ∧ ∀ ξ : Ideal S₃.A, ξ.IsPrime → S₃.u.TE ξ → I₃ ≤ S₃.π ξ := by
  intro l _ O _ _ _ _ F C₀ hF S₂ S₃ I₂ hI₂ hT
  by_cases htop : S₃.u.ee = ⊤
  · refine ⟨⊤, ?_, ?_⟩
    · show S₃.mB ≤ _
      rw [top_sup_eq, Ideal.radical_top]
      exact le_top
    · intro P hP hTE
      exact absurd (top_le_iff.mp (htop ▸ hTE.2.2)) hP.ne_top
  · haveI : Nontrivial (S₃.B ⧸ S₃.u.ee.comap S₃.inc) :=
      Ideal.Quotient.nontrivial_iff.mpr (Ideal.comap_ne_top _ htop)
    exact finite_lambda_two_three_surjection_compat hF S₂ S₃ I₂ hI₂ hT (S₃.B ⧸ S₃.u.ee.comap S₃.inc) (S₃.A ⧸ S₃.u.ee)
      (Ideal.Quotient.mkₐ O _) (Ideal.Quotient.mkₐ O _) (Ideal.quotientMapₐ S₃.u.ee S₃.inc le_rfl)
      (Ideal.Quotient.mkₐ_surjective O _) (Ideal.Quotient.mkₐ_surjective O _)
      Ideal.quotientMap_injective (fun b => rfl) (Ideal.Quotient.mkₐ_ker O _)


[ACP_SRC_TWORUNS] Actual same-frame two-rank and two-place call, with separate rings and ideals
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/ModularityArgument/ThresholdArguments/DenseThresholdCases/TwoRuns.lean#L80-L109
theorem UpperTheory.Gal.projectedFormsSystemPart.twoRuns_alt (hA : GlobalTheory.Published.GlobalArtinReciprocity) (hMod : _root_.UpperTheory.V.W.ModUAlt) (hLam : W.LamEx)
    {Ω : Type} [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] (ι : Qbar l ≃+* ℂ) {c : Coef l}
    [CharP (ResidueField c.O) l] {N : ℕ} [NeZero N] {e : ℕ} {ψ : DirichletCharacter c.O N}
    {η : G ℚ Ω →* ℤˣ} {σ g₁ : G ℚ Ω} {B : ValuationSubring Ω} {M : ℕ} (H : LinkHyp c N e η σ B M)
    {L : Type} [Field L] [NumberField L] [IsCMField L] [Algebra L Ω] [IsAlgClosure L Ω] {Φ : Fr.Frame l (ResidueField c.O)}
    {k : GalLink η (Chr.Psi l e (Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ)) σ g₁ B M L Φ} (v : Φ.V)
    {c₁ : Coef l} [Fr.IsCoef l c₁.O] {f : ResidueField c.O →+* ResidueField c₁.O}
    {ka kb : G L Ω →* (Qbar l)ˣ} (hL : W.Large L c₁) (ha : IsKa σ M ι 2 ka) (hb : IsKa σ M ι 3 kb)
    (e₂ : ∀ p₀ : ℕ, p₀ ≠ 0 → ∃ R : CReal k c₁ f 2 ka, ¬ R.s.p ∣ p₀)
    (e₃ : ∀ p₀ : ℕ, p₀ ≠ 0 → ∃ R : CReal k c₁ f 3 kb, ¬ R.s.p ∣ p₀)
    (s₂ : ∀ (R : CReal k c₁ f 2 ka) J, R.B₀.PointTheoremBaseObject ι J → R.T.IdealSpecializationPoint J)
    (s₃ : ∀ (R : CReal k c₁ f 3 kb) J, R.B₀.PointTheoremBaseObject ι J → R.T.IdealSpecializationPoint J) :
    FinPt c₁.O (Φ.ext f) 3 ∧ _root_.UpperTheory.V.Pr.AutPtAlt k 3 ι kb ∧ _root_.UpperTheory.V.Pr.AutPtAlt k 2 ι ka := by
  have one : ∀ (R₂ : CReal k c₁ f 2 ka) (R₃ : CReal k c₁ f 3 kb), FinPt c₁.O (Φ.ext f) 3 ∧
      _root_.UpperTheory.Gal.projectedFormsSystemPart.AutPtOAlt R₃.s.v0 k 3 ι kb ∧ _root_.UpperTheory.Gal.projectedFormsSystemPart.AutPtOAlt R₂.s.v0 k 2 ι ka := fun R₂ R₃ => by
    obtain ⟨J₂, h₂, d₂⟩ := _root_.UpperTheory.V.Pr.modularity_linked_ends hMod hLam ι H v R₂ hL (s₂ R₂)
    obtain ⟨J₃, h₃, d₃⟩ := _root_.UpperTheory.V.Pr.modularity_linked_ends hMod hLam ι H v R₃ hL (s₃ R₃)
    obtain ⟨g₂, g₃, hfin⟩ := UpperTheory.V.Pr.absMain l c₁.O _ R₂.T R₃.T J₂ J₃ h₂ h₃
    exact ⟨hfin, _root_.UpperTheory.Gal.projectedFormsSystemPart.autPtO_alt hA Ω l ι c N e ψ η σ g₁ B M H L Φ k c₁ f 3 _ R₃ hb J₃ g₃ d₃,
      _root_.UpperTheory.Gal.projectedFormsSystemPart.autPtO_alt hA Ω l ι c N e ψ η σ g₁ B M H L Φ k c₁ f 2 ka R₂ ha J₂ g₂ d₂⟩
  obtain ⟨R₂, -⟩ := e₂ 1 one_ne_zero
  obtain ⟨R₃, -⟩ := e₃ 1 one_ne_zero
  obtain ⟨R₂', n₂⟩ := e₂ R₂.s.p R₂.s.p_prime.ne_zero
  obtain ⟨R₃', n₃⟩ := e₃ R₃.s.p R₃.s.p_prime.ne_zero
  obtain ⟨hfin, a₃, a₂⟩ := one R₂ R₃
  obtain ⟨-, b₃, b₂⟩ := one R₂' R₃'
  exact ⟨hfin, fun A _ _ _ ρ h1 h2 r h3 h4 => _root_.UpperTheory.Gal.projectedFormsSystemPart.glueN (by norm_num) (UpperTheory.Gal.projectedFormsSystemPart.v0_ne n₃)
      (a₃ A ρ h1 h2 r h3 h4) (b₃ A ρ h1 h2 r h3 h4),
    fun A _ _ _ ρ h1 h2 r h3 h4 => _root_.UpperTheory.Gal.projectedFormsSystemPart.glueN (by norm_num) (UpperTheory.Gal.projectedFormsSystemPart.v0_ne n₂)
      (a₂ A ρ h1 h2 r h3 h4) (b₂ A ρ h1 h2 r h3 h4)⟩


[ACP_SRC_FINPTDOWN] Retained finPtDown: descent from the extended coefficient frame to the original frame
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/CurveDVRSpecialization/FinPtDown.lean#L113-L155
theorem UpperTheory.V.Pr.finPtDown :
    UpperTheory.V.Pr.FinPtDown := by
  intro l _ c c₁ f _ _ Φ n H A _ _ hA _ _ ρ hρ hψ g
  haveI : IsDiscreteValuationRing A := hA.toIsDiscreteValuationRing
  haveI := UpperTheory.V.UnitaryThreeInertia.Coef.finite_residue c₁
  obtain ⟨B, _, _, hB, φ, j₁, hc, hfin, hinj, hloc, hj⟩ :=
    fpd_ext (A := A) f (algebraMap (ResidueField c.O) A)
  letI : Algebra (ResidueField c₁.O) B := j₁.toAlgebra
  letI : Algebra c₁.O B := (j₁.comp (residue c₁.O)).toAlgebra
  haveI : IsScalarTower c₁.O (ResidueField c₁.O) B := IsScalarTower.of_algebraMap_eq fun _ => rfl
  haveI hl : IsLocalHom (algebraMap c₁.O B) := RingHom.isLocalHom_comp j₁ (residue c₁.O)
  haveI : IsCD c₁.O B := { toIsDiscreteValuationRing := hB, complete := hc, isLocalHom := hl, fin := hfin }
  let ρ₀ := ρ.map φ
  let ρ₁ : NRep (Φ.ext f) n B := ⟨ρ₀.r, ρ₀.ψ, ρ₀.pol, ρ₀.diag, ρ₀.ord⟩
  have key : ∀ a, ResidueField.map φ (ResidueField.map (algebraMap c.O A) a) =
      ResidueField.map (algebraMap c₁.O B) (f a) := by
    intro a
    obtain ⟨b, hb⟩ := residue_surjective (f a)
    obtain ⟨a, rfl⟩ := residue_surjective a
    rw [← hb, ResidueField.map_residue, ResidueField.map_residue, ResidueField.map_residue,
      IsScalarTower.algebraMap_apply c.O (ResidueField c.O) A, ← hj]
    exact congrArg (fun t => residue B (j₁ t)) hb.symm
  have hz : ∀ (s : Φ.Γ) (m : ℤ), (((Φ.ext f).Ψ s ^ m : (ResidueField c₁.O)ˣ) : ResidueField c₁.O) =
      f ((Φ.Ψ s ^ m : (ResidueField c.O)ˣ) : ResidueField c.O) := by
    intro s m
    show ((Units.map f.toMonoidHom (Φ.Ψ s) ^ m : (ResidueField c₁.O)ˣ) : ResidueField c₁.O) = _
    rw [← map_zpow]
    rfl
  have h1 : IsLiftA c₁.O ρ₁ := by
    refine ⟨⟨fun i j => fpd_cont φ (hρ.cont.1 i j), fun v i => fpd_cont φ (hρ.cont.2 v i)⟩,
      ⟨fun s i => ?_, fun v i s => ?_⟩, fun s i j hij => ?_⟩
    · exact (congrArg (ResidueField.map φ) (hρ.bar.1 s i)).trans ((key _).trans (congrArg _ (hz _ _).symm))
    · exact (congrArg (ResidueField.map φ) (hρ.bar.2 v i s)).trans ((key _).trans (congrArg _ (hz _ _).symm))
    · exact (congrArg (ResidueField.map φ) (hρ.diag s i j hij)).trans (map_zero _)
  have h2 := H B ρ₁ h1 (fun v i s hs h => by
    have h' : Φ.Ψ (Φ.loc v s) = 1 := Units.ext (f.injective ((congrArg Units.val h).trans (map_one f).symm))
    show Units.map φ.toMonoidHom (ρ.ψ v i s) = 1
    rw [hψ v i s hs h', map_one]) g
  apply Polynomial.map_injective φ hinj
  rw [← Matrix.charpoly_map, Polynomial.map_prod]
  refine h2.trans (Finset.prod_congr rfl fun i _ => ?_)
  rw [Polynomial.map_sub, Polynomial.map_X, Polynomial.map_C, ← hj]
  exact congrArg (fun t => Polynomial.X - Polynomial.C (j₁ t)) (hz _ _)


[ACP_SRC_LINKEDEND] Actual common linked endpoint applies finPtDown at line 67
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/ModularityArgument/ThresholdArguments/DenseThresholdCases/LinkedEndE.lean#L18-L67
theorem UpperTheory.Gal.projectedFormsSystemPart.linked_endomorphism_dense :
    GlobalTheory.Published.GlobalArtinReciprocity → UpperTheory.V.W.LocCFTAll → _root_.UpperTheory.V.W.ModUAlt → UpperTheory.V.W.LevEx → UpperTheory.V.W.LamEx → UpperTheory.V.W.ProModularRingExists → _root_.UpperTheory.V.Z.Sym.UnitaryThreeAutomorphyDense → _root_.UpperTheory.V.Sd.Seed2Alt → UpperTheory.V.Sd.Twist → UpperTheory.V.Sd.AlgCyc → UpperTheory.V.Sd.AlgRes → _root_.UpperTheory.V.Z.Sym.Pl.LinkedEndEAlt := by
  intro hA _ hMod hLev hLam hB0 hS3 hS2 hTw hCyc hRes Ω _ _ _ l _ ι c _ N _ e ψ he hev η σ g₁ B M hM hram L _ _ _ _ Φ C₀ k hB hok hbig hseed
  obtain ⟨K, _, _, _, N', w, χ', η', θ, j, hD, hne, hord, hη, hcong, hdisc⟩ := hseed
  haveI : IsAlgClosure L Ω :=
    { isAlgClosed := IsAlgClosure.isAlgClosed ℚ
      isAlgebraic := haveI : Algebra.IsAlgebraic ℚ Ω := IsAlgClosure.isAlgebraic
        Algebra.IsAlgebraic.tower_top (K := ℚ) L }
  have hM0 : M ≠ 0 := by
    rintro rfl
    obtain ⟨hσ, hc⟩ := k.c
    have h1 : σ ∈ η.ker := k.split ⊤ (by rw [← ValuationSubring.mem_nonunits_iff, Nat.cast_zero]; exact zero_mem _) ⟨by
      refine mem_Dec.2 (eq_top_iff.2 fun x _ => ?_)
      rw [ValuationSubring.mem_pointwise_smul_iff_inv_smul_mem]
      exact ValuationSubring.mem_top _, hσ⟩
    have h2 := k.del ⟨σ, hσ⟩
    rw [hc, hok.del_c] at h2
    have h3 : η σ = 1 := h1
    rw [h3] at h2
    exact absurd h2 (by decide)
  have H : UpperTheory.V.Pr.LinkHyp c N e η σ B M := ⟨he, hM, hM0, hram, hB, hD.cc⟩
  have hv : Nonempty Φ.V := by
    by_contra h
    rw [not_nonempty_iff] at h
    have := hbig.1
    simp [UpperTheory.V.Fr.Frame.d] at this
  obtain ⟨v⟩ := hv
  have hker : η.ker = galOf ℚ K Ω := by
    rw [← hD.η_ker]
    ext s
    rw [MonoidHom.mem_ker, MonoidHom.mem_ker, ← Units.val_eq_one, ← Units.val_eq_one, hη s]
    exact_mod_cast Iff.rfl
  haveI := hD.imag
  obtain ⟨κ, hκ⟩ := hTw K Ω hD.quad l hok.l_odd hdisc ι σ hD.cc
  obtain ⟨hk3, hk2⟩ := UpperTheory.V.Pr.kaOf hCyc hRes Ω l ι c N e ψ η σ g₁ B M H L Φ k
  obtain ⟨ka, hk2, hka⟩ := hk2 K hker κ hκ
  obtain ⟨c₁, hle, hL, hv3, hv2, f, hf⟩ := UpperTheory.V.Pr.coefEx l c L Ω _ _ hk3.alg.1 hk2.alg.1
  haveI := UpperTheory.V.Pr.coefIs l c₁
  obtain ⟨m₃, hm₃, -, r₃, -, s₃⟩ := hS3 K L Ω l ι σ N' w χ' η' θ j hD hne M hM0
    (by rw [mul_comm]; exact (Nat.mul_dvd_mul_left l hev.two_dvd).trans hM)
    (UpperTheory.V.Z.Sym.Pl.linkUnr Ω l _ η _ σ g₁ B M L Φ k) (UpperTheory.V.Z.Sym.Pl.chiUnr Ω l c N e ψ χ' j hord hcong M hM L) 2
  obtain ⟨m₂, hm₂, -, r₂, -, -, -, s₂⟩ := hS2 K L Ω l ι σ N' w χ' η' θ j hD κ ka hκ hka 2
  obtain ⟨hfin, a₃, a₂⟩ := _root_.UpperTheory.Gal.projectedFormsSystemPart.twoRuns_alt hA hMod hLam ι H v hL hk2 hk3
    (UpperTheory.Gal.projectedFormsSystemPart.cRealAv hLev hB0 Ω l ι c N e ψ η σ g₁ B M H L Φ k c₁ f 2 ka C₀ (Or.inl rfl) hok hbig hle hf hk2 hv2)
    (UpperTheory.Gal.projectedFormsSystemPart.cRealAv hLev hB0 Ω l ι c N e ψ η σ g₁ B M H L Φ k c₁ f 3 _ C₀ (Or.inr rfl) hok hbig hle hf hk3 hv3)
    (fun R₂ => _root_.UpperTheory.V.Pr.seedPtOf_alt hA Ω l ι c N e ψ η σ g₁ B M H L Φ k c₁ f 2 ka R₂ hk2 χ' j hord hcong m₂ hm₂ _ (by
      convert s₂ using 2 with i i i <;> fin_cases i <;> simp [UpperTheory.V.Fr.w]))
    (fun R₃ => _root_.UpperTheory.V.Pr.seedPtOf_alt hA Ω l ι c N e ψ η σ g₁ B M H L Φ k c₁ f 3 _ R₃ hk3 χ' j hord hcong m₃ hm₃ _ (by
      convert s₃ using 2 with i i i <;> fin_cases i <;> simp [UpperTheory.V.Fr.w]))
  exact ⟨UpperTheory.V.Pr.finPtDown l c c₁ f hle hf Φ 3 hfin, a₃, ka, hk2.alg, a₂⟩
